## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [1] [Training Continuous Chain of Thought Models: A Tale of Two Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16972)
*Varun Yerram, He He, Eunsol Choi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (75.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 C-MTP，一种更简单快速的连续思维链（Continuous CoT）直接监督方法，将每个连续 latent 建模为 CoT 轨迹中 token 嵌入的平均值。在简单推理任务上表现与间接监督方法相当，但在复杂长推理任务上（数百 token）所有现有方法性能大幅下降约 65%，揭示了当前连续 CoT 方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续 CoT 方法（如 COCONUT）通过间接监督训练，需要自回归慢速生成，训练效率低。虽然已有直接监督方法（如 DiCoT），但性能不如间接监督。本文旨在提出一种更简单、更快的直接监督方法，并系统评估连续 CoT 方法在复杂长推理任务上的表现。

Method: 提出 C-MTP（Continuous Model of Thought with Pooling），将每个连续 latent 直接定义为 CoT 轨迹中对应 token 嵌入的平均值（mean pooling），通过均方误差损失监督。相比 DiCoT 的分布近似方法更简单直接。在训练时，使用预训练语言模型编码器将离散 CoT 轨迹转换为连续 latent 序列，然后训练模型从问题直接生成这些连续 latent。

Result: 在简化 CoT 轨迹（<100 token）的 GSM8K 和 PrOntoQA 上，C-MTP 优于 DiCoT，与间接监督方法（COCONUT）性能相当。但在复杂任务（GSM-Plus、MATH、ProofWriter，数百 token）上，所有连续 CoT 方法性能大幅下降约 65%，远低于离散 CoT 基线。

Conclusion: C-MTP 提供了一种更简单有效的连续 CoT 直接监督方案，但实验表明当前连续 CoT 方法在处理长推理轨迹时存在根本性局限，无法有效压缩和利用长序列的语义信息。这为未来连续推理研究指明了关键挑战方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Continuous Chain-of-Thought（连续思维链）方法，属于在连续潜空间中进行推理的范式，与 [CANONICAL] 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 高度匹配。论文虽主要聚焦语言推理，但其方法（C-MTP）和问题设定（连续 latent 表示替代离散推理轨迹）是该子主题的核心内容。

Importance reason: Directly addresses continuous language model reasoning limitations, a top priority subtopic, with novel findings on long-trace failure. Signals: continuous chain of thought, direct supervision method, long reasoning trace failure, continuous latent representation, C-MTP

Abstract: Continuous Chain-of-Thought methods replace verbose reasoning traces with a short sequence of dense latent representations. Earlier continuous CoT methods indirectly supervise the latent representations such that its final state match that of verbose reasoning traces, requiring autoregressive, slow generation during training. We introduce C-MTP, a simpler, faster direct supervision approach that models each latent as an average of the embeddings in the CoT traces to be compressed. Our approach outperforms a prior direct supervision method that approximates the distribution of compressed tokens, and performs competitively to slower indirect supervision approaches in existing evaluation setup with simplified CoT traces (less than 100 tokens). Lastly, we extend the evaluation of Continuous CoT methods to complex tasks with longer reasoning traces ($\ge$ few hundreds reasoning tokens). We find both direct and indirect supervision training methods perform poorly (roughly 65\% performance drop) in this setting, revealing the limitations of current continuous CoT methods. The code and checkpoints are released at

</details>

## 连续语言模型与语言流 [[Back]](#toc)

连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模，包括 ELF、CoLa、扩散语言模型与 flow matching language model。

### [2] [Trace-Based On-Policy Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16872)
*Haolin Ren, Ziyang Huang, Chenhao Yuan, Jun Zhao, Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 TOPD（Trace-Based On-Policy Distillation），一种面向掩码扩散语言模型的教师监督蒸馏框架，通过让目标模型在其自身的去噪轨迹上接受教师 token 级 Reverse-KL 监督，无需奖励估计即可实现推理能力迁移，在数学推理任务上达到甚至超越 RL 训练的性能，同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）是自回归生成的有前景替代方案，但其面向推理的后训练面临挑战：SFT 需要密集但 off-policy 的掩码状态，RL 依赖稀疏奖励或价值建模。现有方法难以在保持密集教师监督的同时与模型自身的去噪状态对齐。

Method: TOPD 框架包含三个步骤：(1) 从目标 dLLM 采样 on-policy 扩散轨迹；(2) 在对应的部分去噪状态下，从教师模型获取 token 级分布；(3) 使用 token 级 Reverse-KL 散度作为目标函数更新目标 dLLM。该方法保留了密集的教师监督，同时确保训练与模型自身的去噪状态对齐。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，TOPD 使 SDAR-4B-Chat 在 MATH500 准确率上匹配其 RL 训练版本 TraDo-4B-Instruct，静态评估提升 +5.7，动态评估提升 +4.5。相比 RL 训练，TOPD 仅需 1/4 的 rollout 轮次，实现了估计 96.0 倍的模型计算加速。

Conclusion: TOPD 证明了在扩散语言模型上，通过 on-policy 蒸馏可以在不依赖奖励建模的情况下有效迁移推理能力，且计算效率远优于 RL 方法。该方法为 dLLM 的后训练提供了新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（dLLM）的后训练蒸馏方法，属于连续语言模型范畴。扩散语言模型在连续潜空间中进行去噪生成，与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model training via on-policy distillation, a core priority subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, on-policy distillation, reasoning transfer, compute efficiency

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) are a promising alternative to autoregressive generation. However, reasoning-oriented post-training for dLLMs remains challenging. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) for dLLMs requires dense but often off-policy masked states, while reinforcement learning (RL) relies on sparse rewards or value modeling. This paper proposes \textbf{trace-based on-policy distillation (TOPD)}, a teacher-supervised framework that transfers reasoning ability to a target dLLM without reward estimation. The key idea is to supervise a dLLM on its own denoising trajectory, focusing on the trace-aligned token decisions that form the final response. Specifically, TOPD samples on-policy diffusion trajectories from the target dLLM, obtains teacher token distributions from a teacher model on the corresponding partially denoised states, and updates the target dLLM with a token-level Reverse Kullback-Leibler (Reverse-KL) objective. This design preserves dense teacher supervision while aligning training with the model's own denoising states. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, TOPD enables SDAR-4B-Chat to match the MATH500 accuracy of its RL-trained counterpart TraDo-4B-Instruct, with gains of +5.7 under static evaluation and +4.5 under dynamic evaluation. Compared with the RL-trained counterpart, TOPD achieves this with 4$\times$ fewer rollout rounds, corresponding to an estimated 96.0$\times$ to-accuracy model-compute speedup.

</details>


### [3] [FlowBlock: Wavefront-Parallel Decoding for Self-Correcting Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17652)
*Bing Tian, Haikun Liu, Xiaocheng Zhong, Zhuohui Duan, Zhaokai Luo, Huayi Jin, Zhiyong Wang, Xiaofei Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FlowBlock 是一种无需训练的并行解码框架，通过门控波前解码和异构波前打包，在自纠正扩散语言模型上实现块间并行，显著提升吞吐量并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块级扩散大语言模型（dLLM）在块级别顺序解码，虽然支持块间 KV 缓存复用，但块间解码严格串行。现有后训练方法解锁块间并行性效果有限且常降低精度。作者观察到自纠正 dLLM 提供了一种无需训练的替代方案：token-to-token（T2T）编辑可以修复使用略微过时的上游上下文生成的 token，因此下游块只需要一个信息性的草稿而非最终化的前驱块。这促使作者设计无需训练的并行解码框架。

Method: FlowBlock 包含两个核心机制：(1) 门控波前解码（Gated Wavefront Decoding）——仅在就绪门条件满足时将块纳入有界波前，通过 T2T 编辑联合优化活跃块，并在窗口化块因果掩码下按顺序提交块，保持精确的冻结前缀 KV 缓存复用；(2) 异构波前打包（Heterogeneous Wavefront Packing）——为每个请求分配独立波前，同时将异步窗口打包成密集、形状稳定的批量前向传播。

Result: 在 LLaDA-2.1 和 LLaDA-2.0 上，FlowBlock 将每秒 token 数（TPS）提升最高 2.95 倍和 4.01 倍，延迟降低最高 53.6% 和 77.1%，平均准确率提升 1.3 个百分点。与基于训练的 D2F 基线相比，FlowBlock 实现了更高准确率和最高 16 倍的批量服务吞吐量。

Conclusion: FlowBlock 证明了自纠正扩散语言模型可以无需训练地实现高效的块间并行解码，通过利用 T2T 编辑的容错特性将块最终性从硬依赖转变为调度资源，在保持甚至提升生成质量的同时大幅加速推理。

Classification reason: 该论文研究块级扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的并行解码，属于连续语言模型（扩散语言模型）范畴，与 canonical 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model decoding efficiency with a training-free parallel framework, aligning with top-priority subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, parallel decoding, training-free, self-correcting

Abstract: Block-wise diffusion large language models (dLLMs) decode sequentially at the block level, enabling effective KV-cache reuse across blocks but making inter-block decoding strictly serial. Prior work has attempted to unlock inter-block parallelism through post-training methods, but achieves only modest speedups and often degrades accuracy. We observe that self-correcting dLLMs offer a training-free alternative: token-to-token (T2T) editing can repair tokens drafted with a slightly stale upstream context, so a downstream block requires only an informative draft rather than a finalized predecessor. This turns block finality from a hard dependency into a scheduling resource. We propose \textbf{\flowblock{}}, a training-free parallel decoding framework built on two mechanisms. (i) \emph{Gated Wavefront Decoding} admits blocks into a bounded wavefront only when a readiness gate is satisfied, jointly refines active blocks via T2T editing, and commits blocks in order under a windowed block-causal mask that preserves exact frozen-prefix KV caches reuse. (ii) \emph{Heterogeneous Wavefront Packing} assigns each request an independent wavefront while packing asynchronous windows into dense, shape-stable batched forwards. Across different benchmarks, \flowblock{} improves tokens per second (TPS) over LLaDA-2.1 and LLaDA-2.0, two serial block-wise dLLMs, by up to 2.95$\times$ and 4.01$\times$, while reducing latency by up to 53.6\% and 77.1\%, respectively. It also improves average accuracy by 1.3 points. Compared with D2F, a training-based inter-block-parallel baseline, \flowblock{} achieves higher accuracy and up to 16$\times$ higher batched serving throughput.

</details>

## 具身世界模型与规划控制 [[Back]](#toc)

### [4] [Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory for Proactive Embodied Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16247)
*Bingrui Sima, Lizhong Wang, Xiaoya Lu, Kun He, Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory 框架，通过风险充分拓扑信念图、基于代理的事实记忆和经验记忆，结合自动测试-验证-写入循环，使具身智能体在闭环交互中主动缓解动态危险而不阻碍任务进展，在 IS-Bench 上显著提升安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 驱动的具身智能体在家庭任务中难以主动处理动态出现的危险，现有安全方法要么运行时阻断不安全动作，要么引发过度谨慎，严重阻碍任务进展。作者希望打破安全与进展之间的权衡，将安全从阻碍进展的护栏转变为主动的风险缓解。

Method: 提出 Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Risk-Sufficient Topological Belief Graph (RSG) 用于在部分可观测条件下持续追踪安全相关状态；(2) Agency-Grounded Factual Memory 用于精确的危险预测；(3) Experience Memory 注入程序性元技能以指导可执行的、不阻碍进展的缓解动作。此外，提出自动 Test-Verify-Write 循环，使智能体在测试时从执行轨迹中持续优化缓解元技能。

Result: 在 IS-Bench 上，该框架在多个 VLM 骨干上显著提升安全成功率，例如在 Qwen3-VL-8B 上提升 +30.3%，能够主动缓解危险而不阻碍任务进展。

Conclusion: Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory 框架有效打破了具身安全与任务进展之间的权衡，通过持续记忆和在线自我进化实现了主动式危险缓解，为具身智能体的安全交互提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦具身智能体在家庭任务中的安全风险主动缓解，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 (embodied_world_model) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proactive embodied safety via self-evolving memory is novel but only tangentially touches world model and continuous representation priorities. Signals: world model for embodied safety, self-evolving memory, risk-sufficient topological belief graph, proactive hazard mitigation, IS-Bench benchmark

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have empowered embodied agents to execute complex household tasks, they struggle to proactively handle dynamically emerging hazards during closed-loop interactions. Existing safety approaches often rely on runtime guardrails to block unsafe actions or induce excessive caution, which severely stalls task progress instead of actively resolving the underlying risks. To break this safety-progress trade-off, we introduce the Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory framework, which reframes embodied safety from progress-stalling guardrails to proactive hazard mitigation. The framework consists of a Risk-Sufficient Topological Belief Graph (RSG) for persistent safety-relevant state tracking under partial observability, an Agency-Grounded Factual Memory for precise hazard anticipation, and an Experience Memory that injects procedural Meta-Skills to guide executable, progress-preserving mitigation. Furthermore, we propose an automated Test-Verify-Write loop, allowing agents to continually refine their mitigation Meta-Skills from execution traces at test time. Experiments on IS-Bench demonstrate that our framework substantially boosts the Safe-Success rate across multiple VLM backbones (e.g., +30.3% on Qwen3-VL-8B), enabling agents to proactively mitigate hazards without stalling task progress. Code is available at .

</details>


### [5] [Certifiable Safe Model-Based Reinforcement Learning with Control-Affine Dynamics Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16501)
*Hao Zhou, Yanze Zhang, Cameron Reid, Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (57.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可认证安全的模型强化学习框架，通过控制仿射随机傅里叶特征（ARFF）学习机器人动力学，并利用自适应共形预测（ACP）量化不确定性，结合控制屏障函数（CBF）实现数据驱动的安全策略合成，在cartpole和3D四旋翼平台上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法通常依赖部分已知的名义动力学模型，并通过数据驱动模型补偿残差不确定性，但往往忽略了残差不确定性的结构（如控制仿射分量），导致过于保守的行为或无效的安全保证。本文旨在利用控制仿射结构来提升安全策略的精确性和效率。

Method: 1) 使用控制仿射随机傅里叶特征（ARFF）对机器人动力学进行控制仿射形式建模，具有随数据集大小扩展的计算效率并减少模型偏差；2) 采用自适应共形预测（ACP）对学习到的控制仿射动力学中的安全约束不确定性进行无模型、高效的量化；3) 结合控制屏障函数（CBF）进行可认证安全的控制器合成。

Result: 在cartpole和3D四旋翼仿真平台上验证了所提框架的有效性，表明该方法能够在保证安全性的同时减少保守性，提供可认证的安全保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的安全强化学习框架通过显式建模控制仿射动力学结构并量化不确定性，实现了更高效且可认证的安全策略，为机器人安全探索与控制提供了新的理论支撑和实用方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的安全强化学习框架，学习控制仿射动力学模型，面向机器人安全探索与策略控制，属于具身智能/机器人领域的世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Importance reason: Focuses on safe RL with control-affine dynamics, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Safe RL, Control-affine dynamics, Control barrier functions, Adaptive conformal prediction, Robotics

Abstract: Safe model-based reinforcement learning (RL) often bridges control-theoretic analysis and RL for robots to safely explore (partially) unknown system dynamics while deriving control actions for task efficiency. The control performance and safety assurance typically rely on prior knowledge of partially modeled nominal system dynamics and the data-driven models that compensate for residual model uncertainties. However, existing methods often overlook the structure of residual model uncertainties (e.g., components affine in control), which could lead to overly conservative robot behaviors or invalid safety guarantees under the safe learning-based controllers. This paper proposes a safe reinforcement learning framework that learns control-affine dynamics with a certifiable data-driven safe policy using control barrier functions (CBF). Specifically, we first use Control-Affine Random Fourier Features (ARFF) to model robot dynamics in a control-affine form, which offers computational efficiency that scales with dataset size and reduces potential model bias for model-based reinforcement learning. Then, a model-free, efficient uncertainty quantification method using adaptive conformal prediction (ACP) is applied to quantify the uncertainty in the safety constraint arising from the learned control-affine dynamics. This allows for data-driven safety assurance amenable to principled and efficient controller synthesis with CBF. Simulation results on the cartpole and the 3D quadrotor platforms demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型、世界模型和智能体规划器各自推动了物理智能的发展，但它们缺乏统一的执行抽象、共享状态、语义验证以及跨异构具身形态的持久经验积累。这导致认知规划与物理执行之间耦合紧密，难以实现可扩展、可验证、可积累经验的具身智能系统。

Method: 提出 PhyAgentOS 运行时系统，核心设计包括：(1) 会话中心运行时，将会话而非单个动作作为最小调度、兼容性预检、监督执行、证据收集和验收单元；(2) 状态即文件协议，将跨层状态物化为 Markdown+YAML 格式，形成可检查、可版本化的记录，解耦 Agent 与 Runtime 层；(3) 会话验证器，通过证据驱动的判定（成功/失败/重规划）区分执行终止与语义任务完成；(4) 认知记忆，将验证结果整合为可复用知识与纠正经验，形成试错闭环；(5) 分层安全机制，包括预检、动作桥、安全守卫、心跳监控和目标约束；(6) 渐进式验证策略，从游戏到仿真再到真实机器人逐步验证。

Result: 在 Optimus-67、StarDojo 和 DST-Dojo 上进行了基准测试，在 19+ 个仿真和物理具身形态上验证，并在 LIBERO、Calvin 和 RoboCasa365 上使用多种 VLA 模型取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: PhyAgentOS 通过系统级的调度、验证、记忆和安全服务，为具身智能体提供了一种解耦认知与执行、支持经验积累和跨形态泛化的运行时基础，展示了系统级抽象在物理智能中的关键作用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PhyAgentOS，一个为具身智能体设计的运行时操作系统，核心贡献在于解耦认知规划与物理执行、提供会话级调度与验证、以及经验记忆闭环。这与世界模型方向下的 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配，因为论文直接面向机器人/具身智能体的规划、执行、验证与经验积累。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied agent OS architecture, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Embodied agent operating system, Cognitive-physical decoupling, State-as-a-File protocol, Session-centered runtime, No retraining experience loop

Abstract: Vision-language-action models, world models, and agentic planners each advance physical intelligence, yet their composition lacks a common execution abstraction, shared state, semantic verification, and persistent experience across heterogeneous embodiments. We present PhyAgentOS, a runtime foundation delivering scheduling, verification, memory, benchmarking, and safety as system-level services. Its Session-Centered Runtime treats a session, not an action, as the minimum unit of scheduling, compatibility preflight, supervised execution, evidence collection, and acceptance. To decouple cognition from physical execution, the cognition-physics boundary is a file system: the State-as-a-File protocol materializes cross-layer state as Markdown with YAML, yielding inspectable, versionable records without code dependencies between Agent and Runtime layers. These views form a unified cognitive state space aligning intent, capabilities, environment, execution, and experience. The SessionVerifier distinguishes execution termination from semantic task completion via evidence-grounded verdicts of success, failure, or replan. Verified outcomes are consolidated through epistemic memory into reusable knowledge and corrective lessons, closing a trial-and-error loop without retraining. Benchmarking reuses the deployment session and verification path, so results trace to real execution. Layered safety constrains both policy-driven and agent-driven execution: preflight, action bridges, SafetyGuard, heartbeat monitoring, and target-local constraints. Validation is progressive: games test cognitive planning, simulation adds dynamics and control, real robots add hardware noise, with the cognitive layer held constant. PhyAgentOS is benchmarked on Optimus-67, StarDojo, and DST-Dojo, validated on 19+ simulated and physical embodiments, and gains on LIBERO, Calvin, and RoboCasa365 across multiple VLA models.

</details>

Conclusion: G2-Nav通过将VLM的语义推理转化为可解释的代价地图，并引入多层次安全机制，有效弥合了抽象社交推理与真实世界部署之间的鸿沟，为机器人社交导航提供了一种可靠且可解释的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出G2-Nav框架，利用VLM将语义推理转化为代价地图，用于机器人社交导航（social navigation），属于面向机器人具身智能的规划与控制方法，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Abstract: Social navigation requires the robot to reason and respond in complex real-world environments. While recent works attempt to incorporate human-level intelligence into robot planning using large Vision-Language Models (VLMs), end-to-end frameworks often create an unpredictable black-box, and existing instruction-following methods are not designed for full autonomy. To bridge this gap, we present G2-Nav, a novel framework that grounds abstract social reasoning and guards safe real-world deployment. Instead of asking the VLM for direct planning decisions, G2-Nav translates its semantic reasoning into a vision-language costmap with reliability and interpretability. The VLM evaluates traversable regions and social agents from open-set perception, mapping social context into the costmap. To improve real-world robustness, the VLM performs semantic verification on upstream tracking, and we introduce a high-frequency safety check to guard against system latency prior to trajectory generation. We demonstrate through real-world experiments that G2-Nav delivers safe, efficient, and socially compliant autonomous navigation in unstructured environments. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度正则化的JEPA世界模型（Depth-Regularized JEPA），在训练中引入深度几何先验和各向同性潜空间正则化（SIGReg），从真实户外机器人视频数据中学习更鲁棒、更可迁移的潜空间动力学表征。在18M参数模型上，该方法在视觉里程计探测误差降低33%，异常检测分离度显著提升，且多步潜空间rollout保真度在域迁移下表现更优，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型在视觉复杂的真实户外环境中学习鲁棒动力学仍面临挑战，尤其是在不可预测的非结构化场景中。作者希望利用深度作为几何先验，结合潜空间正则化，使紧凑的世界模型能从真实机器人数据中学习到更通用、更可迁移的表征，同时不增加推理时的计算负担。

Method: 在LeWM（Learned World Model）基线基础上，引入两项改进：(1) 深度监督——利用深度估计作为几何先验，约束潜空间表征与场景几何结构一致；(2) SIGReg（各向同性正则化）——最大化任务无关的潜空间多样性，同时约束多样性的组织结构，使表征达到与场景几何一致的最高熵分布。此外，采用训练时过参数化（增加训练时模型容量但不增加推理时参数）来满足更高的复杂度需求。模型在真实农业机器人视频数据上训练，使用冻结表征的视觉里程计探测、预测器异常检测和多步潜空间rollout保真度进行评估。

Result: 与基线LeWM相比：视觉里程计探测误差降低33%；域内和域外（TartanGround基准）异常检测分离度显著提升；多步rollout保真度在域迁移下表现更优，且增益随rollout步长增加而增大；在光照和阴影等非直接3D几何相关的物理理解任务上，异常检测分离度也有提升。

Conclusion: 轻量级的训练时几何先验（深度监督）能使紧凑的JEPA世界模型在真实户外数据上学习到更强、更可迁移的表征，且不增加推理开销。深度作为物理先验可以增强世界模型在多种任务上的泛化能力，为在真实机器人场景中部署高效世界模型提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于JEPA架构的世界模型，利用真实户外机器人视频数据学习鲁棒的潜空间动力学，并引入深度先验和正则化提升表征迁移性。这直接对应世界模型方向下的[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）规范子主题，因为论文聚焦于农业机器人平台、视觉里程计探测、预测器异常检测等具身智能相关任务。

Importance reason: Depth-regularized JEPA world model improves latent dynamics transferability on real outdoor robot data, aligning with world model and efficient inference priorities. Signals: world model, JEPA, depth geometric prior, latent regularization, real outdoor robot data

Abstract: World models, especially based on JEPA architectures, have been shown to learn robust dynamics of various environments. However, learning from visually complex real-world data remains a challenge, especially in unpredictable outdoor environments. We introduce depth as a geometric prior during training in learning more robust latent dynamics directly from robot video data and handling visual complexity. This combines depth supervision with an isotropy-inducing latent regularizer (SIGReg), maximizing task-agnostic latent diversity while constraining how that diversity is organized, with the combined objective targeting the highest-entropy representation consistent with scene geometry. To satisfy this greater complexity without increasing inference time, we also add training-only overparameterization. Training an 18M-parameter model on video from a real agricultural robot, we evaluate with frozen-representation visual odometry probes, predictor-based surprise detection, and multi-step latent rollout fidelity. Compared to the baseline LeWM, our method lowers visual odometry probe error by 33%, substantially increases surprise-score separation both in-domain and on the out-of-domain TartanGround benchmark, and improves multi-step rollout fidelity under domain shift, with gains that grow with rollout horizon. Notably, we also see improvements in surprise-score separation on physics understanding that is not directly tied to 3D geometry, such as lighting and shadows. These results show that a lightweight training-time geometric prior makes a compact JEPA world model more useful and more transferable on real outdoor data with strong underlying representations, without adding inference overhead. Our work suggests that depth as a physically grounded prior can enhance world model generalization on a variety of tasks.

</details>


### [9] [Test-Time Scaling for World Action Models via Zero-Shot Geometric Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17454)
*Zesen Zhao, Minkyoung Cho, Hui shen, Boyuan Zheng, Kunxiao Gao, Yulong Cao, Z.Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gated-GeoWAM，一种无需训练的测试时扩展框架，利用交叉视图深度重投影一致性作为零样本几何评估指标，为 World Action Models 选择最优动作 rollout，并通过轻量级门控机制仅在必要时触发额外采样，在多个机器人基准上提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（test-time scaling）在基础模型推理中有效，但机器人控制中需要决定额外计算是否值得。World Action Models (WAMs) 天然支持这种决策：每个 rollout 同时输出动作块和预测的未来观测。本文旨在利用 WAMs 的这一特性，设计一种无需训练的、有选择性的测试时扩展框架，在提升任务成功率的同时控制计算开销。

Method: 提出 Gated-GeoWAM 框架：(1) GeoWAM：固定预算的 Best-of-N 选择器，使用冻结的几何基础模型（如 MASt3R）计算交叉视图深度重投影一致性分数，对 N 个 rollout 的未来预测进行排序并选择最优动作块；(2) Gated-GeoWAM：添加轻量级动作-未来一致性门控，仅当初始 rollout 内部不一致时才调用 GeoWAM 进行额外采样，否则直接使用初始 rollout。

Result: 在 RoboCasa、LIBERO Long 和 RoboTwin 2.0 五个基准-骨干设置上，固定预算 GeoWAM (N=8) 在所有设置中均提升任务成功率，例如 RoboCasa 组平均从 66.3% 提升至 68.4%（Cosmos Policy）和从 80.8% 提升至 82.5%（X-WAM）。Gated-GeoWAM 平均恢复 74.8% 的始终开启增益，但仅在 26.2% 的决策点触发额外采样。离线诊断表明交叉视图重投影是有效的无标签选择器，并识别出假低分选择是性能随 N 增大而饱和或下降的失败模式。

Conclusion: 本文验证了利用几何一致性作为零样本评估指标可以有效提升 World Action Models 的测试时性能，且门控机制能以较低计算成本实现大部分增益。交叉视图深度重投影一致性是一种有效的无标签 rollout 选择器，但假低分选择问题值得进一步研究。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 World Action Models (WAMs) 的测试时扩展方法，通过几何评估选择最优动作 rollout，属于机器人具身智能中的动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics world models with test-time scaling, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world action models, test-time scaling, zero-shot geometric evaluation, robotics, cross-view depth reprojection

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves foundation-model inference by spending additional computation, but robot control requires deciding whether extra compute is useful before executing an action. World Action Models (WAMs) make this decision natural: each rollout exposes both an action chunk and predicted future observations. We propose \methodgated, a training-free selective test-time scaling framework for WAMs. We first instantiate \method, a fixed-budget Best-of-$N$ selector that ranks sampled rollouts by cross-view depth reprojection consistency of their predicted futures, computed with a frozen geometry foundation model. \methodgated\ adds a lightweight action--future consistency gate that invokes \method\ only when the initial rollout appears internally inconsistent. Across five benchmark--backbone settings on RoboCasa, LIBERO Long, and RoboTwin~2.0, fixed-budget \method\ improves $N{=}8$ task success in every setting, e.g., raising the RoboCasa group average from $66.3\%$ to $68.4\%$ with Cosmos Policy and from $80.8\%$ to $82.5\%$ with X-WAM. With gating enabled, \methodgated\ recovers on average $74.8\%$ of the always-on success gain while triggering additional sampling on only $26.2\%$ of decision points. Offline diagnostics show that cross-view reprojection is a strong task-label-free selector, and we identify false low-score selections as a failure mode that helps explain why performance can saturate or degrade as $N$ increases.

</details>

*Yancheng Zhu, Wanli Ma, Chen Han, Irvin Haozhe Zhan, Bingfeng Qin, Yixin Xu*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在仿真和真实动态障碍物导航基准上，该方法显著提升了导航成功率并降低了碰撞率。在Unitree Go2机器人上的零样本sim-to-real迁移实验表明，控制器无需微调即可在室内杂乱环境和动态室外环境中导航，其避障行为与仿真中观察到的碰撞率降低一致。

Conclusion: 通过训练时的预测性监督，可以有效地将动态障碍物的短期演化信息编码到策略的循环状态中，从而在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升动态环境下的导航性能。该方法具有良好的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Signals: world model via JEPA-style predictor, zero-cost inference, sim-to-real on Go2, no continuous token or video generation, no autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning navigation policies for legged robots select actions reactively from current observations and short-term memory, with limited capacity to anticipate how moving obstacles will evolve in the near future. In dynamic environments, this reactivity causes the robot to respond too late because collision risk depends on short-horizon scene structure rather than on current obstacle positions alone. Lightweight predictive supervision applied to the policy's recurrent state during training can encode anticipatory obstacle dynamics without modifying the inference-time controller. We augment a reactive LSTM-SRU navigation backbone with an auxiliary JEPA-style predictor and SIGReg regularization: during training, the predictor supervises the deterministic hidden state to anticipate its own next state; at inference, it is fully discarded, incurring zero additional computational cost. On simulated and real-world navigation benchmarks with dynamic obstacles, our method substantially improves navigation success while reducing collision rates through the predictive training signal alone, without additional inference-time parameters. Real-robot deployment on a Unitree Go2 demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer: the controller navigates cluttered indoor and dynamic outdoor environments without fine-tuning, with evasive behavior consistent with the collision reduction observed in simulation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了推理步骤对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型鲁棒性的影响，对比了无推理、文本思维链和潜空间迭代循环三种架构在LIBERO和SimplerEnv上的表现。发现潜空间迭代模型在随机噪声和白盒攻击下任务成功率急剧下降，而其他两种模型保持稳健。推理输出的监控信号在自适应攻击下失效，表明当前行为级探测方法存在根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直觉上，在行动前进行推理的策略应该比直接映射观测到动作的模型更能吸收扰动输入。本文旨在验证这一假设，探究推理设计是否真的能提升VLA模型的鲁棒性，以及推理输出能否作为运行时安全信号。

Method: 选取三种覆盖不同推理谱系的VLA模型（无推理、文本思维链、潜空间迭代循环），在LIBERO和SimplerEnv环境中对视觉、推理、动作三个阶段分别施加随机噪声和白盒对抗攻击，系统评估任务成功率变化。同时设计plan-action一致性探测器和action-anomaly探测器，测试推理输出作为安全信号的可行性。

Result: 潜空间迭代模型在噪声和攻击下任务成功率急剧下降，而其他两种模型保持稳健。推理深度变化对鲁棒性影响极小，表明脆弱性源于结构而非累积效应。plan-action一致性探测器在朴素评估下表现近乎完美，但在自适应攻击下退化为随机水平。在匹配FPR校准下，融合该探测器与action-anomaly探测器从未将防御成功率提升至未防御水平以上。

Conclusion: 推理在VLA模型中是一把双刃剑：潜空间迭代推理反而引入结构性脆弱性，且推理输出的监控信号在自适应攻击下不可靠。任何可行的防御必须先满足输出级行为探测的基本前提条件。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在具身任务中的鲁棒性，涉及机器人策略、动作规划与控制，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLA robustness, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLA robustness, reasoning robustness, latent iterative model fragility, white-box attack, safety monitoring failure

Abstract: Does adding a reasoning step make a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model more robust to perturbation? Intuitively, a policy that reasons before acting should absorb a perturbed input better than one that maps observations directly to actions. We test this premise head-on across three models that span the reasoning spectrum (no reasoning, a text chain-of-thought, and a latent iterative loop), perturbing each at the vision, reasoning, and action stages on LIBERO and SimplerEnv. Two questions organize the study: does the reasoning design shift robustness, and can the reasoning be read back at runtime as a safety signal? We find that the latent-iterative model is by far the least robust: under both stochastic noise and white-box perturbation its task success collapses, while the other two hold. This fragility is structural rather than cumulative: varying the reasoning depth at inference barely moves it. Reasoning outputs can in principle be monitored, but the monitors fail under fair tests. A plan--action consistency probe that looks near-perfect under naive evaluation falls to chance under adaptive attack. Under matched-FPR calibration, fusing it with an action-anomaly probe never lifts defended success above undefended. Scoped to these output-level behavioral probes under white-box vision-stage attack, this ceiling is a precondition that any viable defense must first satisfy.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于预训练的潜空间世界模型（Ctrl-World），引入强化学习框架PAVXploreRL。通过奖励驱动训练显式优化PAV目标：物理合理性奖励、动作遵循性奖励和视觉保真度奖励。联合利用ID专家轨迹和噪声驱动的OOD动作探索进行训练，无需OOD动作的配对视频监督。

Result: 在多个基准上平均提升5.6%，生成更高质量的PAV属性。作为策略评估器时，能提供更可靠的表现估计，并减少了先前仅使用专家数据的世界模型（如Ctrl-World）的过估计偏差。

Conclusion: PAVXploreRL通过强化学习显式优化PAV目标并引入OOD动作探索，有效提升了动作条件世界模型的泛化能力和评估可靠性，为具身AI中的策略评估提供了更优方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PAVXploreRL，一个基于强化学习的动作条件世界模型框架，面向具身AI中的策略评估与动作泛化。核心关注动作条件世界模型的物理合理性、动作遵循性和视觉保真度，属于具身世界模型与规划控制范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous representation priorities, but lacks direct connection to top-weighted continuous language/multimodal subtopics. Signals: world model, reinforcement learning, action exploration, latent space, physical plausibility

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are a key component of embodied AI, serving as scalable policy evaluators that reduce reliance on expensive real-world rollouts. To accurately capture diverse action-induced dynamics, such models should satisfy three key objectives-Physical Plausibility (P), Action Adherence (A), and Visual Fidelity (V), collectively referred to as PAV-while remaining robust to both in-distribution (ID) expert demonstrations and out-of-distribution (OOD) actions. However, existing methods primarily rely on ID action-video pairs and pixel-level reconstruction losses, which do not explicitly optimize PAV objectives and generalize poorly beyond expert data. To address this, we propose PAVXploreRL, a reinforcement learning framework built on a pretrained latent world model that explicitly optimizes PAV objectives through reward-driven training. To improve action generalization, our method jointly leverages ID trajectories and noise-driven OOD action exploration, without paired video supervision. Experiments show that PAVXploreRL consistently outperforms pretrained baselines, achieving a 5.6% average gain across benchmarks and producing higher-quality PAV properties. As a policy evaluator, it also yields more reliable performance estimates and reduces the overestimation bias of prior expert-only world models such as Ctrl-World. Code:

</details>

TL;DR: RoboHarness 提出一个统一框架，将异构机器人控制策略（VLA、RL、TAMP等）封装为可复用的智能体技能，利用多模态执行记忆和在线证据进行能力感知的任务分解与路由，并通过 Memory Bridge 稳定策略切换，实现零样本长时域规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时域机器人规划方法通常假设同质化的预定义技能且适用性固定，无法有效利用异构策略的互补优势，且忽略了策略间能力边界的不确定性和跨策略分布不匹配问题。

Method: RoboHarness 包含三个核心组件：(1) 能力感知分解与路由——利用多模态执行记忆和在线证据刻画各策略的能力边界，将长任务分解并路由到合适的策略；(2) Memory Bridge——检索下一策略的历史执行轨迹，估计其分布内状态区域，引导机器人进入该区域以实现稳定切换；(3) 将 VLA、RL 策略和 TAMP 系统封装为统一接口的 agentic skills。

Result: 在三个公开基准、500个定制任务和135个真实机器人实验上，RoboHarness 实现了有效的能力感知路由和稳定的策略编排，在零样本长时域规划和分布外鲁棒性方面取得显著提升。

Conclusion: RoboHarness 证明了通过记忆驱动的异构策略编排可以有效解决长时域机器人规划中的能力边界不确定性和策略切换不稳定问题，为构建可扩展的机器人技能组合提供了通用框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长时域机器人任务规划，通过异构策略编排（VLAs、RL策略、TAMP等）实现长期任务分解与执行，属于具身智能中的规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot policy orchestration, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world_model, long-horizon planning, heterogeneous policies, zero-shot, memory bridge

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks require diverse capabilities that no single policy can reliably provide. Heterogeneous policies offer complementary strengths, but orchestrating them requires reasoning over uncertain capability boundaries and cross-policy distribution mismatch, which are largely overlooked by existing planning methods built on homogeneous, predefined skills with fixed applicability. We propose RoboHarness, a unified framework that encapsulates independently developed robot control systems as reusable agentic skills. Although instantiated in this work with VLAs, RL policies, and task-and-motion planning (TAMP) systems, RoboHarness is designed as a general framework compatible with a broader range of robot policies, such as navigation policies, model predictive controllers, and world-action models. RoboHarness uses multi-modal execution memory and online evidence to characterize policy capability boundaries for capability-aware decomposition and routing. To stabilize policy handoffs, its Memory Bridge retrieves execution trajectories associated with the next policy, estimates its in-distribution state region, and guides the robot toward that region without joint policy retraining. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks, 500 customized tasks, and 135 real-robot experiments demonstrate effective capability-aware routing and stable policy orchestration, yielding substantial improvements in zero-shot long-horizon planning and out-of-distribution robustness.

</details>

Abstract: This paper focuses on the problem of Embodied Task Planning, where an agent is required to execute a sequence of atomic actions within an interactive environment to complete a user-specified task. Though a variety of simulators and datasets have previously been built for this task, these efforts are largely isolated, with each using its own observation format, action type, and task domain. This fragmentation complicates comprehensive model evaluation and hinders the scalability of training data. As an effort towards generalizable embodied planning, we propose UniETP, a unified interface integrating four commonly-used simulators (AI2-THOR, VirtualHome, Habitat, BEHAVIOR). UniETP is characterized by both standardization and diversity. On one hand, it formalizes all the simulators into a consistent observation and action space, and builds an evaluation system to support complicated task goal. On the other hand, it enhances task diversity and complexity across dimensions like task logic, instance grounding, and instruction understanding, constructing a new dataset with varied levels of difficulty in an automatic manner. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark are conducted to evaluate the embodied planning capabilities of recent models and analyze the performance bottlenecks. Codes and data will be available at .

### [15] [Learning Adaptive Safety Margins for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18200)
*Junyi Hu, Shuaihang Yuan, Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala, Anthony Tzes, Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应安全裕度学习方法，通过上下文条件的安全评判器对扩散规划器生成的轨迹进行排序，结合安全、效率和距离约束三项损失，在 HM3D 和 MP3D 的 PointGoal 导航任务中取得最优结果，并零样本迁移到 Unitree G1 人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在杂乱室内环境中导航时，固定安全裕度往往不准确：保守裕度导致绕路和超时，激进裕度导致近边界捷径。扩散规划器能生成多样轨迹，但如何可靠选择最优轨迹仍是瓶颈。

Method: 提出上下文条件的安全评判器（safety critic），学习自适应安全裕度偏好来对扩散规划器生成的轨迹候选进行排序。评判器包含三项互补损失：(i) 安全项——包含安全裕度预算惩罚和控制障碍函数残差；(ii) 效率项——包含平滑度惩罚和安全门控的绕路比惩罚；(iii) 距离约束匹配项——将学习到的预算与真实 ESDF 距离对齐。训练时使用特权 ESDF 几何信息，再通过两阶段师生蒸馏迁移到纯感知选择器。

Result: 在 HM3D 和 MP3D 数据集上的 PointGoal 导航任务中，包括跨数据集迁移，该方法在成功率（SR）和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）上均优于强扩散、优化和 RL 基线。纯仿真训练后可直接迁移到 Unitree G1 人形机器人，无需任务特定微调。

Conclusion: 自适应安全裕度学习能有效解决固定安全裕度在视觉导航中的校准问题，通过上下文条件的安全评判器显著提升扩散规划器的轨迹选择质量，且具有良好的仿真到真实迁移能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于扩散规划器的视觉导航，提出上下文条件的安全评判器用于机器人路径规划与选择，属于具身智能中的规划控制问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Solid robotics navigation paper but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: Adaptive safety margin for visual navigation, Diffusion planner with learned safety critic, Zero-shot transfer to humanoid robot, Outperforms baselines on HM3D/MP3D, No direct link to continuous language or multimodal generation

Abstract: Robots in cluttered indoor spaces often fail not because they cannot generate collision-free paths, but because a fixed safety margin is mis-calibrated: conservative margins cause detours and timeouts, while permissive margins lead to near-boundary shortcuts under perception bias. Diffusion-based planners propose diverse trajectory candidates from egocentric RGB-D, yet reliable selection remains the bottleneck. We propose a context-conditioned safety critic that learns an adaptive clearance preference for ranking diffusion proposals, decomposed into three complementary terms: (i) a safety term with a clearance-budget penalty and a control-barrier-function residual for waypoint- and transition-wise safety, (ii) an efficiency term combining a smoothness penalty with a safety-gated detour-ratio penalty that avoids detours without incentivizing risky shortcuts, and (iii) a distance-constraint matching term that anchors the learned budget to realized ESDF clearances to prevent margin collapse. We train the critic with privileged ESDF geometry in simulation and distill it into a perception-only selector via a two-stage teacher-student procedure. On PointGoal navigation in HM3D and MP3D, including cross-dataset transfer, our method achieves the highest success rate (SR) and success weighted by path length (SPL) among strong diffusion, optimization, and RL baselines. Trained purely in simulation, it transfers to a Unitree G1 humanoid and navigates cluttered indoor scenes without task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [16] [Patch Policy: Efficient Embodied Control via Dense Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18236)
*Gaoyue Zhou, Zichen Jeff Cui, Ada Langford, Bowen Tan, Yann LeCun, Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (71.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Patch Policy 是一种轻量级架构扩展，使基于 Transformer 的机器人策略能够直接利用预训练 ViT 的密集 patch 特征（而非全局池化特征），通过块因果注意力掩码保持时序因果性，在多个模拟和真实环境中取得显著性能提升，参数量仅为 OpenVLA-OFT 的 0.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人策略要么将每帧观测压缩为单个全局 token（丢失细粒度空间信息），要么从头训练视觉骨干（无法利用大规模视觉预训练的优势），而像 VLA 这样使用密集 patch 特征的方法又因依赖数十亿参数的 VLM 骨干而过于笨重缓慢。作者希望填补这一空白，在保持轻量高效的同时利用密集预训练视觉特征。

Method: Patch Policy 的核心是一个块因果注意力掩码（block-causal attention mask），它在标准策略的时序因果性基础上，允许模型在每个观测时刻关注多个 patch token 以及其他状态信息。该架构直接使用预训练 ViT 的密集 patch 特征作为输入，无需完整的 VLM 骨干，是一种极简的 Transformer 策略扩展。

Result: 在 4 个模拟环境和 3 个真实世界环境套件中，Patch Policy 相比使用全局池化表征的最先进策略实现了 40% 的相对提升；同时超越微调后的 OpenVLA-OFT 18%，而参数量仅为其约 0.7%。

Conclusion: Patch Policy 为机器人社区提供了一种高效利用视觉表征学习进展的通用方案，在不牺牲训练效率和推理速度的前提下，显著提升了机器人策略的性能，尤其适用于需要高频反应式控制的场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Patch Policy，通过密集视觉 patch 特征提升机器人策略的性能，属于具身智能中的机器人控制与策略学习，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances efficient embodied world models by leveraging dense visual representations, aligning with high-priority world_model and efficient inference directions. Signals: world model, efficient inference, dense visual representations, embodied control, pretrained ViT

Abstract: Pretrained dense visual features from Vision Transformers (ViTs) are powerful yet have been underutilized in robot learning. Modern robot policies either compress each observation into a single global token, or rely on visual backbones trained from scratch, sacrificing both fine-grained spatial detail and the benefits of large-scale visual pre-training. While there exist policies that do operate on dense patch features like large vision-language-action models (VLAs), they tend to be heavy and slow, inheriting the full cost of a billion-parameter vision-language model (VLM) backbone. We close this gap with Patch Policy, a minimal architectural extension that enables transformer-based policies to consume dense pre-trained patch tokens directly without the computational overhead of a full VLM. At its core is a block-causal attention mask that preserves the temporal causality of standard policies while letting the model attend over many patch tokens per observation, alongside other state information. Patch Policy is lightweight, fast, and highly effective. Across four simulated and three real-world environment suites, our method achieves a 40% relative improvement over policies using state-of-the-art global-pooled representations. Furthermore, it surpasses fine-tuned OpenVLA-OFT by 18% while using roughly 0.7% of the parameters. We believe Patch Policy provides a pipeline for the robotics community to readily leverage continuing progress in visual representation learning, without sacrificing the training efficiency or inference speed required for high-frequency, reactive control. Videos can be viewed at

</details>

*Letian Cheng, Qi Zhang, Yisen Wang*

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAGE，一种基于子目标条件动作生成的潜世界模型规划方法。通过引入先验条件规划器，用目标条件生成器预测可达的潜子目标，并以此指导候选动作序列的生成，替代传统的随机初始化，显著提升了长时程规划性能。在 PushT 和 OGBench Cube 任务上，长时程规划成功率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型在长时程规划中面临候选动作空间随规划步数指数级增长的问题，固定候选预算下难以生成高质量的未来轨迹供世界模型评估，导致长时程规划性能严重下降。

Method: 提出 SAGE 框架：在每个规划阶段，一个目标条件生成器首先预测未来指定时长内可达的潜子目标，然后以该子目标为条件生成候选动作序列。通过使用不同时长的子目标作为先验，平衡细粒度局部控制与高层长时程进展。冻结的世界模型评估并优化这些子目标条件化的候选方案后执行。

Result: 在 PushT 和 OGBench Cube 基准上，当目标偏移为150时，SAGE 将 PushT 成功率从 12.7% 提升至 64.7%，OGBench Cube 成功率从 26.7% 提升至 67.3%，同时保持短时程规划性能不下降。

Conclusion: 将潜子目标分解与先验条件动作生成相结合，能够显著改善潜世界模型的长时程规划能力，为世界模型在复杂机器人任务中的应用提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究潜空间世界模型中的长时程规划问题，提出基于子目标条件的动作生成方法以改进规划质量，属于世界模型方向中具身智能/机器人规划控制的范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong world model planning method but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or generation. Signals: latent world model planning, subgoal-conditioned action generation, long-horizon planning improvement, PushT and OGBench Cube tasks, prior-conditioned planner

Abstract: Latent world models have emerged as a powerful planning paradigm by learning action-conditioned predictive dynamics and using them as internal simulators to imagine and evaluate candidate action sequences. However, as the planning horizon grows, performance becomes increasingly constrained by proposal quality: a fixed candidate budget must search an exponentially larger action space, making it difficult to expose the world model to high-quality candidate futures for evaluation. In this paper, we introduce a prior-conditioned planner that replaces random proposal initialization with structured guidance. At each planning stage, a goal-conditioned generator predicts the next reachable latent subgoal for a specified duration, which is then used to condition the generation of candidate action sequences. To capture semantic information across temporal scales, we use subgoals of varying durations as priors, balancing fine-grained local control with higher-level long-horizon progress. Then the frozen world model evaluates and refines these subgoal-conditioned proposals before execution. Experiments on PushT and OGBench Cube show that coupling latent subgoal decomposition with prior-conditioned action generation substantially improves long-horizon planning while preserving strong short-horizon performance. To be specific, when the target offset is $150$, it raises PushT success from $12.7\%$ to $64.7\%$ and OGBench Cube success from $26.7\%$ to $67.3\%$.

</details>

Method: 提出基于世界模型（World Model）的网络控制方法。世界模型从历史数据中训练，能够预测动作对未来网络状态的影响。控制器利用模型的 uncertainty estimate（不确定性估计）来鲁棒地寻找最优网络配置变更。优化目标可以在不重新训练模型的情况下动态切换。

Result: 在模拟闭环控制实验中，该方法在平衡节能与服务质量方面优于传统方法和强化学习方法。在真实网络数据上，世界模型能够有效预测网络状态，控制器在不同吞吐量约束下能提出合理的反事实动作。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的方法为移动网络控制提供了一种灵活、鲁棒且高效的解决方案，能够动态适应不同的优化目标，在节能与服务质量之间取得更好的平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于世界模型的移动网络控制方法，使用历史数据训练世界模型预测网络状态，并利用模型不确定性进行鲁棒优化控制。虽然应用领域是移动网络而非机器人/具身智能，但其核心思想（世界模型用于动作规划与控制）与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配，属于该子主题的泛化应用。

Importance reason: World model applied to mobile network control, but far from user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative video. Signals: world model for network control, robust optimization with uncertainty, energy-saving QoS balance, real network data evaluation, no generative or multimodal aspects

Abstract: The increasing complexity of mobile networks necessitates intelligent and dynamic control strategies for efficient, energy-conserving management. We propose a world model-based approach for network control that enables adaptive configuration of crucial parameters. The world model is trained from historical data and predicts the impact of its actions on future network states. Our controller leverages the model's uncertainty estimate to robustly find optimal network configuration changes. Furthermore, the optimization objective can be changed dynamically without model retraining. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach in simulated closed-loop control of a mobile network energy-saving feature. Our results show improved performance in balancing energy savings with quality of service, compared to traditional methods and reinforcement learning approaches. Finally, we show the world model performance on real network data from, and evaluate counterfactual actions proposed by the controller under various throughput constraints.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式文学模拟系统要么将角色视为静态人设模仿，要么进行孤立的场景生成，无法捕捉角色与世界随时间共同演化的动态过程。作者希望建模文学模拟为长程过程，其中角色交互、场景推进、角色与世界状态持续更新。

Method: 提出EvolvingWorld框架，包含两个耦合模块：(1) Character Agent负责多角色扮演和持续人设演化；(2) LLM-based World Model负责全局和位置/实体级状态维护及场景推进。采用开放模式(open-schema)框架支持多种文学世界。设计了7个可训练任务（场景初始化、交互生成、状态更新），从57本书构建数据集，提出轨迹级LLM-as-Judge评估协议。

Result: 实验表明EvolvingWorld在长程模拟中能有效维持角色与世界的连贯发展，在10维度20指标的评估中表现优于基线方法。

Conclusion: EvolvingWorld通过角色与世界协同演化的框架，为交互式文学模拟提供了更动态、更连贯的解决方案，证明了LLM世界模型在文学场景中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出EvolvingWorld框架，使用LLM构建交互式文学世界的世界模型，支持角色与世界的协同演化、场景推进和状态持久更新，属于可交互世界建模范畴。虽然面向文学而非物理世界，但其核心是LLM-based World Model用于交互式模拟与环境状态维护，与[可交互与物理世界建模]主题高度契合。

Importance reason: Focuses on literary world simulation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world model, role-play agents, open-schema, long-horizon simulation, literary worlds

Abstract: This paper introduces EvolvingWorld, a framework and benchmark for character and world co-evolution in interactive literary worlds. Existing systems either treat interactive literary simulation as static persona imitation or isolated scene generation, failing to capture how characters and worlds evolve together over time. To address this, EvolvingWorld models literary simulation as a long-horizon process where characters interact, scenes progress, and character and world states are persistently updated. Unlike prior systems relying on fixed schemas, EvolvingWorld adopts an open-schema framework to support simulation across diverse literary worlds. The framework consists of two coupled modules: a Character Agent for multi-character role-play and persistent profile evolution, and an LLM-based World Model for global and location/entity-level state maintenance and scene progression. Based on this architecture, we formulate 7 trainable tasks for scene initialization, interaction generation, and state update. We construct a dataset from 57 books, producing 138,596 supervised training samples and 222 snapshots for testing. Furthermore, we introduce a trajectory-level LLM-as-Judge evaluation protocol spanning 10 dimensions and 20 metrics. Experiments show that EvolvingWorld can improve long-horizon simulation by effectively maintaining persistent, coherent character and world development.

</details>

### [20] [Apple-$π$: Benchmarking Thinking with Video Towards Law-Grounded Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16401)
*Runmao Yao, Kairui Hu, Yukang Cao, Ruisi Wang, Shulin Tian, Ziang Cao, Weichen Fan, Ziqi Huang, Yuhao Dong, Hao Li, Zhaoxi Chen, Zhongang Cai, Lei Yang, Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

TL;DR: Apple-PI是首个基于物理定律评估视频生成模型的基准，包含400个经典力学视频数据集Orchard、三阶段科学推理评测协议（感知-公式化-演绎）以及混合评估套件。对11个模型的评测表明，当前最佳视频模型仅得0.473分，远未达到可靠的物理世界模拟器水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型被视作潜在的世界模型，但已有基准仅从输出层面评估物理合理性，无法验证模型是否通过忠实、基于物理定律的推理过程得出结果。缺乏能够诊断模型在哪个阶段（感知、公式化还是演绎）失败的细粒度评测工具。

Method: 构建Apple-PI基准，包含三部分：1) Orchard数据集——400个经典力学视频，分为单定律任务（无混杂因素诊断）和多定律任务（泛化探测）；2) 三阶段评测协议——基于科学推理的感知(Perception)、公式化(Formulation)、演绎(Deduction)，使用chain-of-frames提示在信息图标注的首帧上，将生成视频视为模型的可视化推理轨迹；3) 混合评估套件——结合MLLM主观评分和基于物理定律的客观度量，实现阶段级诊断。

Result: 对11个模型的评测显示，最佳视频模型仅得0.473分。阶段级、支柱级和来源级分析揭示了从感知到公式化再到演绎的瓶颈、弱多定律状态转移能力以及持续的Sim-to-Real差距。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型远未成为可靠的基于物理定律的世界模拟器。Apple-PI可作为诊断基础，指导未来视频模型向具备物理智能的世界模型发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了Apple-PI基准，用于评测视频生成模型是否具备基于物理定律的推理能力，属于生成模型评测。world_model方向下有generation_benchmark子主题（非规范但已存在），multimodal_generation方向下也有generation_benchmark规范子主题。论文核心是评测视频世界模型的物理定律遵循能力，因此主方向选world_model，同时匹配multimodal_generation。

Importance reason: Directly evaluates video generation models as world models, aligning with top-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model benchmark, video generation evaluation, physical law grounding, chain-of-frames reasoning, sim-to-real gap analysis

Abstract: Modern video generation models are increasingly hailed as emerging world models with an internalized grasp of physical law. Yet existing benchmarks largely evaluate physical plausibility only at the output level, without verifying whether the model arrives there through a faithful, law-grounded reasoning process. We introduce Apple-PI, the first benchmark that anchors video-model evaluation explicitly in physical laws. Apple-PI comprises three components. 1) Orchard: a dataset of 400 videos covering ten canonical tasks in classical mechanics. It separates single-law tasks for confounder-free diagnosis from multi-law tasks for probing generalization. 2) Benchmark Protocol: a three-stage protocol based on scientific reasoning, including Perception, Formulation, and Deduction. It uses chain-of-frames prompting on infographic-annotated first frames, treating the generated video as the model's visible reasoning trace. 3) Evaluation Suite: a hybrid evaluation suite that combines MLLM-based subjective scoring with physics-law-grounded objective measures. This enables stage-resolved diagnosis of not only whether a model fails, but where it fails. Benchmarking 11 models shows that current video models remain far from reliable law-grounded world simulators, with the best video model scoring only 0.473. Our stage-, pillar-, and source-resolved analyses further expose a Perception-to-Formulation-to-Deduction bottleneck, weak multi-law state transfer, and a persistent Sim-to-Real gap. These findings position Apple-PI as a diagnostic foundation for guiding future video models toward world models with law-grounded physical intelligence.

</details>

### [21] [VIDAR: Visual-Inertial Dense Alignment and Reconstruction via a Geometric Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17171)
*Diyari Mohammed Salih, Lingxiang Hu, Naima AitOufroukh-Mammar, Fabien Bonardi*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 空间 3D/4D 世界模型

TL;DR: VIDAR 是一个视觉-惯性密集对齐与重建框架，将 SVO+IMU 里程计与 Depth Anything 3 结合，利用视觉-惯性前端提供度量锚定（相机位姿、尺度、世界坐标系），使基础模型的稠密几何预测在时间上对齐并融合为全局重建。在 EuRoC 和 TUM RGB-D 上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目基础模型（如 Depth Anything 3）能提供稠密几何信息，但缺乏稳定的度量尺度，难以直接用于实际重建任务。现有方法要么依赖真值位姿，要么无法保证尺度一致性。作者希望将视觉-惯性里程计的度量信息与基础模型的稠密几何优势结合，实现无需真值位姿的度量稠密单目重建。

Method: VIDAR 框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 视觉-惯性前端（SVO+IMU 里程计），提供度量尺度的相机位姿和一致的世界坐标系；(2) 深度基础模型 Depth Anything 3 (DA3)，提供稠密局部几何。作者研究了两种对齐策略：位姿条件化 DA3（将位姿信息注入 DA3）和解耦对齐策略（将 DA3 预测的深度图通过视觉-惯性前端提供的位姿和尺度进行对齐）。最终将对齐后的稠密几何融合为全局重建。

Result: 在 EuRoC 数据集上，位姿注入策略将尺度误差降低至约 1%，达到 0.463 的平均 F@0.10；解耦混合策略进一步提升至 0.676（无需真值位姿）。在 EuRoC 和 TUM RGB-D 上的实验表明 VIDAR 是一种实用的度量稠密单目重建方法。

Conclusion: VIDAR 通过将视觉-惯性里程计与单目深度基础模型耦合，成功实现了度量稠密的单目重建。该方法无需真值位姿即可获得高精度的稠密重建结果，为实际应用中的单目重建提供了一条实用路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉-惯性密集对齐与重建框架，利用单目几何基础模型（Depth Anything 3）结合IMU里程计进行度量稠密重建，属于空间3D场景建模与重建，与 canonical subtopic "空间 3D/4D 世界模型" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Combines visual-inertial odometry with a geometric foundation model for metric-scale dense reconstruction, but is in robotics (cs.RO) and lacks direct connection to the user's priority subtopics. Signals: Robotics application, metric dense reconstruction, visual-inertial fusion, Depth Anything 3, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Monocular foundation models provide dense geometry but usually lack a stable metric scale. This paper presents VIDAR, a visual-inertial dense reconstruction framework that couples SVO+IMU odometry with Depth Anything 3. VIDAR uses the visual-inertial front end as a metric anchor: it provides camera poses, scale, and a consistent world frame for aligning dense foundation-model predictions across time. The foundation model then contributes detailed local geometry that is fused into a global reconstruction. We study both pose-conditioned DA3 and a decoupled alignment strategy. On EuRoC, pose injection reduces scale error to about 1\% and reaches 0.463 mean F@0.10; the decoupled hybrid improves this to 0.676 without ground-truth poses. Results on EuRoC and TUM RGB-D show that VIDAR is a practical route to metric dense monocular reconstruction.

</details>


### [22] [ReViV: Reconstructing the Viewer and the View in 4D from Monocular Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17790)
*Xiaozhong Lyu, Gen Li, Zhiyin Qian, Xucong Zhang, Marc Pollefeys, Siyu Tang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 空间 3D/4D 世界模型

Importance: 值得关注 (71.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReViV 是首个从单目第一人称RGB视频中统一重建观看者（全身动作、手部动作、注视方向）与观看场景（相机轨迹、深度）的4D动态表示的框架，采用掩码生成式第一人称Transformer，在单一前馈架构中联合建模多模态信号，实现快速推理，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称4D重建方法要么依赖预计算的相机轨迹等辅助输入，要么将场景感知和人体自运动建模作为独立问题处理，忽略了二者的强相互依赖性，且推理速度慢。亟需一个统一的、高效的端到端框架来同时重建观看者与观看场景的4D动态。

Method: 提出 ReViV，核心是 Masked Generative Egocentric Transformer（MGET）。将任务形式化为学习RGB视频、相机轨迹、注视方向、全身动作、手部动作、深度等多模态信号的联合概率分布。采用掩码生成式建模，在单一前馈架构中同时重建观看者（ego-body、手、注视）与观看场景（深度、相机轨迹）的时序一致4D表示。

Result: 在 HoloAssist、HOT3D、ARCTIC、Aria Digital Twin、TACO 等多个基准上，ReViV 在全身重建、手部重建、注视重建、相机跟踪方面达到SOTA精度，同时在第一人称深度估计上保持极具竞争力的性能，且推理速度快于现有方法。

Conclusion: ReViV 证明了统一的第一人称4D重建框架的可行性和有效性，通过联合建模观看者与观看场景的相互依赖关系，在精度和效率上均优于分离式方法，为可穿戴设备的第一人称感知与交互提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文从单目第一人称视频中联合重建观看者（人体/手/眼动）与观看场景（深度/相机轨迹）的4D动态表示，属于动态场景与空间世界建模，映射到世界模型方向下的[空间3D/4D世界模型]规范子主题。同时涉及4D动态场景生成，也匹配多模态生成下的[4D与动态场景生成]子主题。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous multimodal representation priorities, with high efficiency and unified 4D reconstruction from egocentric video. Signals: world model, unified multimodal, efficient inference, continuous visual token, 4D reconstruction

Abstract: Egocentric devices, such as wearable front-facing cameras, provide a unique perspective for capturing the continuous interaction between a human viewer and the surrounding environment. A holistic and efficient multimodal model capable of reconstructing this 4D representation is therefore highly desirable. However, existing approaches often rely on auxiliary inputs such as pre-computed camera trajectories, treat scene perception and human ego-motion modeling as separate problems despite their strong interdependency, and suffer from slow inference time. To address these limitations, we present ReViV, the first unified framework for holistic egocentric 4D reconstruction that extracts both viewer and view dynamics from a single monocular RGB video. We formulate the task as learning the full joint probability distribution over multimodal signals, including RGB video, camera trajectory, gaze direction, full-body motion, hand motion, and depth. Powered by a Masked Generative Egocentric Transformer, ReViV operates within a single feed-forward architecture to simultaneously reconstruct the temporally consistent 4D reconstruction across the viewer and the view with fast inference speed. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks, including HoloAssist, HOT3D, ARCTIC, Aria Digital Twin, and TACO, demonstrate that ReViV achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and efficiency across holistic ego-body, hand, and gaze reconstruction, camera tracking, while maintaining highly competitive egocentric depth estimation without relying on heavy task-specific priors. Code and models are fully open-sourced: .

</details>

*Darshan Deshpande*

TL;DR: 本文提出使用掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）作为文本世界模型，用于强化学习中的环境模拟。相比自回归语言模型，MDLM通过双向锚点感知去噪实现更好的连贯性、真实性和轨迹多样性。作者构建了239,403条状态-动作轨迹数据集，并在多个环境中验证了MDLM世界模型在零样本迁移下最高提升47%的绝对性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习需要多样化的训练环境，但手工设计的固定奖励环境随着模型性能提升而失效，且长程稀疏奖励会导致模式坍缩。现有自回归世界模型存在从左到右的偏置，无法对全局相互依赖的状态锚点（如工具模式、历史轮次、预期结果）进行条件控制。

Method: (i) 将文本世界建模形式化为可控状态转移动力学问题，分解为初始状态、任务上下文、工具模式、领域规则和引导指令五个组件；(ii) 构建包含239,403条轨迹、覆盖9个开源环境和12个前沿模型家族的数据集；(iii) 对比自回归LM和掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM），利用MDLM的双向锚点感知去噪实现更好的生成质量；(iv) 提出即插即用的GRPO训练框架，配合确定性状态检查进行零样本迁移。

Result: MDLM在连贯性、真实性和轨迹多样性上优于参数规模4倍的自回归LLM，且推理延迟相当。在ScienceWorld、ALFWorld、AppWorld三个OOD环境上，使用LFM2.5、Qwen3、Mistral三种1.2B-7B智能体骨干，零样本迁移下取得最高47%的绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散语言模型作为文本世界模型，凭借双向去噪能力克服了自回归模型的左到右偏置，在可控性、多样性和零样本迁移方面表现优异，为强化学习中的环境模拟提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的文本世界模型，用于强化学习中的环境状态模拟与动作条件生成。虽然世界模型通常与视频相关，但本文聚焦于文本状态空间中的世界建模（text-based world model），属于世界模型方向中"视频世界模型"（video_world_model）的广义范畴——该子主题涵盖生成式世界模型、动作条件生成与未来预测，本文的文本世界模型是其中的一种具体形式。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous language model priorities, with novel MDLM approach for steerable text-based world modeling in RL. Signals: world model, masked diffusion language model, steerable text-based world model, bidirectional anchor-aware denoising, RL agent training

Abstract: Recent growth in reinforcement learning (RL) has surfaced a need for diverse, specialized training environments. Hand-curated environments with fixed task and reward difficulties become ineffective signals as model performance improves, and sparse rewards over long horizons induce mode collapse on specific workflows or tool structures. World models that simulate environment states have matched pure rollout performance, making them promising for scaling diversity on-demand. However, autoregressive (AR) world models suffer from a left-to-right bias preventing conditioning on globally interdependent state anchors such as tool schemas, prior turns, and expected outcomes. We (i) formalize text-based world modeling as a steerable transition-dynamics problem decomposed into initial state, task context, tool schemas, domain rules, and steering directives, and (ii) curate 239,403 grounded state-action trajectories spanning nine open-source environments and twelve frontier model families. We compare AR LMs and masked diffusion language models (MDLMs), showing MDLMs, via bidirectional anchor-aware denoising, achieve better coherence, groundedness, and empirically validated rollout diversity than LLMs over 4x their parameter size, at comparable inference latency. We introduce a plug-and-play GRPO training framework with deterministic state checks, and perform zero-shot transfer ablations on three OOD environments (ScienceWorld, ALFWorld, AppWorld) across three 1.2B-7B agent backbones (LFM2.5, Qwen3, Mistral), achieving up to 47% absolute gains over baselines without environment-specific fine-tuning. We further conduct behavioral analysis of failure modes under adversarial scenarios and human evaluation on realism, outcome correctness, and training utility. We open-source our work to encourage research in this direction.

</details>

Result: 提出了从博弈算法到基础模型时代RL的统一视角，展示了RL如何连接决策制定、环境建模和基础模型能力，涵盖多智能体交互、扩散世界模型和交互式视频生成等多个方向的研究成果。

Conclusion: RL作为目标驱动的适应性框架，在从策略博弈到生成式世界模型的广泛复杂序列决策领域中具有统一性和普适性，为理解智能行为的基本原则提供了更广阔的视角。

Classification reason: 论文第二部分重点研究基于扩散的世界模型、交互式视频世界模型（actions shape future observations）以及长时序建模，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）范畴。

Importance reason: Directly covers world models, diffusion-based world models, and RL for video generation, aligning with top-weighted priority subtopics. Signals: world model, diffusion-based world model, RL for video generation, interactive video world model, long-horizon modeling

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a framework for sequential decision making under explicit objectives. In its classical form, RL studies how an agent should act to maximise long-term reward in a dynamic environment. In richer settings, the problem extends beyond a single agent and fixed environment: intelligent behavior may require strategic interaction, adaptation to uncertainty, and reasoning over high-dimensional worlds. This thesis studies RL from two perspectives: algorithms in games and RL in the era of foundation models. The first part focuses on multi-agent RL in games. It examines how incentives, policies, and equilibrium concepts interact in competitive and general-sum environments, spanning two-player zero-sum games, large-scale video games, and multi-player settings with general structure. These works investigate learning in multi-agent systems and the behavior of RL methods in interactive environments. The second part studies RL with generative and foundation models, motivated by the idea that prior knowledge can enrich sequential decision making. Pretrained generative models and learned world models serve as representation tools and structured priors for planning, control, and policy optimization. The thesis develops diffusion-based world models, investigates RL for efficient video generation, explores generative models as policy classes, and studies interactive video world models in which actions shape future observations. It also addresses long-horizon modeling through architectures with memory. Together, these contributions present a unified view of RL as objective-driven adaptation in complex sequential domains. From strategic games to generative world models, the thesis highlights how RL connects decision making, environment modeling, and emerging foundation-model capabilities, offering a broader perspective on the principles underlying intelligent behavior.

</details>


### [25] [Thinking in Video: Can Video Generators Really Reason About the Real World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17523)
*Yongheng Zhang, Guang Yang, Ruihan Hou, Qiguang Chen, Ziang Liu, Xiaolong Liu, Manman Zhang, Yanchao Hao, Zheng Wei, Hao Wu, Libo Qin, Peishan Dai, Yinghui Li, Di Yin, Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.1) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出"Thinking in Video"范式，认为视频生成模型不仅是生成工具，更是因果推理的媒介。为评估视频生成器是否具备真实世界推理能力，提出Causal-Generative Dual-Judge (CGDJ)评测框架，从显式因果感知（时空展平VQA）和隐式生成感知-预测差距两个维度审计世界模型一致性。实验发现开源模型虽有合理动态但近乎零显式因果感知，闭源系统推理与生成的对齐仍有限，且存在音视频错位现象，挑战了"世界模拟器"的说法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型被宣传为"世界模型"或"世界模拟器"，但其生成的视频可能只是记忆了外观模式而非真正理解因果关系。现有评测指标将感知保真度与语义逻辑分开评估，无法判断模型是否具备因果推理能力。因此需要一套统一的评测框架来检验视频生成器是否真正能进行因果推理。

Method: 提出Causal-Generative Dual-Judge (CGDJ)评测框架，包含两个视角：(1) 显式因果感知（Explicit Causal Perception）——通过时空展平的视觉问答（VQA）测试模型是否将视频场景解读为推理问题；(2) 隐式生成感知-预测差距（Implicit Generative Perception-Prediction Gap）——评估模型是否将因果后果渲染为一致的未来视频。对多个开源和闭源视频生成器进行系统评测。

Result: 开源模型能生成看似合理的动态，但显式因果感知近乎为零；先进闭源系统在推理与生成之间表现出更强的但仍有局限的对齐；存在音视频错位现象——模型在语言上能正确表达因果逻辑，但在视觉渲染中却无法一致呈现。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型作为世界模型的因果推理能力仍然有限，存在"感知-预测差距"和"音视频错位"问题，挑战了"世界模拟器"的说法。视频生成模型距离真正的因果推理和世界理解还有显著差距。

Classification reason: 论文研究视频生成模型是否具备对真实世界的因果推理能力，核心是评估视频生成器作为世界模型的推理能力，属于世界模型方向。同时涉及视频生成评测，也匹配多模态生成方向。子主题复用 [CANONICAL] video_world_model，因为论文聚焦于视频生成式世界模型的因果推理评测。

Importance reason: Directly evaluates world model consistency in video generators, a core priority subtopic with high weight. Signals: world model evaluation, causal reasoning in video generation, perception-prediction gap, CGDJ benchmark, challenges world simulator narrative

Abstract: Recent advances in world models and video generation have given rise to an emerging reasoning paradigm that leverages video generative models to simulate, predict, and reason about real-world dynamics. We redefine this paradigm as Thinking in Video, where video is not merely an output artifact but a medium for constructing, extending, and verifying causal thought. However, this promise remains unverified: convincing rollouts may reflect memorized appearances rather than causal understanding, while existing metrics separate perceptual fidelity from semantic logic. To evaluate whether video generators support such reasoning, we introduce the Causal-Generative Dual-Judge (CGDJ), auditing World Model Consistency from two perspectives. Explicit Causal Perception tests whether a generator reads a video scenario as a reasoning problem through spatio-temporal flattened visual question answering, while Implicit Generative Perception-Prediction Gap evaluates whether it renders the causal consequence as a consistent future video. Applying CGDJ to representative open- and closed-source generators reveals a clear Perception-Prediction Gap: open-source models produce plausible dynamics despite near-zero explicit causal perception, whereas advanced closed-source systems show stronger but still limited alignment between reasoning and generation. Further analysis exposes audio-visual misalignment, where models verbalize correct causal logic more reliably than they render it, challenging the "world simulator" narrative.

</details>

### [26] [GEAR: Reconstruction of Classical Paintings via Geometry Grounding and Appearance Restitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17519)
*Qinyu Zhang, Xinda Liu, Yunchen Li, Yunzhuo Liu, Chenxi Hu, Kang Li, Guohua Geng*

Main category: cs.MM

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: 本文提出古典绘画到3D（CP3D）新任务及GeAR框架，通过几何接地和外观恢复两阶段方法，从单幅古典绘画重建几何合理且外观忠实于原作的3D场景，并构建了HeriArch基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古典绘画蕴含丰富的空间、文化和历史内容，将其重建为可探索的3D场景对数字保存、沉浸式展览和文化参与具有重要价值。然而，与照片不同，古典绘画通常以单视图、风格化方式描绘场景，缺乏透视、光照和深度线索，现有基于自然图像先验的3D重建方法难以恢复几何合理且视觉忠实的3D表示。

Method: 提出GeAR（Geometry Grounding and Appearance Restitution），一个无需训练的两阶段框架。第一阶段（几何接地）：将输入绘画转换为具有更一致明暗和光照线索的几何接地表示，提升3D高斯重建的稳定性。第二阶段（外观恢复）：在空间约束和多视图一致性下恢复忠实于原作的跨视图外观，恢复在接地阶段被削弱的绘画纹理和细节。

Result: 在构建的HeriArch基准（10,160幅高分辨率古典艺术作品）上的大量实验和用户研究表明，GeAR在几何合理性、外观忠实度和人类偏好方面持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: GeAR有效解决了从单幅古典绘画重建3D场景的挑战，通过几何接地和外观恢复的两阶段策略，在无需训练的情况下实现了几何合理且视觉忠实的3D重建，为古典绘画的数字保存和沉浸式展示提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出从单幅古典绘画重建3D场景的任务（CP3D），使用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行几何重建与外观恢复，属于3D生成与重建范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on 3D reconstruction from classical paintings, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: 3D reconstruction, classical paintings, single-view, training-free, benchmark dataset

Abstract: Classical paintings preserve rich spatial, cultural, and historical content, making their reconstruction as explorable 3D scenes valuable for digital preservation, immersive exhibition, and cultural engagement. Yet, unlike photographs, they often depict scenes in a single-view, stylized manner, with weak perspective, lighting, and depth cues. Existing 3D reconstruction methods are largely built on natural-image priors, making it difficult to recover geometrically plausible and visually faithful 3D representations from such inputs. To address this challenge, we introduce Classical Painting-to-3D (CP3D), a new task that aims to recover a 3D representation from a single classical painting while jointly ensuring geometric plausibility, appearance fidelity to the source artwork, and plausible novel-view synthesis. We further propose GeAR, a training-free two-stage framework for Geometry Grounding and Appearance Restitution. GeAR first converts the input painting into a geometry-grounded representation with more coherent shading and illumination cues, improving the stability of 3D Gaussian reconstruction. It then restores artwork-faithful appearance across views under spatial constraints and multi-view consistency, recovering the painterly textures and details weakened during grounding. In addition, we construct HeriArch, a curated benchmark of 10,160 high-resolution classical artworks for systematic evaluation of CP3D. Extensive experiments and user studies show that GeAR consistently outperforms strong baselines in geometric plausibility, appearance fidelity, and human preference. Code and dataset will be released publicly.

</details>


### [27] [Depth Estimators Are Implicit Neural Fields for 3D Scene Geometry Inpainting and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16286)
*Yingzhao Jian, Zihao Lin, Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: 本文提出 Neural Depth Field (NDF)，核心洞察是深度估计器可以同时作为场景级隐式神经场。作为估计器，它通过学习观测深度数据适应目标域；作为隐式场，它拟合已有几何以保持一致性。通过单次测试时优化，NDF同时解决了预测结果与观测几何不一致以及分布外数据不可靠的问题。实验表明，NDF在室内扫描到卫星图像等多种场景数据上生成高保真、全局一致的几何，跨视图不一致性降低63.3%，补全精度提升23.1%，达到3D场景几何补全的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界3D场景数据往往存在几何不完整问题。现有方法使用深度估计器补全缺失结构，但存在两个关键问题：(1) 预测结果与已观测几何不一致；(2) 在分布外数据上不可靠。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出 Neural Depth Field (NDF)，将深度估计器重新解释为场景级隐式神经场。通过单次测试时优化，NDF同时学习观测深度数据（作为估计器适应目标域）和拟合已有几何（作为隐式场保持一致性），从而在补全缺失几何的同时保证与观测数据的一致性。

Result: 在室内扫描到卫星图像等多种场景数据上，NDF生成高保真且全局一致的几何。跨视图不一致性降低63.3%，补全精度提升23.1%，达到3D场景几何补全的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明深度估计器可以同时作为隐式神经场，通过测试时优化统一了深度估计与几何一致性约束，为3D场景几何补全提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Neural Depth Field (NDF)，将深度估计器视为场景级隐式神经场，用于3D场景几何补全与重建。这属于3D场景重建与神经渲染范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel use of depth estimators as implicit neural fields for 3D inpainting, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous language/video generation. Signals: 3D scene geometry inpainting, Neural Depth Field, test-time optimization, cross-view consistency, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: The 3D geometry of real-world scene data is often incomplete. Mainstream methods use depth estimators to inpaint missing structure. However, their prediction results can be inconsistent with observed geometry, or unreliable on out-of-distribution data. To solve these problems, we propose Neural Depth Field (NDF). Our key insight is that a depth estimator can also be a scene-level implicit field. As an estimator, it adapts to the target domain by learning observed depth data. As an implicit field, it fits the existing geometry to maintain consistency. Under this view, NDF addresses both problems through a single test-time optimization. Experiments show that NDF produces high-fidelity and globally consistent geometry across diverse scene data, ranging from indoor scans to satellite imagery. It reduces cross-view inconsistency by 63.3\% and improves inpainting accuracy by 23.1\%, achieving state-of-the-art performance in 3D scene geometry inpainting. The code is available at: .

*Xiaoye Zhu, Weixin Li, Junan Huo, Bozhong Wang, Jia Zeng, Yi Yang, Cen Chen, Qi Liu*

TL;DR: CLARE 是一个自进化的 3D 智能体，通过解耦生成流水线为四个认知角色，主动识别并解决用户意图与 3D 工具参数之间的不对称问题，在 3D 资产创建任务中通过多轮对话澄清模糊指令，并利用模拟交互自进化澄清策略，在 3D-Clarify 基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 智能体将用户模糊指令视为噪声，默认在单轮假设下盲目执行，导致意图不对称问题严重。作者旨在将这种不对称视为策略性对话的机会，通过主动澄清来提升 3D 工具编排的鲁棒性。

Method: CLARE 将生成流水线解耦为四个认知角色（分析、澄清、执行、进化），在调用计算昂贵的 3D 工具前拦截并解决不明确的指令。通过模拟多轮交互自进化澄清策略，并优化多轮奖励函数以平衡交互效率与任务完成度。同时构建了 3D-Clarify 基准，包含 620 个带有系统注入歧义、缺失信息和错误细节的交互场景。

Result: CLARE 在单步和多步任务上分别达到 60.40% 和 43.34% 的成功率，比现有基线方法提升超过一倍，验证了主动澄清对鲁棒 3D 执行的关键作用。

Conclusion: 主动澄清是解决 3D 工具编排中意图不对称的关键，CLARE 通过自进化策略有效平衡了交互效率与任务完成度，为 3D 智能体设计提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 CLARE，一个面向 3D 工具编排的智能体，涵盖 text-to-3D 生成、单视图/多视图重建、点云编辑等 3D 任务，属于 3D 生成与重建范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 three_d_generation。

Importance reason: Novel agent framework for 3D tool orchestration, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D asset creation, intent asymmetry, self-evolving agent, multi-turn clarification, 3D-Clarify benchmark

Abstract: A fundamental intent asymmetry plagues modern 3D asset creation: while state-of-the-art 3D toolchains demand precise, executable parameters, ordinary users typically provide vague, underspecified instructions. Current 3D agents treat this ambiguity as noise, defaulting to blind execution under a single-turn assumption. To address this limitation, we introduce CLARE, a clarification-aware and evolutionary 3D agent that treats intent asymmetry not as an execution error, but as an opportunity for strategic dialogue. By decoupling the generation pipeline into four specialized cognitive roles, CLARE intercepts and resolves underspecified instructions before invoking computationally expensive 3D tools to seamlessly execute tasks across five diverse domains: text-to-3D generation, single-view reconstruction, multi-view reconstruction, point cloud editing, and post-processing. Crucially, rather than relying on rigid manual rules, CLARE self-evolves its clarification policy via simulated multi-turn interactions. By optimizing a Multi-turn Reward, the agent internalizes the delicate balance between interaction efficiency and task completion. To rigorously test this, we construct 3D-Clarify, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 620 interaction scenarios with systematically injected ambiguity, missing information, and mistaken details. CLARE achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 60.40% and 43.34% success rates on single-step and multi-step tasks, respectively, more than doubling existing baselines. Both quantitative and qualitative results demonstrate that proactive clarification is the missing key to robust 3D execution. Code is available at .

</details>

Result: 在多种3D生成模型上的定量和定性评估表明，该方法在类别无关的3D形状编辑任务上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于耦合神经形状（CNS）表示的3D形状编辑框架，属于3D物体/场景编辑与生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D shape editing, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: 3D editing, category-agnostic, neural shape representation, KV-cache replacement, latent feature regularization

Abstract: This paper presents a latent-space 3D shape editing framework built upon a coupled neural shape (CNS) representation and a neural feature volume optimization. This work extends CNS-Edit, built on Coupled Neural Shape optimization, to CNS-Edit++, by generalizing the category-specific coupled representation to category-agnostic 3D shape editing with foundation models. The Coupled Neural Shape (CNS) representation couples a global latent code that captures high-level shape semantics with a 3D neural feature volume that provides spatial context for local shape manipulation. Then we formulate a coupled neural shape optimization procedure that co-optimizes these two components subject to a given editing operation. Our framework can be instantiated on both the category-specific 3D inversion model and category-agnostic 3D foundation models. We provide various shape editing operators, including copy, resize, delete, mix, point-wise drag, and region-wise drag, each of which is formulated as an objective to guide the CNS optimization. To preserve regions outside the editing area, we further introduce two complementary region-wise control mechanisms, i.e., KV-cache replacement and latent feature regularization. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations across different 3D generative models demonstrate the strong capabilities of our approach over state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>
