TL;DR: Scene-SAM3D 是一个无需微调的训练自由框架，将单视图 3D 生成模型 SAM3D 扩展到多视图场景资产生成。通过选择互补视图、潜速度融合和轻量级刚性物体高斯优化，在 Replica 和 ScanNet++ 上显著提升了场景级 3D 生成质量，同时降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单图像 3D 生成模型（如 SAM3D）在真实场景中因严重遮挡、冗余观测和跨视图不一致而表现不足，难以生成可靠的场景级 3D 资产。需要一种无需额外训练的方法来利用多视图信息生成高质量场景资产。

Method: Scene-SAM3D 包含三个关键步骤：(1) 选择一组紧凑的互补视图，减少观测冗余同时为遮挡区域提供额外证据；(2) 基于所选视图进行高效潜速度融合（latent velocity fusion），在规范空间中整合多视图证据并抑制跨视图冲突；(3) 轻量级刚性物体高斯优化（rigid-object Gaussian optimization），在 200 次迭代内优化场景布局同时保持生成物体的几何结构。

Result: 在 Replica 和 ScanNet++ 数据集上，场景级 Chamfer Distance 分别降低 43.8% 和 30.9%；在相同多视图设置下，流模型采样 FLOPs 和延迟降低近 20%。实例级和场景级均有一致提升。

Conclusion: Scene-SAM3D 有效将单视图 3D 生成模型扩展到多视图场景生成，通过视图选择、潜空间融合和轻量级优化，在无需微调的情况下显著提升了场景 3D 资产的质量和效率，为具身智能应用提供了更可靠的 3D 场景资产。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Scene-SAM3D，从多视角图像生成 3D 场景资产，涉及 3D 场景生成、3DGS 优化，属于 [CANONICAL] 3D 生成与重建 (three_d_generation) 范畴。

Importance reason: Extends single-view 3D generation to multi-view scenes without fine-tuning, but the subtopic (3D generation) is not a priority and lacks direct alignment with continuous language/multimodal or video generation. Signals: training-free multi-view extension, scene-level 3D generation, latent velocity fusion, rigid-object Gaussian optimization, reduces CD and FLOPs

Abstract: High-quality 3D scene assets are critical for embodied applications such as robotic manipulation, navigation, and simulation. Despite their strong object priors, recent single-image 3D generation models such as SAM3D remain insufficient for real-world scenes, where severe occlusions, redundant observations, and cross-view inconsistencies make reliable scene generation challenging. We introduce Scene-SAM3D, a training-free framework that extends SAM3D from single-view object generation to calibrated multi-view scene asset generation. Scene-SAM3D selects a compact set of complementary views, reducing observation redundancy while providing additional evidence for regions occluded in individual views. Based on the selected views, it performs step-efficient latent velocity fusion to integrate multi-view evidence and suppress cross-view conflicts in canonical space. Finally, a lightweight rigid-object Gaussian optimization refines the scene layout within 200 iterations while preserving the generated object geometry. Experiments on Replica and ScanNet++ demonstrate consistent improvements at both instance and scene levels, with our method reducing scene-level CD by 43.8% on Replica and 30.9% on ScanNet++, while cutting flow-model sampling FLOPs and wall-time latency by nearly 20% under the same multi-view setting. Code will be released at .

</details>


### [31] [TopoGS: Planar Reconstruction via Topology-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16838)
*Shanshan Pan, Jiale Chen, Yilin Liu, Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: TopoGS 是首个在 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架中显式集成平面约束与拓扑约束的方法，通过从多视图图像分割中提取全局 2D 拓扑关系并锚定高斯原语，联合优化平面参数、渲染保真度和拓扑邻接关系，在 ScanNet++ 上实现了 SOTA 的结构化、拓扑一致且视觉保真的 3D 场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 3DGS 的平面重建方法将平面视为孤立的离散集合，缺乏拓扑连接性，导致重建结果碎片化、边界不对齐，无法满足精确空间分析和专业设计工作流的需求。

Method: TopoGS 从多视图图像分割中提取全局 2D 拓扑关系，将高斯原语锚定到这些结构元素上，联合优化平面参数、渲染保真度和拓扑邻接关系，并通过严格的多视图一致性约束来减少几何不对齐。

Result: 在 ScanNet++ 数据集上的大量实验表明，TopoGS 达到了 SOTA 性能，能够生成准确、拓扑一致且视觉保真的场景表示。

Conclusion: TopoGS 通过显式引入拓扑约束有效解决了 3DGS 平面重建中的碎片化和边界不对齐问题，为生成结构化、可编辑的 3D 场景表示提供了鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TopoGS，在 3D Gaussian Splatting 框架中引入拓扑约束进行平面重建与结构化 3D 场景重建，属于 3D 生成与重建方向，与 canonical 子主题 three_d_generation 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D reconstruction topology, not aligned with user's priority on continuous language/video generation or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, planar reconstruction, topological constraints, ScanNet++ dataset, structured 3D models

Abstract: Extracting structured, parametric 3D representations from raw images remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision and graphics. While recent advancements in the 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) pipeline integrate planar primitives to yield compact and editable geometry, these approaches typically treat planes as isolated, discrete sets. This lack of topological connectivity hinders robust geometric reasoning, leading to fragmented reconstructions and misaligned boundaries that fall short of the precision for rigorous spatial analysis and professional design workflows. To address this, we introduce TopoGS, the first 3DGS framework to explicitly integrate both planar and topological constraints for coherent 3D reconstruction. Specifically, we extract global 2D topological relationships from multi-view image segmentations and anchor Gaussian primitives to these structural elements. This formulation enables the joint optimization of plane parameters, rendering fidelity, and topological adjacency. By enforcing strict multi-view consistency alongside these topological constraints, our method significantly mitigates geometric misalignments and produces connected, structured 3D models. Extensive evaluations on the ScanNet++ dataset demonstrate that TopoGS achieves state-of-the-art performance, providing a highly robust solution for generating accurate, topologically sound, and visually faithful scene representations.

</details>


### [32] [Autoregressive B-Rep Shape Generation with Parametric Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17093)
*Dafei Qin, Rui Xu, Zeyu Shen, Kaichun Qiao, Hongyang Lin, Qixuan Zhang, Huaijin Pi, Lan Xu, Jingyi Yu, Wenping Wang, Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: ParaCAD 是一个自回归框架，用于点云条件化的 B-Rep 生成，直接操作原生参数化曲面，通过曲面中心 tokenization 编码面类型和连续参数，再通过全局求交恢复边和顶点，生成高保真、水密的 CAD 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 B-Rep 生成方法依赖均匀采样的点或网格表示，丢失了原生曲面类型和参数信息，限制了几何保真度和下游可用性。作者希望直接生成原生参数化曲面，保留 CAD 几何的固有语义。

Method: ParaCAD 提出曲面中心 tokenization，将每个面编码为曲面类型和连续参数；自回归模型先生成带约束 UV 域的参数化曲面，再通过全局曲面求交恢复边和顶点，构建完整的 B-Rep 结构。以点云为条件输入，支持用户引导重建。

Result: 实验表明 ParaCAD 生成的 B-Rep 在几何精度、鲁棒性、水密性和下游可用性上均优于基于点的基线方法，点云对齐忠实。

Conclusion: 直接操作原生参数化曲面进行自回归 B-Rep 生成是可行且有效的，能够保留 CAD 几何语义，提升生成质量与实用性，可无缝集成到现有 3D 生成管线中。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ParaCAD，一个面向 CAD 的 B-Rep 形状生成框架，属于 3D 生成范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 涵盖 3D 物体/场景生成、Mesh 生成等，B-Rep 是 CAD 中核心的 3D 几何表示，因此映射到该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D CAD B-Rep generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks the specified boost keywords. Signals: 3D generation, CAD modeling, autoregressive generation, parametric surfaces, point cloud conditioning

Abstract: Generative CAD modeling has broad design and application potential. Despite significant advances in Boundary Representation (B-Rep) generation, the dominant representation in CAD, existing methods largely depend on uniformly sampled point- or grid-based geometry representations, sacrificing native surface types and parameters and thereby limiting geometric fidelity and downstream usability. We present ParaCAD, an autoregressive framework for point-cloud-conditioned B-Rep generation that directly operates on native parametric surfaces. ParaCAD introduces a surface-centric tokenization that explicitly encodes each face by its exact surface type and continuous parameters, preserving the intrinsic semantics of CAD geometry. Our model first generates parametric surfaces with constrained UV domains, and then constructs a valid B-Rep by globally intersecting these surfaces to recover edges and vertices. ParaCAD places point-cloud-conditioned generation at the core of B-Rep synthesis, making it practical for user-guided reconstruction and seamless integration into existing 3D generation pipelines. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ParaCAD produces accurate B-Reps with faithful point-cloud alignment, outperforming point-based baselines in geometric precision, robustness, watertightness and downstream usability.

</details>


### [33] [CaT-GS: Efficient 3DGS Rendering for Large Scale Scenes via Inter-frame Caching and Tile Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17842)
*Tingjia Zhang, Bo Chen, Shengzhong Liu, Fan Wu, Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CaT-GS 提出了一种高效的 3DGS 渲染管线，通过投机性多帧预处理、帧间缓存和 tile 级负载均衡调度，在大规模场景下实现最高 10 倍加速，显著提升了 GPU 利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 在大规模场景中性能严重下降，现有优化方法要么需要昂贵的场景重训练，要么只关注管线的狭窄环节，忽视了实际部署中的关键效率瓶颈。作者通过系统分析识别出三大冗余与低效来源：帧间预处理冗余、基于视点的遮挡冗余以及 tile 级负载严重不均衡。

Method: CaT-GS 包含三个核心设计：(1) 投机性多帧预处理方法，消除连续帧间的冗余计算；(2) 帧间缓存机制，消除视点冗余的渲染阶段；(3) 专用内核重构光栅化任务，缓解 tile 负载不均衡，大幅提升 GPU 利用率。

Result: 在多个大规模场景数据集上，CaT-GS 相比原始 3DGS 实现最高 10 倍加速，相比此前最优方法提升高达 70%，同时保持高保真渲染质量。

Conclusion: CaT-GS 通过系统性地解决 3DGS 在大规模场景中的计算冗余和负载不均衡问题，建立了一个高保真实时渲染的新基准，为 3DGS 在大规模场景中的实际部署提供了高效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在大规模场景下的高效渲染优化，属于3D生成与重建方向中的神经渲染子领域，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS rendering efficiency, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3DGS acceleration, large-scale scene rendering, inter-frame caching, tile scheduling, GPU utilization improvement

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have advanced neural rendering with high fidelity and speed. However, its performance degrades significantly in large-scale scenes due to the computational burden of tile-based rasterization. Existing optimization efforts either require costly scene re-training or focus on narrow aspects of the pipeline, overlooking critical inefficiencies in real-world deployments. Through a comprehensive analysis, we identify three primary sources of redundancy and low GPU utilization: redundant inter-frame pre-processing, viewpoint-based occlusion redundancy, and severe tile-level load imbalance. To address these issues, we propose CaT-GS, a novel and efficient 3DGS rendering pipeline. CaT-GS introduces a speculative multi-frame preprocessing method to eliminate redundant computations across consecutive frames, and an inter-frame caching mechanism to eliminate viewpoint redundant rendering stages. Furthermore, it refactors rasterization tasks with a dedicated kernel to mitigate tile load imbalance, significantly boosting GPU utilization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CaT-GS achieves a speedup of up to 10 times over the original 3DGS and up to 70% over previous state-of-the-art methods, establishing a new benchmark for high-fidelity, real-time rendering of large-scale scenes.

</details>


### [34] [Exploration Matters for Escaping the Blur Trap in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17965)
*Chengbo Wang, Guozheng Ma, Jinhong Wu, Tie Ji, Yizhen Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (49.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文分析了 3D Gaussian Splatting 优化中的梯度偏差问题，将其定义为"模糊陷阱"(Blur Trap)，并分为远侧和近侧两种子类型。作者提出两种简单的探索策略——随机播种(Random Seeding)和随机分裂(Random Splitting)——分别缓解两类模糊陷阱，在多个数据集上实现了高质量渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 在优化过程中由于显式建模引入的梯度偏差，导致非凸优化容易收敛到局部次优解（模糊陷阱），影响渲染质量。现有工作缺乏对这一根本性优化问题的理论分析和有效解决方案。

Method: 首先对 3DGS 优化公式进行严格数学分析，识别出导致模糊陷阱的优化偏差，并将其分为远侧模糊陷阱和近侧模糊陷阱两类。然后提出两种简单探索策略：Random Seeding（随机播种）用于缓解远侧模糊陷阱，Random Splitting（随机分裂）用于缓解近侧模糊陷阱。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验验证表明，引入这些探索算子能够有效且互补地克服模糊陷阱，实现高质量的渲染性能。

Conclusion: 简单的显式探索策略可以有效解决 3DGS 优化中的模糊陷阱问题，提升场景重建和渲染质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的优化问题，属于 3D 场景表示与重建方法，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS optimization, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, optimization bias, blur trap, random seeding, random splitting

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) employs Gaussian primitives for explicit scene representation, facilitating real-time, high-fidelity reconstruction and novel view synthesis of complex scenes. However, the explicit modeling inherent in 3DGS introduces a gradient bias during optimization, rendering its non-convex optimization process highly susceptible to convergence toward local suboptimal solutions. This constitutes a fundamental limitation in 3DGS optimization, which we term the Blur Trap. To address this limitation, we integrate simple explicit exploration into the 3DGS optimization framework. First, through rigorous mathematical analysis of the 3DGS optimization formulation, we identify the underlying optimization bias responsible for the Blur Trap and categorize it into two distinct subtypes: the Far-Side Blur Trap and the Near-Side Blur Trap. Subsequently, we propose two highly straightforward exploration strategies (Random Seeding and Random Splitting) to mitigate the far-side and near-side blur traps, respectively. Experimental validation demonstrates that the incorporation of these exploration operators effectively and complementarily overcome the Blur Trap, achieving high-quality rendering performance across multiple datasets. Project page:

*Shizeng Jiang, Hao Zhang, Xuerui Ma, Ying Hu, Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: QIRF 提出一种受量子启发的非正交函数空间压缩方法，通过构建高斯重叠矩阵和辐射响应密度矩阵，利用广义特征分解识别局部子空间并选择代表性高斯原语，在保持重建质量的同时将高斯数量和存储量平均压缩约 3.54 倍，渲染速度提升 34.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 需要大量高斯原语表示复杂场景，导致存储和渲染成本高昂。现有压缩方法主要关注原语级剪枝、属性量化等，但忽略了高斯基函数之间因强重叠和非正交性导致的函数空间冗余。

Method: 将相邻高斯原语建模为局部非正交基，构建解析高斯重叠矩阵和辐射响应密度矩阵（RRDM）来表征功能冗余和渲染相关性，通过广义特征分解识别主导局部子空间并选择代表性高斯原语，结合 RRDM 响应模型和细节感知保护机制在激进剪枝下保留高频结构。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks and Temples 和 Deep Blending 的 13 个场景上，平均减少 71.7% 的高斯数量和原始 PLY 存储（约 3.54 倍压缩），PSNR 平均提升 0.10 dB，渲染速度提升 34.3%。

Conclusion: 非正交函数空间冗余是显式高斯辐射场中一种重要但未被充分探索的表示冗余来源，QIRF 有效利用了这一点实现高效压缩。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting 的压缩方法，属于 3D 场景表示与渲染领域，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel compression method for 3DGS but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting compression, non-orthogonal function-space redundancy, quantum-inspired method, rendering speed improvement, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-quality real-time rendering by representing a scene with a large collection of anisotropic Gaussian primitives. However, complex scenes often require millions of Gaussians, resulting in substantial storage and rendering costs. Existing compression methods mainly reduce redundancy through primitive-wise pruning, attribute quantization, clustering, or neural coding, while redundancy caused by strongly overlapping and non-orthogonal Gaussian basis functions remains largely unexplored. We present QIRF, a quantum-inspired non-orthogonal function-space compression method for 3D Gaussian Splatting. QIRF models neighboring Gaussian primitives as a local non-orthogonal basis and formulates primitive reduction as a subspace-aware selection problem. Specifically, an analytic Gaussian overlap matrix and a radiance-response density matrix are constructed to characterize functional redundancy and rendering relevance. Generalized eigendecomposition is then used to identify the dominant local subspace and select representative Gaussian primitives. An RRDM-based response model and detail-aware safeguarding further preserve visually important high-frequency structures under aggressive pruning. Experiments on 13 scenes from Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks and Temples, and Deep Blending show that QIRF reduces the Gaussian count and raw PLY storage by 71.7 percent on average, corresponding to approximately 3.54 times compression, while maintaining reconstruction quality comparable to 3DGS and achieving a marginal average PSNR improvement of 0.10 dB. QIRF also improves the average rendering speed over 3DGS by 34.3 percent. These results suggest that non-orthogonal function-space redundancy is an important yet underexplored source of representational redundancy in explicit Gaussian radiance fields.

</details>

*Kaiyuan Tang, Daniel Burke, Chaoli Wang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 INR 的体数据表示方法本质上有损，即使微小的重建误差也会在渲染和下游分析中传播放大。因此需要一种无损的 INR 框架来保证体数据的精确重建。

Result: 在多个不同的体数据集上实现了零比特错误率和比特精确重建，以紧凑的表示实现了保真渲染和下游分析。

Conclusion: Lossless-INR 首次实现了对 3D 科学体数据的无损隐式神经表示，为需要高保真度的科学可视化与分析任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Lossless-INR，一种基于位平面分解的无损隐式神经表示框架，用于 3D 科学体数据。这属于 3D 场景/体数据的神经表示与重建方法，与 [CANONICAL] 3D 生成与重建 子主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on lossless volumetric INR for scientific data, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: Lossless reconstruction, bit-plane decomposition, octree block-partitioning, ternary feature-grid network, scientific volumetric data

Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) methods provide continuous coordinate-to-value mappings and integrate naturally with direct volume rendering, making them attractive for representing volumetric data. However, existing INR-based approaches for volumetric data are inherently lossy, and even small reconstruction errors can propagate through rendering and downstream analysis. In this work, we explore Lossless-INR, a lossless INR framework for 3D scientific volumetric data based on bit-plane decomposition. By decomposing each voxel value into binary bit-planes, we reformulate reconstruction as per-bit binary classification, so that exact recovery reduces to predicting every bit correctly. To make this optimization tractable while keeping the representation compact, we combine an octree block-partitioning strategy that adaptively subdivides complex regions with a ternary feature-grid network whose grid entries are parameterized by a ternary set of values. Experiments on diverse volumetric datasets show that this design can achieve zero bit-error rate and bit-exact reconstruction, enabling faithful rendering and downstream analysis with a compact representation. The code is available at .

</details>

*Lingwei Dang, Juntong Li, Zonghan Li, Hongwen Zhang, Liang An, Wei Min, Yebin Liu, Qingyao Wu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手-物交互合成是动画制作和具身AI的基础，但现有方法难以生成多视角一致的HOI视频，主要挑战在于复杂的手部运动和严重的遮挡问题。

Method: 提出HarmoHOI框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 混合多视角扩散Transformer（MoM-DiT），将RGB视频和3D点轨迹共同建模，通过将点轨迹表示为伪视频来对齐3D信号与2D潜空间；(2) 全局运动对齐扩散（GMA-Diffusion），将粗点轨迹优化为度量尺度、全局对齐的3D轨迹。采用混合数据课程学习策略，从单视图数据迁移先验到多视角生成。

Result: 在视觉质量、运动合理性和多视角几何一致性上达到最先进性能，有效生成了多视角一致的HOI视频。

Conclusion: HarmoHOI通过联合建模2D外观和3D运动，成功实现了多视角一致的手-物交互视频合成，验证了将3D几何信号与2D潜空间对齐的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多视角手-物交互视频合成，涉及多视角一致性的动态场景生成，属于多模态生成方向。虽然论文聚焦于手-物交互这一具体任务，但其核心是多视角动态场景/视频生成，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "4D 与动态场景生成"（dynamic_4d_generation）的主题高度吻合，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Strong diffusion-based 4D generation with multi-view consistency, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive video generation. Signals: diffusion framework, multi-view hand-object interaction, 3D point tracks, multi-view diffusion transformer, global motion alignment

Abstract: Hand-Object Interaction (HOI) synthesis is a cornerstone for animation production and embodied AI. Despite the strong priors of video foundation models, multi-view consistent HOI synthesis remains challenging due to complex hand motions and occlusions. We present HarmoHOI, a unified diffusion framework that jointly and harmoniously generates synchronized multi-view HOI videos and globally aligned 3D point tracks. Our core insight is that robust multi-view consistency fundamentally requires globally aligned 3D geometry and motion. To this end, we propose a Mixture of Multi-view Diffusion Transformer that co-models RGB videos and 3D point tracks. By representing point tracks as pseudo-videos, we align 3D geometric signals with the 2D latent space of foundation models, thereby minimizing the domain gap and easing adaptation of priors. To further ensure geometry consistency, we introduce Global Motion Aligning Diffusion, which refines coarse point tracks into metric-scale, globally aligned 3D trajectories. HarmoHOI enables on-the-fly co-evolution of 2D appearance and 3D motion during denoising. To overcome the scarcity of multi-view HOI data, we employ a hybrid data curriculum learning strategy that successfully transfers generic priors from single-view data to synchronized multi-view generation. Experimental results show that HarmoHOI achieves state-of-the-art performance in visual quality, motion plausibility, and multi-view geometric consistency. Project page available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动视频生成方法在人与物体交互场景中面临两个关键挑战：1）跨主体个性化（inter-subject）难以同时保证高主体保真度和准确的人-物交互模式，尤其是当物体为抽象概念（如logo）时；2）内主体参考（intra-subject）输入（如OCR图、多视角输入）本应提升保真度，但现有方法缺乏理解这种潜在对应关系的机制。

Method: HOMIE 提出统一框架处理 inter- 和 intra-subject 两种输入设置。核心创新包括：1）全局多模态引导（Global Multimodal Guidance），在自注意力中引入 MLLM 提取的语义特征以更好地对齐 VAE token；2）模态参考嵌入（Modality-Reference Embedding），区分 MLLM 特征和 VAE token 的 token 类型，并关联内主体参考图像 token，从而在不牺牲文本编码器可控性且无需昂贵重对齐的情况下，有效利用 MLLM 知识。

Result: 大量实验表明，HOMIE 在多种 HOCVP 任务上达到了 SOTA 性能，在主体保真度和交互准确性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: HOMIE 通过巧妙的 MLLM 集成策略，在不破坏文本可控性的前提下有效提升了人-物交互视频个性化生成的质量，为 subject-driven 视频生成中的人-物交互场景提供了统一有效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人-物交互视频个性化生成（subject-driven video generation），属于主体驱动/概念定制生成任务，与 canonical subtopic "主体与概念驱动生成"（customization_generation）高度匹配。同时涉及 MLLM 增强理解，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with multimodal generation and subject-driven video personalization, but lacks direct connection to top-priority continuous language/multimodal models. Signals: multimodal generation, subject-driven video generation, MLLM integration, self-attention guidance, human-object interaction

Abstract: Human-object centric video personalization (HOCVP) is a core task within subject-driven video generation. However, existing methods suffer from two key limitations. First, most approaches focusing on inter-subject personalization still struggle to strike a balance between high subject fidelity and accurate interaction patterns between humans and diverse objects, especially when objects represent abstract concepts such as logos. Second, while intra-subject references (e.g., OCR maps, multi-view inputs) are expected to enhance subject fidelity, most existing works lack mechanisms to understand such latent correspondence. To address both challenges, we propose HOMIE, an HOCVP framework that tackles both inter- and intra-subject input settings in a unified manner. Compared to previous approaches, HOMIE proposes a better MLLM integration strategy to extract knowledge of reference-level relationships without compromising the controllability of text encoders or incurring costly re-alignment. Specifically, we introduce global multimodal guidance within self-attention to better align MLLM-derived semantic features with VAE tokens. Furthermore, we propose modality-reference embedding to differentiate tokens from MLLM features and VAE tokens and associate intra-subject reference image tokens. Extensive experiments validate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across various HOCVP tasks. Project Page:

Abstract: Recent work leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate executable code for pedagogical animations using libraries such as Manim. However, ensuring spatial correctness and visual legibility remains challenging, as existing frameworks emphasize pedagogical content while overlooking geometric occlusions. We propose the Symbolic Geometric Agent (SGA), a plug-and-play module for code-centric animation pipelines that intercepts LLM-generated code, performs partial execution to extract symbolic scene graphs, and applies targeted refinement when spatial conflicts are detected. We further introduce the Manim Visual Quality Score (MVQS), a deterministic rendering-free proxy for spatial integrity. Experiments on the MMMC-Code benchmark across four LLM backbones and two agentic pipelines show that SGA achieves a peak MVQS of 73.11 (Code2Video + GPT-5.1), corresponding to a 16.1% relative improvement over the raw baseline, and improves MVQS in 7 of 8 backbone x pipeline configurations.

*Todd Zhou*

TL;DR: 本文提出CIB-Med-1基准，用于评估扩散模型在胸部X光片医学图像编辑中的目标外漂移（off-target drift），并引入一种约束扩散引导方法，在保持目标编辑效果的同时显著减少对非目标临床特征的语义干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型医学图像编辑评估仅关注目标分数是否提升，忽略了编辑过程中对共病特征、采集伪影等非目标临床特征的语义干扰（off-target drift），存在reward-hacking问题。

Method: 提出CIB-Med-1轨迹级基准，评估胸腔积液定向编辑中的目标进展、反转率和14个临床相关干扰轴上的语义漂移；并提出一种约束扩散引导基线，在优化目标进展的同时限制非目标变化。

Result: 约束编辑方法在保持目标进展（ρ_trend=0.88 vs 0.90）的同时，将中位数off-target漂移从0.46降至0.20，90百分位漂移从0.98降至0.33；盲法人类验证显示与预期排序的一致性更高（τ=0.61 vs Pix2Pix的0.29）。

Conclusion: 医学图像编辑应作为轨迹级语义控制来评估，而非端点分数最大化；语义不稳定性是有结构的而非偶然的，约束引导可有效缓解off-target漂移。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型在医学图像编辑中的应用，属于图像编辑范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑" (visual_editing) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical image editing, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keyword 'medical diagnosis'. Signals: Medical domain, Off-target drift evaluation, Constrained diffusion guidance, Chest radiography, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Diffusion models can now edit medical images in visually plausible ways, but the standard evaluation question is too narrow: did the target score increase? In clinical imaging, target findings are entangled with co-morbidities, acquisition effects, and selection bias, so a model can appear successful by changing correlated non-target findings rather than isolating the intended pathology. We introduce CIB-Med-1, a trajectory-level benchmark for controlled biomarker editing in chest radiography. CIB-Med-1 evaluates directional pleural effusion editing through calibrated target progression, inversion rate, and off-target semantic drift over 14 clinically motivated nuisance axes. The benchmark exposes a reward-hacking failure mode in which diffusion editors increase effusion scores while simultaneously altering parenchymal, cardiomediastinal, pleural, chronic, or artifact-related findings. We further present a constrained diffusion guidance baseline that optimizes target progression subject to bounded off-target change. Across held-out radiographs, the constrained editor preserves target progression ($\rho_{\mathrm{trend}}=0.88$ vs. $0.90$ for unconstrained guidance) while reducing median off-target drift from $0.46$ to $0.20$ and 90th-percentile drift from $0.98$ to $0.33$. Drift magnitude tracks empirical target--off-target association, supporting the view that semantic instability is structured rather than incidental. A blinded human validation probe with radiology trainees further shows stronger agreement with intended progression orderings ($\tau=0.61$ vs.\ $0.29$ for Pix2Pix). These results argue that medical image editing should be evaluated as trajectory-level semantic control, not as endpoint score maximization.

</details>

Method: 提出 Alignment-Aware Bridge Matching (A²BM)，在桥接匹配训练过程中引入对齐分数（alignment score），使模型学习区分真实语义对应与错位伪影。推理时将对齐分数作为控制变量，通过提示最高对齐分数来获得强对齐的高保真翻译结果。

Result: 在受控合成实验和真实任务（跨传感器超分辨率、像素级无监督域自适应）上，A²BM 在翻译保真度上一致优于 GAN、扩散模型和 Schrödinger 桥基线方法。

Abstract: Paired image-to-image translation underpins a wide range of computer vision tasks, including image editing, sensor translation, and domain adaptation. Bridge matching and flow matching have recently emerged as powerful frameworks, extending diffusion models to arbitrary source and target distributions. However, their standard formulations assume perfectly aligned training pairs, treating all source-target correspondences as equally reliable. In practice, real-world applications often involve weakly aligned pairs due to changes of acquisition conditions, including e.g. asynchronous captures, different illuminations, or misregistration. In this work, we introduce Alignment-Aware Bridge Matching (A${}^2$BM), a bridge matching method that leverages image pairs alignment during training. By incorporating alignment scores, the model learns to disentangle true semantic correspondences from misalignment artifacts. At inference time, we use the alignment score as a control variable over translation fidelity, with strongly aligned outputs obtained when prompting the model with the highest alignment score. We validate A${}^2$BM on both controlled synthetic experiments and on challenging real-world tasks, including cross-sensor super-resolution and pixel-space unsupervised domain adaptation. In all settings, A${}^2$BM consistently improves translation fidelity over strong GAN-, diffusion-, and Schr{ö}dinger bridge-based baselines, establishing alignment conditioning as a principled solution for image translation models with weakly aligned data.

</details>

Abstract: Recent advances in physics-grounded video generation leverage physics simulation as a physical prior to guide video synthesis toward physically plausible outcomes. The simulation process is controlled by physical specifications, which are typically generated by a vision-language model in a single pass. Such one-shot prediction often fails to accurately translate user intent into executable simulations, particularly for fine-grained object dynamics, complex motion trajectories, and temporally structured interactions. In this paper, we propose PhysAgent, a reflective agentic framework that closes the loop among physical program generation, physics simulation, stage-specific verification, and targeted program repair. Beyond improving the control of coupled physical parameters, our framework enables the agent to progressively realize complex trajectories, multi-stage interactions, and precise event outcomes by treating each physical program as an executable hypothesis. In addition, we design a set of physics-control APIs to support more stable and complex motion behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PhysAgent produces more physically plausible videos, achieves better prompt alignment, and generalizes more effectively across diverse physical scenarios.

</details>

Abstract: Style transfer remains a fundamental and highly important task across various data modalities, enabling creative manipulation conditioned by both reference images and textual descriptions. Recently, methods utilizing Gaussian Splatting have emerged as a unified representation for 2D images, video, 3D scenes, and 4D dynamics. However, representing videos and 2D images with Gaussian Splatting is structurally sub-optimal for dense continuous domains. The number of required Gaussians often approaches the total number of pixels, raising questions about the actual utility of such a representation for these specific modalities. In contrast, Implicit Neural Representations have established themselves as a much more popular and natural choice across all these data domains. Implicit Neural Representations naturally provide significant advantages, including data compression, inherent capabilities for super resolution, and seamless integration with deep generative models. To this end, we introduce OmniStyle-INR, a novel framework that leverages network-based continuous representations as a truly universal domain. Our approach successfully performs high-quality style transfer across all visual modalities, guided seamlessly by both text prompts and visual exemplars.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 大量实验表明，CCS 在抑制身份保持编辑方面持续优于现有保护方法，有效防止 UMM 在编辑过程中保留原始人脸身份信息。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了 UMM 图像编辑中双分支（ViT+VAE）的结构一致性是现有单分支对抗保护失效的关键原因，提出的 CCS 通过联合扰动和跨分支兼容性破坏，为统一多模态模型下的肖像保护提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究统一多模态模型（UMM）中的图像编辑安全问题，提出对抗性保护方法防止未经授权的人脸编辑。核心应用场景是图像编辑，因此映射到 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）"。同时涉及多模态理解与生成一体化模型，因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Signals: unified multimodal model, adversarial protection, cross-branch compatibility, identity-preserving editing, ViT and VAE perturbation

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) have recently demonstrated powerful instruction-based image editing capabilities, but they also raise serious concerns about unauthorized manipulation of personal portraits. Existing adversarial protection methods are mainly designed for either visual understanding or image generation models and often become ineffective when transferred to UMMs, which process an image through multiple complementary visual pathways. In this work, we first conduct a feature-level analysis of unified image editing. We observe that the ViT-based understanding branch and the VAE-based generation branch exhibit non-trivial structural agreement for the same input image. Although perturbing an individual branch can reduce this agreement and induce intermediate hidden-state deviations, such effects are asymmetric and gradually attenuated during multimodal fusion and generation. These observations reveal that single-branch feature distortion is insufficient for consistently disrupting unified image editing. Motivated by this finding, we propose CCS, a unified adversarial protection framework that jointly drives the ViT and VAE representations away from their clean counterparts while explicitly disrupting their cross-branch compatibility through linear CKA. By simultaneously removing stable information from both visual pathways and creating incompatible visual contexts, CCS prevents the UMM from recovering reliable identity information during editing. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CCS consistently outperforms existing protection methods in suppressing identity-preserving edits.

</details>

Signals: generative video compression, weak-network-aware transport, runtime state reuse, cross-clip memory reuse, ultra-low-bitrate video

Abstract: Under the AI Flow framework, communication is shifting from transmitting fidelity-oriented information flows toward delivering task-oriented and perception-oriented token flows across heterogeneous network resources. Video communication is a fundamental component of modern information networks. However, under ultra-low-bandwidth and weak-network conditions, conventional video coding and transmission methods, which are primarily optimized for pixel-level fidelity, often struggle to balance visual usability, transmission efficiency, and robustness to unstable links. With the rapid advancement of generativemodels, video communication is also moving from precise signal reconstruction toward receiver-side perceptual utility and system-level usability. In this paper, we propose Generative Transmission (GenTrans) for video communication under ultra-low-bandwidth and weak-network conditions. Built upon Generative Video Compression (GVC), GenTrans formulates video transmission as a joint optimization problem involving bandwidth, computation, and memory, rather than treating it merely as a signal coding task. By leveraging generative priors, cross-clip memory reuse, runtime state reuse, and weak-network-aware transport, GenTrans significantly reduces transmission overhead while enabling visually coherent and practically useful reconstruction. Experimental results show that GenTrans supports effective video transmission under ultra-low-bitrate and weak-network conditions, achieving improved transmission efficiency, decoding efficiency, and robustness while preserving perceptual quality.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出CamoDreamer，一种基于上下文解耦表征的伪装图像生成方法。通过对比感知上下文桥接、上下文解耦同化流和频率自适应上下文融合三个模块，显式分离物体与背景的条件控制，解决了现有方法中跨上下文表征泄露导致的视觉不一致问题，在轻量级设计下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出CamoDreamer框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 对比感知上下文桥接（Contrast-aware Contextual Bridge），建模跨上下文差异并构建对比感知的双重条件引导；(2) 上下文解耦同化流（Context-Decoupled Assimilation Streams），在潜空间中分离双重条件引导下的生成交互，并用目标感知线索辅助背景渲染；(3) 频率自适应上下文融合（Frequency-Adaptive Contextual Blend），融合解耦特征中的互补高频纹理和低频结构以提升整体一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CamoDreamer在多个指标上以显著优势超越现有方法，同时保持相对轻量化的模型设计。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦物体与背景的条件控制，可以有效解决伪装图像生成中的表征泄露问题，实现更自然、更一致的视觉融合效果。

Classification reason: 该论文研究伪装图像生成（CIG），属于图像生成与编辑范畴。伪装图像生成本质上是将目标物体无缝融入背景的图像编辑/合成任务，与"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）这一[CANONICAL]子主题高度相关，因此复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Camouflage image generation is a niche subtopic not aligned with user's priority subtopics, and lacks strong overlap with boost keywords. Signals: camouflage image generation, context-decoupled representations, contrast-aware bridge, frequency-adaptive blend, lightweight design

Abstract: Camouflage image generation (CIG) focuses on generating visually concealed objects that seamlessly blend into their backgrounds. Existing methods typically follow either background-guided paradigms that adapt object appearance via style transfer, or foreground-guided strategies that outpaint surrounding regions conditioned on object features. However, they still suffer from appearance discrepancy and background artifacts. We attribute these limitations to cross-context representation leakage, where object and background cues are entangled in a coupled conditional space, resulting in ambiguous control and degraded camouflage fidelity. To tackle this, we propose a new context-decoupled generative paradigm, termed CamoDreamer, which aims to isolate contextual conditional guidance and explicitly decouple latent camouflage features into coordinated object and background control streams. First, a Contrast-aware Contextual Bridge is designed to model cross-context discrepancies and construct contrast-aware dual conditional guidance. Second, Context-Decoupled Assimilation Streams are employed to separate generative interactions conditioned on the dual guidance, while facilitating background rendering with target-aware cues in the latent space. Finally, a Frequency-Adaptive Contextual Blend module integrates complementary high-frequency textures and low-frequency structures from decoupled features to improve holistic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CamoDreamer consistently outperforms existing methods with a substantial margin, while maintaining a relatively lightweight design.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的图像重光照方法缺乏显式的特征变换学习机制，在复杂光照条件下容易出现光照变化不稳定或内容保持不一致的问题。

Method: 将重光照形式化为光照特征传输问题，提出 CFT 训练原则。基于 rectified flow，CFT 联合建模两个传输过程：(1) 噪声到图像的生成过程；(2) 光照一致的源图像到目标图像的传输过程。通过轨迹级监督（trajectory-level supervision）实现双传输联合建模，显式分离光照相关变化与内容对齐特征。同时构建大规模人像重光照数据集以支持复杂光照场景。

Result: 在多个重光照基准上一致超越现有 SOTA 方法，并展示出可泛化到风格迁移等其他编辑任务的能力。

Conclusion: CFT 通过显式的光照特征传输机制有效解决了扩散方法在重光照中内容保持与光照变化之间的权衡问题，且具有良好的任务泛化性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图像重光照（image relighting），属于图像编辑任务。现有 canonical subtopic "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 明确涵盖图像编辑、可控编辑等方向，重光照是其中一种典型的图像编辑任务，因此直接复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Image relighting is a niche editing task, not aligned with user's priority on autoregressive generation, world models, or continuous multimodal models. Signals: rectified flow for feature transport, trajectory-level supervision, large-scale portrait dataset, SOTA relighting, generalizes to style transfer

Abstract: Image relighting modifies illumination while preserving non-lighting content such as identity and geometry. Existing diffusion-based methods often suffer from unstable illumination changes or inconsistent content preservation under complex lighting, as they lack an explicit mechanism to learn feature transformations between images. We reformulate relighting as an illumination feature transport problem and introduce Consistent Feature Transport (CFT), a training principle that explicitly enforces illumination-consistent transport between source and target image distributions. Built upon rectified flow, CFT jointly models noise-to-image generation and illumination-consistent source-to-target transport through trajectory-level supervision. This dual-transport formulation encourages isolation of illumination-specific variations while preserving content-aligned features. To support complex lighting scenarios, we construct a large-scale portrait relighting dataset with diverse relighting effects. Experiments show consistent improvements over existing state-of-the-art relighting approaches and demonstrate that CFT can generalize to other editing tasks, including style transfer. Code is available at .

</details>


### [48] [FlowMimic: Mask-free Visual Editing and Generation with Pixel-pair Warped Flow Field for Online Video Editing Data Generation and Modality Mimicry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18227)
*Dingyun Zhang, Lixue Gong, Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (69.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FlowMimic 提出了一种无需掩码标注的视频编辑数据生成方法，通过像素对时序扭曲流场（pixel-pair temporal warped flow field）直接从图像编辑样本实时生成对应的视频编辑样本，并设计了模态模仿生成损失和编辑损失来对齐图像和视频模态的输出分布。此外，论文引入指代表达分割等感知相关任务，以及编辑区域感知的潜空间损失和注意力损失，使模型内化语言引导的视觉编辑能力，无需外部辅助模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频编辑数据的收集依赖人工掩码标注、I2V模型合成等耗时且易出错的过程，导致视频编辑任务的多样性远不如图像编辑。同时，现有语言引导的视觉编辑方法通常依赖外部辅助（如额外微调MLLM或显式提供掩码序列），缺乏模型内化的编辑能力。作者希望解决这两个问题：1）高效生成多样化的视频编辑训练数据；2）让模型自主内化语言引导的视觉编辑能力。

Method: 1) 提出像素对时序扭曲流场（pixel-pair temporal warped flow field），利用图像编辑样本（原图+编辑后图）通过光流扭曲生成对应的视频编辑样本对，无需掩码标注。2) 设计模态模仿生成损失（modality mimic generation loss）和模态模仿编辑损失（modality mimic editing loss），通过相互模仿来对齐图像和视频模态的输出分布。3) 引入指代表达分割等感知任务，以及编辑区域感知的潜空间损失（editing-region-aware latent-level loss）和注意力损失（attention-level loss），使模型内化语言指令驱动的编辑定位与修改能力。

Result: 论文在多个层次的视频编辑任务上验证了方法的有效性，表明仅使用该方法生成的数据即可训练模型学习视频编辑。具体定量和定性结果需参考论文原文。

Conclusion: FlowMimic 通过创新的数据生成策略和模态对齐损失，有效降低了视频编辑数据收集成本，并提升了模型内化语言引导编辑的能力，为统一图像和视频编辑与生成提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需掩码的视频编辑数据生成方法（FlowMimic），并探索在单一模型中统一图像和视频的生成与编辑能力。核心贡献是视频编辑数据生成和编辑能力对齐，属于图像与视频编辑范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）。同时论文也涉及统一多模态模型（理解与生成一体化），因此也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Proposes a mask-free video editing data generation method, but focuses on video editing rather than the user's priority subtopics like autoregressive generation or continuous models. Signals: video editing data generation, pixel-pair warped flow field, modality mimicry, no mask annotation, online data generation

Abstract: In line with the prevailing direction of vision research, we explore the integration of both generation and editing capabilities for video and image modalities within a single model. Current approaches to collecting video editing data typically depend on labour-intensive, time-consuming curated procedures--involving object mask annotation, the use of error-introducing pair synthesis via I2V model and ControlNet-like guidance, and VLM-based quality filtering or refinement--and demonstrate limited task scalability. As a result, the diversity of editing tasks remains substantially narrower than that available for image editing models. We develop a pixel-pair temporal warped flow field that can directly generate corresponding video editing samples in real time from image editing samples, and we demonstrate across multiple levels of video editing tasks that a model can learn video editing using only such data. We regard the image modality as a particular form of the video modality. Accordingly, we design a modality mimic generation loss and a modality mimic editing loss to relatively align the capabilities--and thereby the output distributions--of the two modalities through mutual imitation. Moreover, language-based visual editing entails the comprehension of the editing instruction and the reference visual content, the localization of the region corresponding to that instruction within the reference visual contents, and the modification of that region alone. Existing approaches predominantly rely on external aids, such as fine-tuning an additional MLLM or explicitly supplying a mask sequence as auxiliary input during inference. In contrast, we aspire for the model to internalize this capability. To that end, we introduce sense-related tasks--for instance, referring expression segmentation--along with corresponding editing-region-aware latent-level loss and attention-level loss.

</details>

### [49] [From Sign Language Generation to Humanoid Execution: Vision-Language Guided Retargeting with Collision Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17769)
*Nabeela Khan, Bowen Wu, Runwu Shi, Benjamin Yen, Takeshi Ashizawa, Carlos Toshinori Ishi, Takashi Minato, Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO, cs.HC

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (75.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统级框架，将手语生成（SLG）输出桥接到人形机器人关节空间执行。包含两个组件：1）基于SMPL-X的体积碰撞缓解模块，将生成的手语动作投影到物理可行的姿态；2）基于VLM视觉批评器的重定向算法，识别执行失败模式并触发任务空间修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语生成系统输出的密集3D人体表示在渲染中可容忍自交等伪影，但在人形机器人执行中会导致不可行的逆运动学解、碰撞和不稳定的重定向轨迹。需要解决从SLG到人形机器人执行的物理可行性问题。

Method: 1）体积SMPL-X碰撞缓解模块：将生成的手语运动投影到物理可行的姿态，同时最小化偏离原始轨迹；2）视觉语言引导的重定向算法：基于IK骨干，VLM作为视觉批评器，识别特定于具身系统的失败模式并触发任务空间修正。

Result: 实验表明碰撞处理和感知引导的细化是人形机器人手语执行中缺失的关键组件，所提方法有效减少了自交和碰撞，生成了更稳定可靠的重定向轨迹。

Conclusion: 碰撞缓解和VLM感知引导的细化是连接手语生成与人形机器人执行的关键缺失组件，本文框架为可靠的人形机器人手语执行提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 论文关注手语生成（SLG）输出到人形机器人执行的动作重定向，涉及人体动作生成与碰撞缓解，属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）范畴。同时使用了VLM作为视觉批评器，也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on humanoid robot execution of sign language, which is niche and not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: Sign language generation, Humanoid robot retargeting, Collision mitigation, VLM visual critic, SMPL-X

Abstract: Recent sign language generation (SLG) systems increasingly output dense 3D body representations, which better preserve full-body kinematics and geometry for downstream embodiment on humanoid robots. However, these generated motions frequently exhibit self-intersections such as hand-hand and hand-torso penetration. While such artifacts may be tolerated in offline rendering, they become critical in humanoid execution as they lead to infeasible inverse-kinematics (IK) solutions, collisions, and unstable retargeted trajectories. We present a system-level framework that bridges SLG outputs to humanoid joint-space execution via two components. First, we introduce a volumetric SMPL-X collision-mitigation module that projects generated signing motions toward physically plausible configurations while minimally deviating from the original trajectory. Second, we propose a vision-language-guided retargeting algorithm built on an IK backbone: a VLM serves as a visual critic over rendered humanoid motion, identifies embodiment-specific failure modes, and triggers targeted task-space corrections. Our results highlight collision handling and perception-guided refinement as key missing components for reliable humanoid signing.

</details>

### [50] [CoBind: Stage-Aware Compositional Binding for Training-Free Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16307)
*Kaijie Chen, Ethan Caldwell, Mira Vossen, Julian Hartwell, Serena Whitlock, Adrian Bellamy*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CoBind 是一种无需训练的阶段性组合绑定框架，通过解析提示为组合图、分阶段施加实体-属性-关系约束，并在去噪过程中逐步放松结构引导，显著提升了扩散文生图模型在复杂组合提示下的属性绑定和空间关系生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散文生图模型在处理包含多个实体、属性和关系的复杂提示时，常出现物体遗漏、属性分配错误或空间布局颠倒等问题。已有的无训练方法主要强化 token 级注意力，但未显式建模属性与实体的归属关系，也未考虑去噪过程中不同阶段应施加不同约束。

Method: CoBind 首先将提示解析为包含实体、属性和关系的组合图；在去噪早期阶段，利用实体完整性和关系约束建立全局布局；然后通过跨实体对比优化将属性绑定到目标实体；结构引导在后期去噪步骤中逐步放松以保留纹理和视觉细节；同时根据每个约束的当前满足程度自适应调整引导强度。

Result: 在 T2I-CompBench++、GenEval 等多个基准和多种扩散骨干网络上，CoBind 在属性绑定、空间关系和复杂组合生成方面取得了一致的改进，同时保持了有竞争力的视觉质量。

Conclusion: CoBind 通过阶段感知的组合绑定策略，有效解决了复杂提示下的属性-实体绑定和空间关系问题，无需重新训练或额外标注，是一种实用且高效的训练-free 方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CoBind，一种无需训练的文本到图像扩散模型组合绑定方法，属于文生图扩散模型范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散" (text_to_image_diffusion)。

Importance reason: Training-free compositional binding for text-to-image diffusion is solid but not aligned with your priority on continuous/autoregressive models or world models. Signals: training-free, compositional binding, attribute binding, spatial relations, diffusion backbone

Abstract: Diffusion-based text-to-image models often fail on complex prompts involving multiple entities, attributes, and relations, producing object omissions, incorrect attribute assignments, or reversed spatial layouts. Existing training-free methods mainly strengthen token-level attention, but do not explicitly model which attributes belong to which entities or when different constraints should be enforced during denoising. We introduce \textbf{CoBind}, a training-free framework for stage-aware compositional binding. CoBind parses a prompt into a composition graph of entities, attributes, and relations. It first establishes the global layout using entity-completeness and relation constraints, then binds attributes to their target entities through contrastive cross-entity optimization. Structural guidance is gradually relaxed in later denoising steps to preserve textures and visual details. CoBind also adapts the guidance strength according to the current satisfaction of each constraint, reducing unnecessary latent updates. CoBind requires no retraining or additional annotations. Experiments on T2I-CompBench++, GenEval, and multiple diffusion backbones show consistent improvements in attribute binding, spatial relations, and complex compositional generation while maintaining competitive visual quality.

</details>


### [51] [Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17585)
*Renye Yan, Jikang Cheng, You Wu, Ling Liang, Wei Peng, Athanasios V. Vasilakos, Qingyu Zhao, Yu Zhang, Ehsan Adeli, Kilian M. Pohl, Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (88.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了像素空间扩散Transformer（pDiT），即在原始像素空间而非VAE潜空间中进行扩散建模的方法。论文从模型架构、连续生成机制和统一多模态建模三个角度总结了代表性方法，分析了噪声调度、损失加权、token效率等关键技术挑战，并讨论了将生成与理解统一的高保真端到端视觉基础模型的未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在扩散模型（LDM）依赖固定的VAE视觉tokenizer，会丢弃细粒度纹理和结构细节，且表征学习与扩散训练分离导致重建与生成目标不匹配。像素空间扩散直接在原始像素上建模，消除VAE瓶颈，支持端到端优化，更符合高保真生成需求，同时为统一多模态系统提供基础。

Method: 本文是一篇综述论文，系统梳理了像素空间扩散Transformer（pDiT）的相关工作。从三个视角组织：（1）模型架构——包括DiT架构在像素空间的适配、噪声调度与损失加权设计、token效率优化；（2）连续生成机制——像素空间中的连续建模、flow matching等连续生成方法；（3）统一多模态建模——将原始像素、文本和任务条件表示为共享token空间，由单一Transformer联合处理。

Result: 论文总结了代表性方法（如PixelDiT等），识别了像素空间扩散面临的关键技术挑战：高维建模带来的计算复杂度、噪声调度与损失权重的设计、token效率与可扩展架构设计等，并指出了未来方向。

Conclusion: 像素空间扩散Transformer为高保真端到端视觉生成提供了有前景的范式，消除了VAE瓶颈，支持生成与理解的统一。未来需要解决计算效率、架构可扩展性等挑战，以构建真正的视觉基础模型。

Classification reason: 该论文综述像素空间扩散Transformer（pDiT），核心是图像扩散模型架构（DiT）在像素空间中的扩展，属于文生图与图像扩散这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时论文也涉及连续视觉token/潜空间（连续语言模型方向）和统一多模态理解与生成（多模态理解与生成方向），因此匹配三个方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses continuous multimodal modeling and unified generation-understanding in pixel space, aligning with top-priority subtopics. Signals: continuous language model, unified multimodal, pixel-space diffusion, high-fidelity generation, end-to-end vision foundation model

Abstract: Latent diffusion models (LDMs) enable efficient high-resolution image synthesis by denoising in a VAE-compressed latent space. However, fixed visual tokenizers can discard fine textures and structural details, while separate representation and diffusion training creates a mismatch between reconstruction and generation objectives. These limitations have renewed interest in pixel-space diffusion, which models raw pixels directly, removes the VAE bottleneck, and supports end-to-end optimization. This formulation better matches the demands of high-fidelity generation but introduces challenges in high-dimensional modeling, including noise scheduling, loss weighting, token efficiency, and scalable architecture design. Pixel-space modeling also offers a promising basis for unified multimodal systems: raw pixels, text, and task conditions can be represented in a shared token space and jointly processed by a single Transformer, narrowing the gap between visual understanding and generation. This paper reviews Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers (pDiTs) from the perspectives of model architecture, continuous generative mechanisms, and unified multimodal modeling. We summarize representative methods, identify key technical challenges, and discuss future directions toward high-fidelity, end-to-end vision foundation models that integrate generation and understanding.

</details>


### [52] [MixDiffusion: Mixing Diffusion-based Uni-condition Text-to-Image Generation Models for Multi-condition Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17634)
*Pengcheng Wan, Liang Han, Lin Xu, Bowen Xiao, Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MixDiffusion 提出一种无需训练的扩散框架，通过理论推导的噪声分布集成公式，将多个预训练的单条件文生图扩散模型组合起来，实现任意数量控制条件（边界框、关键点、草图、深度图、参考图、文本等）的多条件图像生成，易于部署且可扩展至新模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散文生图模型大多仅支持单一类型的控制条件（如仅边界框或仅关键点），缺乏灵活的多条件联合控制能力。作者希望在不重新训练模型的前提下，利用已有的单条件扩散模型实现多条件可控图像生成。

Method: MixDiffusion 的核心思想是在每个去噪步骤中，从多个预训练的单条件扩散模型（每个模型对应一种控制条件）的预测噪声分布出发，通过理论推导的集成公式计算出多条件联合的预测噪声分布，从而指导去噪过程。该方法无需训练，仅需在推理时组合多个单条件模型的输出，理论上支持任意数量的控制条件。

Result: 实验表明，MixDiffusion 能够有效整合多种控制条件（如边界框+关键点、草图+深度图等），生成符合所有条件约束的高质量图像。与需要重新训练的多条件方法相比，MixDiffusion 在保持生成质量的同时具有更好的灵活性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: MixDiffusion 提供了一种简单有效的多条件文生图方案，利用现有单条件扩散模型即可实现灵活的多条件控制，无需额外训练。其理论推导的噪声集成公式保证了方法的有效性，且易于扩展到新的控制模态。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MixDiffusion，一种无需训练的多条件文本到图像生成框架，通过集成多个预训练的单条件扩散模型实现多条件控制（边界框、关键点、草图、深度图、参考图、文本等）。核心方法基于扩散模型的噪声分布推导，属于文生图与图像扩散领域的多条件可控生成工作，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散" (text_to_image_diffusion)。

Importance reason: Training-free multi-condition diffusion is useful but not aligned with your priority on continuous language/video models and world models. Signals: training-free multi-condition diffusion, text-to-image generation, controllable generation, noise distribution integration, extensible to new modalities

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) generation have enabled controllable image synthesis by incorporating conditions beyond text. However, most existing diffusion-based methods are limited to a single type of control condition (e.g., bounding boxes or keypoints), which restricts their flexibility. To address this limitation, we propose MixDiffusion, a training-free diffusion framework for multi-condition T2I generation. MixDiffusion theoretically supports an arbitrary number of control conditions, including bounding boxes, keypoints, sketches, depth maps, reference images, and text, by collaboratively integrating multiple pre-trained uni-condition diffusion models. The key insight of the proposed approach is to derive the predicted noise distribution in each denoising step of the diffusion-based multi-condition image generation model from the predicted noise distributions of multiple diffusion-based uni-condition models with a derived integration formula, which is supported by rigorous theory proof. Owing to its training-free nature, MixDiffusion is easy to deploy and readily extensible to new control modalities.

</details>

*Zhenjie Liu, Binyan Chen, Hao Chen, Tong Pan, Shangfei Wang*

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的三阶段流水线框架，用于序列动作视频生成中的身份保持。通过关键帧锚定、身份保持生成和推理增强三个阶段，在IPVG26挑战赛Track 2中获得第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份保持视频生成方法仅处理单一整体提示，无法应对时序结构化规范（即按顺序执行多个动作）。端到端视频生成器在动作累积和变化时容易出现外观漂移，需要一种无需训练的方法来保持身份一致性。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线框架：(1) 动作感知提示润色阶段，将输入重写为指定每个动作终止状态的图像生成提示；(2) 身份保持生成阶段，通过联合条件化参考身份和前驱帧生成关键帧序列，解耦时不变外观与时变姿态；(3) 身份感知推理增强阶段，使用多参考引导和身份驱动噪声搜索合成中间帧，增强采样过程中的身份保真度。

Result: 在IPVG26挑战赛Track 2官方排行榜上获得第三名，展示了竞争性能和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的无需训练的三阶段框架能有效解决序列动作视频生成中的身份保持问题，通过关键帧锚定和推理增强策略缓解了外观漂移。

Classification reason: 该论文研究身份保持的文本到视频生成，属于视频生成任务，与 canonical 子主题"文生视频与视频扩散"（text_to_video_diffusion）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses identity preservation in sequential-action video generation, but lacks direct connection to top-priority continuous language/multimodal models or world models. Signals: video generation, identity preservation, sequential actions, training-free pipeline, keyframe anchoring

Abstract: Identity-preserving text-to-video generation aims to synthesize a video that accurately follows a textual description while maintaining the recognizability of a user-specified subject throughout. The IPVG26 challenge extends this framework from a single holistic prompt to a temporally structured specification. The model additionally receives a sequence of timestamped action captions and must render the subject performing these actions in the specified order. This temporal structure presents a challenge not encountered in previous identity-preserving generation tasks, as the subject must continuously perform a scripted sequence of distinct actions while maintaining a consistent identity. However, end-to-end video generators are prone to appearance drift as motion accumulates and the depicted actions change. We address this challenge with a training-free, three-stage pipeline framework. An action-aware prompt polishment stage first rewrites the inputs into image-generation prompts that specify the terminal state of each action. An identity-preserving generation stage then produces the keyframe sequence by conditioning each frame jointly on the reference identity and its predecessor, thereby decoupling time-invariant appearance from time-varying pose. Finally, an identity-aware inference enhancement stage synthesizes the intermediate segments using multi-reference guidance and identity-driven noise searching, both of which reinforce identity fidelity during sampling. Our method ranked third on the official Track 2 leaderboard, demonstrating competitive performance and strong generality.

</details>


### [54] [The generator is the tracker: Multi-object tracking by painting persistent identity colours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17120)
*Haiyu Yang, Miel Hostens*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (82.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出将视频生成器用作多目标跟踪器：微调一个22B的文生视频扩散模型（LTX-2.3），通过轻量级in-context LoRA将RGB视频片段转换为ID颜色映射视频，其中每个人物被赋予随时间保持一致的独特颜色。通过链式窗口生成长视频，无需检测器、运动模型或重识别模块。在DanceTrack上达到40.3 HOTA，关联分数（AssA 44.1）超过原始基准套件中的所有跟踪器，但检测精度是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标跟踪将身份作为外部状态（轨迹缓冲区、运动模型、外观嵌入）维护，本文探索是否可以让视频生成器直接在像素层面维护身份状态，从而消除对传统跟踪栈的依赖。

Method: 微调22B参数量的文生视频扩散模型LTX-2.3，使用轻量级in-context LoRA，将RGB视频片段转换为ID颜色映射视频（每个行人被赋予独特的平坦颜色，同一颜色代表同一身份）。长视频通过链式窗口生成，每个窗口以前一个窗口的清理尾部为条件。通过简短的延续微调教会模型扩展已有的颜色分配，使身份在链中流动，无需跟踪器、运动模型或重识别模块。

Result: 在DanceTrack测试集上达到40.3 HOTA。关联分数（AssA 44.1）超过原始基准套件中的所有跟踪器，但检测分数是主要短板。控制实验表明生成机制本身起作用：相同生成窗口经经典后处理关联仅得18.2 HOTA；在383个遮挡事件中，生成器在42%的条件下能重新获取身份，而外观嵌入基线得分为零。

Conclusion: 视频生成器可以作为一种新颖的多目标跟踪范式，通过像素级颜色维护身份，展现出独特的错误分布（关联强、检测弱）和遮挡后重识别能力。虽然当前性能远低于专业跟踪器，但为生成式跟踪开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文微调文生视频扩散模型（LTX-2.3）生成ID颜色映射视频用于多目标跟踪，核心方法属于视频扩散模型的应用，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生视频与视频扩散"。

Importance reason: Novel application of video generation to tracking, but tangential to user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: video diffusion for tracking, in-context LoRA, emergent re-identification, 22B model, DanceTrack benchmark

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) is conventionally decomposed into detection followed by association, with object identity maintained as external state: track buffers, motion models, and appearance embeddings. We ask whether a video generator can maintain that state in pixels. We fine-tune a 22B text-to-video diffusion model (LTX-2.3) with a lightweight in-context LoRA to translate an RGB clip into an ID-map clip, a video in which every person is painted a flat, distinct color that persists over time: same color, same identity. Long videos are generated as chained windows, where each window is conditioned on the cleaned tail of the previous one. A brief continuation fine-tune teaches the model to extend a given coloring, after which identity flows through the chain with no tracker, no motion model, and no re-identification module. On the DanceTrack test server, our system, to our knowledge the first generative tracker evaluated there and the only entry with no detector and no tracking stack, reaches 40.3 HOTA. This is well below today's specialist state of the art (>=70 HOTA), but with a unique, inverted error profile: its association score (AssA 44.1) exceeds every tracker of the original benchmark suite while detection remains the sole deficit. Controlled comparisons show the mechanism matters: the same generated windows linked by classical post-hoc association score 2x worse (18.2 HOTA), and frame-to-frame IoU association fragments tracks that the generator's colors keep whole. On 383 mined occlusion events, the generator re-acquires identities after gaps at a 42% conditional rate where appearance-embedding baselines score zero, including gaps longer than its temporal context, evidence that the generator's color assignment functions as an emergent re-identification signal. We release code, checkpoints, and the full pre-registered experimental log.

</details>

*Su Guo, Guangce Liu, Haosen Yang, Jiepeng Wang, Cong Liu, Junqi Liu, Haibin Huang, Hongxun Yao, Chi Zhang, Xuelong Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在单镜头生成上表现优异，但缺乏显式的镜头规划能力，难以生成具有连贯叙事和有效多镜头组合的电影级视频。

Method: 在视频扩散基础模型基础上引入可学习规划令牌（planning tokens），捕获镜头级转换线索并与原始视频生成令牌无缝集成以控制转换时间戳；提出分数阶时间旋转位置编码（FRoPE），使规划令牌能够在帧级别建模镜头转换。

Result: 实验表明 ShotPlan 显著优于现有电影级视频生成方法，在镜头管理的灵活性和镜头间一致性方面表现更强。

Conclusion: 可学习规划令牌结合 FRoPE 能有效赋予视频扩散模型显式的多镜头规划能力，为电影级视频生成提供了一种灵活且一致的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于视频扩散基础模型的多镜头电影级视频生成框架，属于文本/条件到视频生成的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel planning tokens and FRoPE for explicit multi-shot control in video diffusion directly advance controllable video generation and world model capabilities. Signals: video generation, diffusion transformer, controllable generation, planning tokens, FRoPE

Abstract: Current video generation models achieve impressive results in single-shot generation, yet remain limited in cinematic video generation, where coherent narratives and effective multi-shot composition require explicit shot planning. To address this challenge, we propose ShotPlan, a framework for explicit multi-shot cinematic video generation built upon a video diffusion foundation model. Our method introduces learnable planning tokens that capture shot-level transition cues and can be seamlessly integrated with the original video generation tokens to control transition timestamps. Unlike standard video generation tokens, the proposed planning tokens are equipped with Fractional Temporal Rotary Position Embedding (FRoPE), enabling shot transitions to be modeled at the frame level. Experiments demonstrate that ShotPlan significantly outperforms existing cinematic video generation methods, offering more flexible shot management and stronger inter-shot consistency.

</details>

### [56] [Spatial Transport of Integration Error in Generative ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16361)
*Songheng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 高优先级 (92.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式ODE（扩散/流模型）在少步采样时积分误差的空间来源与传输机制。通过扰动实验和线性化传播分析，发现误差在采样过程中被广泛传播：起始阶段不到10%的端点响应保留在源区域。模型自身的速度/预测场沿轨迹的变化可以预测最终的区域误差（rho 0.57-0.70），且对注入变化施加训练惩罚可以降低少步误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流模型在实际应用中通常只使用少量求解步数，这会产生积分误差。现有工作主要关注如何减少误差（如更好的求解器、蒸馏等），但对误差在图像空间中的分布和传播机制缺乏理解。本文旨在回答：误差在哪里被注入？它如何传播到最终结果？

Method: 提出了一种有符号的源-传输（source-and-transport）核算方法，对少步积分误差进行一阶分析。具体包括：(1) 扰动实验，在采样轨迹的不同位置注入局部扰动，追踪其传播；(2) 通过模型自身的线性化动力学传播有符号的单步截断残差，重建端点误差的方向和区域结构；(3) 分析模型速度/预测场沿轨迹的变化（即注入变化）与最终区域误差之间的相关性。

Result: 在5个256²分辨率模型上的实验表明：(1) 采样早期，不到10%的端点响应保留在源区域；(2) 线性化传播重建的端点误差与真实误差的余弦相似度为0.81-0.87；(3) 一个区域的误差更多来自其他区域传输来的误差，而非自身注入；(4) 模型速度/预测场沿轨迹的变化预测最终区域误差的rho为0.57-0.70；(5) 对注入变化施加训练惩罚可以降低少步误差。

Conclusion: 生成式ODE的少步积分误差在空间上被广泛传播，其分布由模型学习到的动力学结构决定。这种结构是可预测的（从模型自身读出），也是可改变的（通过训练惩罚）。该发现为改进少步采样方法提供了新的理论视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究生成式ODE（扩散/流模型）在少步采样时的积分误差空间传输机制，属于生成模型加速与高效化（少步采样）方向下的基础分析工作，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency and acceleration for generative ODEs, a high-priority subtopic, with novel error transport analysis. Signals: generative ODE error analysis, diffusion model acceleration, flow model efficiency, spatial error transport, training penalty for few-step error

Abstract: A trained flow or diffusion model is usually run with only a handful of solver steps, and the integration error this leaves behind is unevenly distributed across the image. We ask where that error is injected and how it reaches the endpoint, and answer with a signed source-and-transport accounting of few-step integration error, tested to first order. A perturbation experiment on five models at 256^2 resolution shows the learned dynamics spread local disturbances widely: near the start of sampling, under 10% of the summed endpoint response remains at the source. Signed one-step truncation residuals, propagated through the model's own linearized dynamics, reconstruct much of the endpoint error's direction and regional structure (cosine 0.81-0.87), and a region's error owes more to what arrives from elsewhere than to its own injection. Structure-destroying nulls, with protocols frozen before evaluation, locate what carries the account: randomizing contribution signs halves it, and reassigning which region receives each contribution, with content, norms, and signs intact, destroys it entirely. Where the injections land is readable from the model itself. The variation of its velocity or prediction field along the trajectory, a structure that emerges during training, predicts the final per-region gap (within-image rho of 0.57-0.70 on fine trajectories, weaker from the cheap solve alone). The prediction is partial because endpoint error depends not only on injected magnitude but on its sign, timing, and transport through the learned dynamics. A training penalty on the injected variation lowers few-step error, so the structure is one a model can be trained to change.

</details>


### [57] [SPARE-GS: Structural Parsimony and Resource Efficiency for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16624)
*Zhang Chen, Shuai Wan, Fuzheng Yang, Jiazhi Xia, Weiyao Lin, Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (66.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SPARE-GS 提出一种全局预算约束优化框架，通过动态平衡3D高斯原语的空间分布与区域表示需求，在减少高斯数量和训练时间的同时提升渲染质量，是一种即插即用的3DGS效率优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在实现高质量实时新视角合成时，存在原语过度增殖的问题，导致训练效率低且表示不够紧凑。现有方法缺乏对原语分布的全局结构性调控。

Method: 将3DGS的结构演化形式化为全局预算约束优化问题，推导出最优性条件（各区域边际效用平衡）。提出SPARE-GS框架：估计容量归一化的区域表示需求，分配自适应目标配额，利用区域预算偏差协调致密化、剪枝和自适应终止，实现更均衡的结构分配。

Result: 在标准、加速和结构增强的3DGS流水线上，平均减少30.38%的高斯数量和23.81%的训练时间，同时平均PSNR有所提升。紧凑表示还改善了后续压缩和剪枝方法的率失真性能。

Conclusion: 全局结构预算调控是一种有效的3DGS效率优化范式，SPARE-GS作为即插即用框架具有广泛的适用性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的表示紧凑性和训练效率优化，通过全局预算约束优化来减少高斯原语数量并加速训练，属于生成模型（新视角合成）的效率优化范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS efficiency, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, budget-constrained optimization, primitive reduction, training acceleration, plug-and-play framework

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-fidelity novel view synthesis in real-time; however its training efficiency and representation compactness are hindered by excessive primitive proliferation. To address this challenge, we formulate the structural evolution of 3DGS as a global budget-constrained optimization problem and derive an optimality condition, which requires the marginal utility of structural resources to be balanced across spatial regions under a finite primitive budget. Based on this formulation, we propose SPARE-GS, a general plug-and-play framework that dynamically aligns the distribution of 3D Gaussian primitives with regional representational demand. SPARE-GS estimates capacity-normalized regional demand, assigns adaptive target quotas, and uses regional budget deviations to coordinate densification, pruning and adaptive termination toward a more balanced structural allocation. Extensive experiments across standard, accelerated, and structure-enhanced 3DGS pipelines demonstrate that SPARE-GS reduces the Gaussian count and training time by an average of 30.38% and 23.81%, respectively, while improving the average PSNR. Moreover, the resulting compact representations reduce downstream processing time and improve the rate-distortion performance of diverse compression and pruning methods, demonstrating the broad applicability of global structural budget regulation.

</details>


### [58] [Rarity-Aware Discrete Diffusion with Spatially Consistent Decoding for Photo-Realistic Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17612)
*Ao Li, Yapeng Du, Yi Xin, Lei Zhu, Le Zhang, Guangtao Zhai, Ce Zhu, Xiaohong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (64.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DiMOO-SR 提出了一种稀有感知的离散扩散超分辨率框架，通过逆频率采样（IFS）优先训练稀有但感知关键的视觉 token，以及空间一致性排序（SCR）在推理时利用局部邻域一致性优化 token 置信度，仅需少量并行解码步骤即可实现与连续扩散模型竞争的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散模型在超分辨率中占主导地位，但将重建建模为连续信号级去噪，难以直接利用现代多模态模型的统一 token 缩放范式。自回归模型虽能原生表示语义 token，但因果解码对高分辨率重建效率低。离散扩散结合了非因果并行预测的优势，但直接应用于超分辨率面临两个挑战：视觉 token 的长尾分布导致稀有但感知关键的纹理欠表示，以及并行解码的空间不一致性引入孤立伪影。

Method: 提出 DiMOO-SR 框架：(1) 训练阶段采用逆频率采样（IFS），根据 token 频率分布对稀有但信息丰富的 token 赋予更高采样权重；(2) 推理阶段采用空间一致性排序（SCR），利用局部邻域 token 的一致性来修正和优化 token 置信度，提升结构连贯性。整体采用离散扩散范式进行非因果并行 token 预测。

Result: 在多个真实世界超分辨率基准上，DiMOO-SR 仅需少量并行解码步骤即可达到与连续扩散模型竞争的感知质量，验证了离散扩散在生成式图像超分辨率中的潜力。

Conclusion: DiMOO-SR 证明了离散扩散模型在图像超分辨率任务中的有效性，通过稀有感知训练和空间一致性推理解决了视觉 token 长尾分布和并行解码不一致性问题，为离散 token 范式在生成式低层视觉任务中的应用提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出离散扩散框架用于图像超分辨率，核心贡献在于通过稀有感知采样和空间一致性解码实现仅需少量并行解码步骤即可达到竞争性感知质量，属于生成模型加速与高效化（少步采样/高效采样）范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"。

Importance reason: Proposes rarity-aware discrete diffusion for efficient image SR, aligning with user's interest in efficient inference and discrete token models. Signals: discrete diffusion, image super-resolution, efficient inference, rare token sampling, spatial consistency decoding

Abstract: Continuous diffusion models have become the dominant paradigm for photo-realistic image Super-Resolution (SR), but they typically formulate reconstruction as continuous signal-level denoising and incorporate semantic priors through external conditioning modules. This makes it less direct to exploit the unified token-based scaling paradigm of modern multimodal models. Autoregressive models provide a more native semantic representation by modeling images as discrete visual tokens, yet their causal decoding is inefficient for high-resolution reconstruction. Discrete diffusion offers a promising middle ground by enabling non-causal, parallel prediction over visual tokens. However, directly adapting discrete diffusion to SR remains non-trivial due to two task-specific challenges: (1) the long-tailed distribution of visual tokens, which under-represents rare but perceptually critical textures; and (2) spatially inconsistent parallel decoding, which may introduce isolated artifacts. To address these issues, we propose DiMOO-SR, a rarity-aware multimodal discrete diffusion framework for photo-realistic image SR. During training, Inverse Frequency Sampling (IFS) prioritizes under-represented but information-rich tokens. During inference, Spatial Consistency Ranking (SCR) refines token confidence using local neighborhood agreement to improve structural coherence. Extensive experiments on widely used real-world SR benchmarks demonstrate that DiMOO-SR achieves competitive perceptual quality with only a few parallel decoding steps, highlighting the potential of discrete diffusion for generative image super-resolution. The code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [59] [Three-Body Scattering for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18198)
*Peng Sun, Zhenglin Cheng, Deyuan Liu, Jun Xie, Xinyi Shang, Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三体散射建模（TBSM），将能量距离转化为每个投射体的吸引-排斥交互，实现一步生成器训练。在ImageNet-256上，像素空间PixelDiT-XL达到FID=2.23，潜空间DiT-XL达到FID=1.63（NFE=1），为高维一步生成提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型通常依赖对抗判别器、预设噪声到数据路径或自回归分解。本文希望探索一种新的范式：通过合适的分布能量诱导样本级运动，为一步生成器提供直接的回归监督，从而避免多步采样或对抗训练。

Method: 提出三体散射建模（TBSM），将能量距离转化为每个投射体的常规模拟交互：每个投射体被一个真实源吸引，同时被一个独立生成的源排斥。在投射体及其条件下，其期望等于1/2 D_E^2(P_θ, Q)的2-Wasserstein梯度流速度。通过在线追踪条件期望来降低场噪声，使用冻结图像特征中的散射训练一步生成器。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，像素空间PixelDiT-XL达到FID=2.23，潜空间DiT-XL达到FID=1.63（NFE=1），证明了TBSM在高维一步生成中的有效性。

Conclusion: TBSM通过三体散射将能量距离转化为样本级监督，实现了高质量的一步生成，建立了扩散相关监督、Drift-like动力学和GAN-like目标之间的设计映射，为高维一步生成开辟了新途径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Three-Body Scattering Modeling (TBSM)，通过能量距离驱动的样本级回归监督实现一步生成（NFE=1），属于生成模型加速与高效化（少步采样/一步生成）方向，因此映射到CANONICAL subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel one-step generative framework with strong ImageNet results, directly relevant to efficient generation and continuous modeling. Signals: one-step generation, energy distance, Wasserstein gradient flow, PixelDiT-XL FID 2.23, DiT-XL FID 1.63

Abstract: Modern generative models typically rely on an adversarial critic, a prescribed noise-to-data path, or an autoregressive factorization. Instead, we show that a proper distributional energy can induce sample-level motion and provide direct regression supervision for a one-step generator. Three-Body Scattering Modeling (TBSM) for generation turns the energy distance into a constant-size per-projectile interaction: each projectile is attracted toward one real source and repelled from one independently generated source. Conditioned on the projectile and its condition, its expectation equals the $2$-Wasserstein gradient-flow velocity of $\frac12D_E^2(P_{\theta},Q)$. A batch of $B$ frozen-target events yields $O(B)$ sample-level losses, each using one reference for its condition instead of the minibatch-wide all-pairs field used by methods such as Drifting Models. Tracking this conditional expectation online can reduce field noise. Using scattering in frozen image features, TBSM trains one-step generators on ImageNet-256, achieving FID${}=2.23$ with pixel-space PixelDiT-XL and FID${}=1.63$ with latent-space DiT-XL at NFE${}=1$. We provide a design map relating diffusion-related supervision, Drift-like dynamics, and GAN-like objectives. These results establish tracked scattering as a route to high-dimensional one-step generation. Code: .

</details>

*Xingkai Peng, Jun Jiang, Jiayang Liu, Kejiang Chen, Weiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.MM, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BSB，一种针对文生视频（T2V）模型的越狱攻击框架。BSB 利用视频生成的时间一致性特性，将有害意图编码为两个无害边界状态之间的过渡，并通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在文本代理空间高效搜索边界状态对，以极少的视频查询实现攻击。在 Veo 3.1、Sora 2、Seedance、Kling v1 等商业模型上，BSB 平均攻击成功率比最强基线提升 18.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对 T2V 模型的越狱攻击方法大多从文生图攻击迁移而来，未能充分利用视频生成固有的时间一致性特性，且需要大量视频查询优化（黑盒场景下不可行），对抗性提示搜索也缺乏结构化探索策略。

Method: BSB 框架将有害意图编码为两个单独无害的边界状态之间的过渡，利用视频生成中插值产生不安全中间帧的特性。为避免在视频空间直接评估所有候选对的高昂代价，BSB 在更廉价的文本代理空间中使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）进行结构化搜索，并定期用稀疏的视频级评估校准搜索结果。

Result: 在 Veo 3.1、Sora 2、Seedance、Kling v1 等主流商业 T2V 模型上的实验表明，BSB 在所有基线方法中取得最优性能，平均攻击成功率相对最强基线提升 18.6%。

Conclusion: 时间一致性是 T2V 模型一个被忽视但至关重要的攻击面，结构化搜索（如 MCTS）能够在有限查询预算下有效发现漏洞。该工作揭示了视频生成模型特有的安全风险，为后续防御研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生视频模型的越狱攻击（jailbreak），属于生成模型的安全与对齐问题。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）涵盖生成模型的对齐、安全、偏好优化等主题，该论文的 jailbreak 攻击正是对齐安全的一个反面研究视角，因此归入此 subtopic 最为合适。

Importance reason: Novel attack on T2V models but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: jailbreak attack, text-to-video, temporal consistency, MCTS, query-efficient

Abstract: Recently, text-to-video (T2V) models have been widely deployed, sparking growing concerns over their robustness against jailbreak attacks. Existing jailbreak methods, mostly adapted from text-to-image attacks, suffer notable drawbacks when applied to T2V systems. They fail to fully leverage temporal consistency, an inherent characteristic of video generation. Besides, these methods demand heavy video query optimization, which is infeasible in practical black-box scenarios. Their adversarial prompt search is also driven by heuristic local signals, lacking principled structured exploration strategies. To tackle these limitations, we propose BSB, a structured, query-efficient jailbreak framework for T2V models. BSB harnesses temporal consistency by encoding harmful intent as the transition between two individually harmless boundary states. Under this paradigm, the attack targets boundary-state pairs whose interpolation tends to produce unsafe intermediate frames during video generation. Directly evaluating all candidate pairs within the video space incurs prohibitive computation cost. Instead, BSB conducts Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) in a cheaper textual proxy space and regularly calibrates search outcomes with sparse video-level evaluations. We conduct comprehensive experiments on mainstream commercial T2V models including Veo 3.1, Sora 2, Seedance and Kling v1. Results show BSB surpasses all existing jailbreak baselines, delivering an average 18.6% relative gain in attack success rate over the strongest competitor across evaluated models. Our findings identify temporal consistency as an understudied yet vital attack surface for T2V models and verify that structured search facilitates effective vulnerability discovery under constrained query budgets.

</details>

*Zitong Huang, Gustavo Lucas Carvalho, Deqing Fu, Robin Jia*
