TL;DR: 提出Token-Level Off-Policy Labeling (TOPL)，一种将后训练重新定义为token级正确性预测任务的off-policy训练范式，通过在文档摘要和机器翻译任务上训练模型区分响应中的好/坏token，实现强分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy方法（如RLHF）在分布偏移下泛化能力有限，而off-policy方法直接训练模型生成off-policy token会引入不良模式。作者希望设计一种能有效利用off-policy数据、避免直接生成off-policy token的替代训练范式。

Method: 提出TOPL：将后训练转化为token级别的正确性预测任务。模型被训练来区分响应中每个token是好是坏（基于与参考响应的匹配），从而自然引导模型生成好token，而非直接训练生成off-policy token。使用LoRA进行高效微调。

Result: 在11个文档摘要数据集上，TOPL在分布外泛化上显著优于多种序列级和token级基线；在机器翻译任务上也有效。消融实验表明token级学习信号是关键，序列级变体无法带来类似收益。TOPL学到的LoRA适配器可作为线性分类头和引导向量。

Conclusion: TOPL是一种有效的off-policy训练范式，通过token级正确性预测实现强分布外泛化，且具有可解释性。该方法可推广到多种忠实生成任务。

Classification reason: 该论文提出TOPL，一种token级别的off-policy训练范式，用于提升生成模型在分布偏移下的忠实性（faithful generation）。这属于生成模型的对齐与偏好优化范畴，与[生成模型对齐与奖励]（generation_alignment）高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on token-level off-policy learning for faithful generation, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Token-level off-policy learning, faithful generation, distribution shift, document summarization, machine translation

Abstract: We propose Token-Level Off-Policy Labeling (TOPL), an off-policy training paradigm that reframes post-training as a token-level correctness prediction task. Our key intuition is that by training the model to distinguish good and bad tokens in a response, we naturally guide the model towards generating good tokens, while avoiding the pitfalls that come with directly training the model to generate off-policy tokens. Experiments on document summarization tasks show that TOPL achieves strong out-of-distribution generalization across 11 datasets against a diverse set of sequence-level and token-level baselines. We further demonstrate that TOPL transfers effectively to machine translation, suggesting that its benefits generalize across different faithful generation tasks. Through ablation studies, we confirm that our token-level learning signal is critical to good performance; sequence-level analogues do not confer similar benefits. Finally, we show that TOPL induces interpretable model updates: the LoRA adapters learned through TOPL function as linear classification heads and steering vectors.

</details>


### [62] [UniNDM: A Unified Noise-driven Detection and Mitigation Framework Against Sexual Content in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16828)
*Yao Huang, Yitong Sun, Huanran Chen, Ruochen Zhang, Shouwei Ruan, Ranjie Duan, Maoxun Yuan, Yinpeng Dong, Hui Xue, Xiaochun Cao, Xingxing Wei*

TL;DR: UniNDM 提出一个统一的基于噪声动力学的框架，用于检测和缓解文生图扩散模型中的隐式色情内容。核心发现是扩散过程早期阶段的预测噪声在正常和色情内容之间具有天然的可分离性，基于此设计了轻量级噪声检测器和噪声增强的自适应负引导缓解方法，并扩展到 DiT 架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图扩散模型对隐式色情提示（如伪装成良性术语或对抗性token的微妙线索）存在脆弱性，而现有安全机制存在根本局限：检测方法主要识别显式内容，无法捕捉隐式恶意意图；缓解方法依赖静态负提示，难以应对多样化的隐式场景。

Method: 提出 UniNDM 框架，核心洞察是扩散过程早期阶段的预测噪声在正常与色情内容间具有可分离性，理论上证明了语义浓度随timestep呈二次增长。基于此开发了轻量级噪声检测器；缓解方面提出噪声增强的自适应负引导（通过LLM动态生成上下文相关的负提示，并优化初始噪声以抑制对显式token的注意力集中），同时扩展到DiT架构的区域约束语义引导。

Result: 在U-Net和DiT模型上，对自然和对抗性数据集的全面实验表明，UniNDM在检测和缓解隐式色情内容方面显著优于SLD、UCE、Safree等现有方法。

Conclusion: UniNDM通过重新审视扩散过程中的噪声动力学，为文生图模型的安全机制提供了统一有效的检测与缓解框架，在几乎零计算开销下实现了优越性能，并可扩展到新兴的DiT架构。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生图扩散模型中的隐式色情内容检测与缓解，属于生成模型的安全对齐与可控性问题。现有 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）涵盖生成模型对齐、guidance 与可控性，与该论文的安全缓解机制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on safety detection/mitigation for text-to-image, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Not in priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched, negative keywords not triggered but topic is off-target, method uses noise dynamics but not for continuous representation or generation, safety/alignment is secondary to user's main directions

Abstract: Despite the impressive generative capabilities of text-to-image diffusion models, they remain vulnerable to implicit sexual prompts, where subtle cues disguised as benign terms or adversarial tokens unexpectedly generate the inappropriate content due to model biases or latent correlations in training data. Existing safety mechanisms face fundamental limitations: detection methods primarily identify explicit content and fail to capture implicit malicious intent, while mitigation approaches rely on static negative prompts inadequate for diverse implicit scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose UniNDM, a unified noise-driven framework that rethinks safety mechanisms through the lens of noise dynamics in diffusion processes. Our key insight is that early-stage predicted noise exhibits inherent separability between normal and sexually explicit content, which we theoretically demonstrates quadratically increasing semantic concentration with timestep. Leveraging this property, we develop a lightweight noise-based detector achieving superior accuracy with virtually no computational overhead. For mitigation, we introduce noise-enhanced adaptive negative guidance: dynamically generating context-specific negative prompts via large language models to handle diverse implicit content, while optimizing initial noise by suppressing attention concentration on explicit tokens to provide comprehensive protection. Besides the U-Net-based diffusion models, we further extend our framework to emerging Diffusion Transformer architectures through region-constrained semantic guidance tailored for their unified multimodal attention. Comprehensive experiments across U-Net models and DiT models on both natural and adversarial datasets demonstrate substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods, including SLD, UCE, Safree, etc. Our code is publicly available at .

</details>

*Siwei Meng, Yawei Luo, Shu Zhang, Ping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PSDPO（物理与语义直接偏好优化），用于解决文生视频生成中物理合理性提升与语义一致性之间的冲突。该方法在标准DPO框架内，通过约束偏好对中物理与语义信号的冲突程度来平衡两者，无需额外模型或损失项。实验表明PSDPO在VideoPhy-2上物理合理性提升达2倍，同时保持VBench上的语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型在提升物理合理性时，往往以牺牲与输入文本的语义一致性为代价。这是因为物理偏好通常通过比较两个视频的动态合理性来判断，而不考虑它们是否忠实于提示文本所描述的场景，导致物理-语义冲突成为系统性问题。

Method: 将物理-语义冲突建模为约束偏好优化问题，提出PSDPO方法。该方法根据每个偏好对的物理信号与语义信号之间的一致性程度来调节该对的贡献。通过梯度级分析证明PSDPO能将冲突对的语义漂移限制在可控残差范围内，并进一步提出分阶段优化协议以可证明地减少累积漂移。整个方法完全在标准DPO框架内运行，无需辅助模型或额外损失项。

Result: 在VideoPhy-2上，PSDPO将物理合理性相比基线提升高达2倍；在VBench上保持强语义一致性，比现有基于偏好的方法实现了更可靠的平衡。

Conclusion: PSDPO有效缓解了文生视频中物理合理性与语义一致性之间的冲突，在标准DPO框架内实现了可控的平衡，为偏好优化在视频生成中的应用提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PSDPO方法，通过直接偏好优化（DPO）框架对文生视频模型进行物理合理性与语义一致性的对齐优化，属于生成模型对齐与偏好优化的范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" (generation_alignment)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses physical plausibility vs. semantic consistency in video generation, a key challenge for world models and controllable generation. Signals: video generation, world model, direct preference optimization, physical plausibility, semantic consistency

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation models have achieved strong visual realism, but improving physical plausibility can come at the cost of semantic consistency with the input text. This tension arises because physical preference is typically determined by comparing dynamics between two videos, without accounting for whether either video faithfully depicts the scene specified by the prompt, making physical-semantic conflict a systematic tendency under this supervision paradigm. We formulate this challenge as a constrained preference optimization problem and propose Physical and Semantic Direct Preference Optimization (PSDPO), which modulates each preference pair's contribution based on the agreement between its physical and semantic signals. A gradient-level analysis shows that PSDPO bounds the semantic drift from conflicting pairs to a controllable residual, and further motivates a staged optimization protocol that provably reduces cumulative drift. The resulting method operates entirely within the standard DPO framework, requiring no auxiliary models or additional loss terms. Experiments show that PSDPO improves physical plausibility by up to $2\times$ over the baseline on VideoPhy-2, while maintaining strong semantic consistency on VBench, achieving a more reliable balance than existing preference-based methods.

</details>


### [64] [Dynamic Defense Profiling Enables Cognitive Jailbreak of Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17779)
*Dongdong Yang, Deyue Zhang, Zhao Liu, Zonghao Ying, Wenzhuo Xu, Jiankai Jin, Xiangzheng Zhang, Quanchen Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 值得关注 (68.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIND，一种认知越狱框架，将文生图模型的对抗性提示生成重新定义为对潜在防御机制的信念状态推理问题。通过多模态裁判、防御画像器和元记忆模块，在推理驱动的进化优化过程中实现自适应越狱，在 I2P 基准上达到 95.62% 的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型越狱攻击主要依赖启发式提示工程或黑盒优化，将模型反馈视为二元信号（成功/失败），忽略了文本拒绝、视觉屏蔽、语义净化等多样化失败模式中蕴含的丰富信息，导致探索效率低下和语义崩溃。

Method: MIND 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 多模态裁判（Multi-modal Judge），对模型反馈进行细粒度分解；(2) 防御画像器（Defense Profiler），迭代更新对潜在防御机制的信念；(3) 元记忆模块（Meta-Memory），检索历史上有效的攻击策略。三者统一在推理驱动的进化优化过程中，实现自适应且语义一致的越狱提示生成。

Result: 在 Stable Diffusion v1.5 的六种预处理和后处理防御设置下，MIND 达到 95.62% 的攻击成功率，显著优于现有方法。在四个商用文生图系统（包括 Wan-2.5）上验证，最高 ASR 达 91.58%。

Conclusion: MIND 通过将越狱攻击建模为对防御机制的信念推理问题，有效提升了攻击效率和语义一致性，揭示了现有防御机制的脆弱性，为文生图模型的安全对齐提供了新的评估视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文生图模型的安全对齐与越狱攻击，属于生成模型的对齐与安全性问题。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）涵盖生成模型的对齐、RLHF、偏好优化、可控性等，而本文的越狱攻击本质上是对安全对齐机制的绕过与评估，属于该范畴。同时，论文也涉及多模态安全（multimodal_safety_attack），但作为生成模型的安全对齐问题，generation_alignment 更贴切。

Importance reason: Focuses on T2I jailbreak, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: T2I jailbreak, cognitive framework, defense profiling, high ASR, not priority subtopic

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) generative models have achieved remarkable progress in synthesizing high-quality visual content, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial misuse, particularly in generating Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) images. Most existing jailbreak attacks primarily rely on heuristic prompt engineering or black-box optimization, treating model feedback as a binary signal (success or failure). This coarse-grained paradigm overlooks the rich information embedded in diverse failure modes, such as textual refusal, visual blocking, and semantic sanitization, resulting in inefficient exploration and severe semantic collapse. In this paper, we propose MIND, a cognitive jailbreak framework that reframes adversarial prompt generation as a belief-state inference problem over latent defense mechanisms. Instead of blindly searching for bypass prompts, MIND actively models the target system's latent defense mechanisms by interpreting multi-modal feedback as high-density signals. Specifically, the framework integrates three core components: (1) a Multi-modal Judge for fine-grained feedback decomposition, (2) a Defense Profiler for iterative belief updating, and (3) a Meta-Memory module for retrieving historically effective attack strategies. These components are unified within a reasoning-driven evolutionary optimization process, enabling adaptive and semantically consistent jailbreak generation. Extensive experiments on the I2P benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of MIND. Under six representative pre-processing and post-processing defense settings applied to the Stable Diffusion v1.5 model, MIND achieves an Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 95.62%, significantly outperforming existing methods. Additionally, the effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated across four widely used commercial T2I systems, achieving the highest ASR of 91.58% on Wan-2.5.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出标签增强条件扩散（LACD），核心思想是为所有无标签样本分配一个统一的"平凡标签"（trivial label），将标注数据与带平凡标签的无标注数据合并为增强数据集，然后在该增强数据集上进行联合去噪分数匹配训练。论文给出了保证目标条件分布可识别性的充分条件，并建立了严格的统计收敛性理论分析。

Result: 理论证明：当无标签样本足够多时，LACD的采样分布在总体变距离上严格快于纯监督估计器收敛，在Wasserstein-1距离上至少不慢于监督估计器。实验在合成数据、图像数据（如MNIST、CIFAR-10）和表格数据上验证了LACD相比纯监督基线在样本效率和生成质量上的显著提升。

Conclusion: LACD是一种简单有效的半监督条件扩散框架，通过标签增强策略充分利用无标签数据，兼具理论保证和实际效果，为条件扩散模型在标注稀缺场景下的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conditional diffusion models have become a powerful and flexible framework for learning complex conditional distributions from labeled data. In practice, however, acquiring high-quality labels is costly and time-consuming, leaving large volumes of unlabeled data unused. To address this, we introduce label-augmented conditional diffusion (LACD), a simple and effective approach that incorporates unlabeled examples by assigning them a designated trivial label and performing joint denoising score matching over the augmented dataset. We provide sufficient conditions guaranteeing population-level identifiability of the target conditional distribution under this scheme. Moreover, we establish rigorous statistical guarantees: when sufficiently many unlabeled samples are available, the sampling distribution produced by LACD converges strictly faster than the purely supervised estimator in total variation distance, and at least as fast in Wasserstein-1 distance. Extensive experiments on synthetic, image, and tabular benchmarks corroborate our theory and show substantial gains in sample efficiency and generative performance compared with the purely supervised estimator.

</details>

Result: CIFAR-10训练模型在GenSyn10上零样本准确率达96.86%，微调后达99.88%。在二分类真实vs合成检测中，微调模型在已知生成器上达97-99.9%准确率，但在未见过的生成器上降至79-96%，表明OOD泛化仍存在显著局限。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative AI has outpaced our ability to reliably detect its outputs, particularly when detectors encounter generators they have not seen before. We introduce GenSyn10, a CIFAR-10-aligned synthetic image dataset of 60,000 images (10 classes, 32$\times$32, 50k/10k split) generated using three architecturally diverse state-of-the-art models: FLUX.2-dev (Rectified Flow Transformer), HunyuanImage-3.0 (MoE Transformer), and Qwen-Image-2512 (Multimodal Diffusion Transformer), to advance research in AI-generated image detection. A central challenge in this domain is that detectors perform well on known generators but degrade on unseen ones. GenSyn10 addresses this limitation by curating data from multiple contemporary architectures under a standardized generation protocol, enabling controlled and systematic evaluation of out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization to novel generators. Images are generated using a template-based prompt engine and downsampled to ensure consistency. We evaluate 17 image classification models under a four-stage protocol: real-data baseline, zero-shot transfer, fine-tuning, and retention. Despite a measurable domain gap, CIFAR-10-trained models achieve up to 96.86\% zero-shot accuracy on GenSyn10, increasing to 99.88\% after fine-tuning. In binary real-vs-synthetic classification, fine-tuned models achieve 97-99.9\% accuracy on seen generators but drop to 79-96\% on images from an unseen generator, highlighting persistent limitations in OOD generalization. These results establish GenSyn10 as a controlled benchmark for studying synthetic image detection beyond single-generator settings, supporting research on robustness, domain adaptation, and cross-generator generalization.

</details>

<details>

Result: 在HVEval+和Human-AGVQA数据集上取得了优越性能，实验证明了数据集和方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HVEval+数据集和MoE-Rater方法在推进AI生成视频质量评估以及促进T2V模型的评估与优化方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: AI-generated human-centric videos play a crucial role in a wide range of modern applications. However, they often suffer from quality issues and semantic mismatches, underscoring the importance of effective quality assessment for such videos. To this end, we extend our previous dataset HVEval with pairwise preference annotations, resulting in HVEval+, the largest holistic quality assessment dataset for AI-generated human-centric videos, which comprises 1k prompts based on a comprehensive taxonomy, 20k videos generated by 24 text-to-video (T2V) models, and extensive human annotations, including 60k mean opinion scores (MOSs) and 60k preference pairs across 3 dimensions (i.e., spatial quality, temporal quality, and text-video correspondence), as well as 20k category-specific question-answer (Q&A) pairs. Along with the HVEval+ dataset, we further propose MoE-Rater, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-inspired and multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based all-in-one method that supports multi-dimensional quality rating, multi-dimensional pairwise comparison, and category-specific question answering within a single model. Specifically, we introduce Mixture of Projector Experts (MoPE) and Mixture of LoRA Experts (MoLE), together with a three-stage training strategy consisting of task-aware pre-training, task-specific adaptation, and adaptive routing optimization, to effectively unify multiple tasks, resulting in superior performance on both HVEval+ and Human-AGVQA datasets. Extensive experiments and comprehensive analysis demonstrate the significant potential of the HVEval+ dataset and the MoE-Rater method in advancing AI-generated video quality assessment and further facilitating the evaluation and optimization of T2V models.

</details>

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) leverages the 3D structure of protein targets, often complemented by other spatial constraints, to generate candidate binding molecules. While diffusion models have dominated as a leading paradigm for high-quality 3D molecule generation, LLM-based methods are rapidly emerging in molecular design and have shown competitive performance in pocket-conditioned molecular generation. However, their ability to reason about physics and 3D spatial environments is largely underexplored. In this work, we systematically analyze whether current general-purpose LLMs are capable of navigating complex 3D constraints compared to established baselines such as specialized diffusion models. We consider 3D ligand generation conditioned on protein pockets together with ligand- and interaction-derived spatial constraints, including anchor fragments, pharmacophore points, and mandatory pocket-ligand interactions. To enable this evaluation, we introduce 3D-Fit - a token-efficient benchmarking strategy for assessing LLM performance on multi-conditioned spatial molecule generation. Our findings reveal a clear pattern in LLM spatial capabilities: while they still lag behind state-of-the-art approaches, they are promising and can handle multiple spatial constraints simultaneously, enabling scaling to heterogeneous setups.

</details>

Result: TPIPS比现有度量更贴近人类感知，且能可靠地泛化到训练分布之外的数据。在文本引导检索、组合搜索和生成模型细粒度评测等任务中展现出新的能力。

Abstract: Human visual similarity judgments are context-dependent. For example, two images may be similar in shape but distinct in color. Existing perceptual similarity metrics, however, collapse these nuances into a single scalar value, offering no mechanism to condition on specific aspects. To bridge this gap, we introduce a large-scale dataset of human similarity judgments over image triplets, where each triplet is annotated across multiple, free-form semantic aspects of similarity. Benchmarking a broad range of frontier vision-language models (VLMs) reveals a considerable performance gap compared to human annotators' consensus. Leveraging our data, we fine-tune a VLM to produce our Text-Prompted Image Perceptual Similarity (TPIPS) metric, capturing multiple senses of visual similarity depending on the specified text prompt. We demonstrate that TPIPS aligns more closely with human perception and generalizes reliably beyond the training distribution. Finally, we show that TPIPS unlocks new capabilities in text-guided retrieval, compositional search, and the fine-grained evaluation of generative models. Our code, data, and trained models are at

### [70] [Test-Time Registers as Global Priors for Tokenized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16824)
*Cheng-Yao Hong, Yifan Wang, Yuewei Lin, Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 自回归视觉生成

Importance: 值得关注 (77.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 RegToken，一种无需训练的方法，通过将 Vision Transformer 中注意力汇聚（attention sink）相关的 register 结构转化为全局先验 token，用于 tokenized 图像生成。该方法通过 NFN 层定位、TokenRank 子空间提取和投影-守恒更新三个步骤，在不修改预训练权重的情况下提升生成质量（FID-5k 从 20.5 降至 20.1，SigLIP 从 3.6 升至 3.9）并加速测试时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力模型中的 attention sink 现象（少数 token 反复吸引注意力并积累异常大的激活值）在视觉 Transformer 中与 register 密切相关，现有研究主要从可解释性角度分析 register，但尚未探索能否将其作为即插即用的信号用于生成任务。本文旨在验证 register 结构能否被转化为轻量级全局先验以改进 tokenized 图像生成。

Method: RegToken 包含三个步骤：(i) NFN-based layer localization——利用 NFN（Neural Feature Norm）定位包含丰富 register 信息的层；(ii) TokenRank-guided subspace extraction——通过 TokenRank 引导提取 register 子空间；(iii) projection-and-conservation update——在 register 子空间上进行投影与守恒更新，将 register 结构转化为少量全局先验 token。这些 token 被插入到冻结的紧凑 1D token 生成流水线中，无需修改预训练权重。

Result: 在 ImageNet 上使用 OpenCLIP 和 DINOv2 的实验表明：register 特征比 [CLS] 读出和 patch 均值特征具有更强的低频集中性，且与像素空间 DCT 低频能量存在一致（虽适度）的相关性。插入 RegToken 后，FID-5k 从 20.5 降至 20.1，SigLIP 从 3.6 升至 3.9，测试时优化步数（Steps@τ）从 74 降至 52。

Conclusion: 本文表明，通常被视为注意力伪影的 register 结构可以被重新利用为轻量级全局先验，用于改进 tokenized 图像生成。RegToken 无需重新训练即可提升生成质量和加速优化，为利用注意力模型内部结构进行生成任务提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 tokenized image generation（token化图像生成），属于自回归视觉生成范式。论文提出的 RegToken 方法在紧凑的 1D token 生成流水线上进行改进，属于自回归视觉生成范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "自回归视觉生成"。

Importance reason: Directly addresses tokenized image generation with a training-free method, aligning with autoregressive image generation and efficient inference priorities. Signals: tokenized image generation, training-free, attention sink, register tokens, global priors

Abstract: Attention-based models often develop attention sinks, where a small number of tokens repeatedly attract attention and accumulate unusually large activations. In vision transformers, these outliers are closely related to registers, which have been diagnostically linked to global, low-frequency image structure. Existing work has largely studied registers through interpretability analyses and linear probes, leaving open whether they can be operationalized as plug-and-play signals for generation without retraining. We revisit this question in tokenized image generation. Using OpenCLIP and DINOv2 on ImageNet, we find that test-time register features exhibit stronger low-frequency concentration than both [CLS] readouts and patch-mean features, and show a consistent (albeit moderate) correlation with pixel-space DCT low-frequency energy. Motivated by these diagnostics, we introduce RegToken, a training-free procedure that converts register structure into a small set of global prior tokens by (i) NFN-based layer localization, (ii) TokenRank-guided subspace extraction, and (iii) a projection-and-conservation update on the register subspace. Inserted into a frozen compact 1D token generation pipeline, RegToken improves ImageNet generation and alignment metrics (e.g., FID-5k 20.5 to 20.1, SigLIP 3.6 to 3.9) without modifying pretrained weights, and accelerates test-time optimization (Steps@$\tau$ 74 to 52). Overall, our results suggest that structures often viewed as attention artifacts can be repurposed as lightweight global priors for tokenized generation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D感知音频生成方法仅建模空心刚体的表面几何，忽略了内部填充状态（如水、米粒等）这一关键物理因素，该因素显著影响声学共振和阻尼特性。因此需要一种能够感知物体内部填充状态的细粒度冲击声音生成方法。

Method: 1) 构建FillImpact数据集：包含88个真实物体、5000+录音，涵盖不同内部填充物（水、米）、连续填充水平和不同敲击材料。2) 提出FillGauss框架：将3DGS几何特征、精确3D空间敲击坐标和细粒度文本物理条件融合到潜扩散架构中，实现位置感知、敲击物感知和填充物感知的音频生成。

Result: 实验表明FillGauss能够生成符合物理原理的高保真冲击声音，在物理一致性音频生成任务上达到新SOTA水平。声学分析证实收集的数据与声学共振和阻尼的物理定律高度一致。

Conclusion: FillGauss首次将内部填充状态纳入3D感知声音生成，证明了结合3DGS几何表示与物理条件控制能够生成物理可信的跨模态音频，为物理驱动的多模态音频生成建立了新基准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于3D视觉信息（3DGS）的物理感知冲击声音生成，属于跨模态条件音频生成任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel task and dataset for filling-aware impact sound generation, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, impact sound generation, FillImpact dataset, latent diffusion, multimodal generation

Abstract: Synthesizing physically plausible impact sounds from visual observations remains a great challenge in multi-modal AI. Existing 3D-aware audio generation methods primarily model the surface geometry of hollow rigid bodies. However, they fundamentally overlook internal filling states, a critical physical factor that drastically modulates acoustic resonance and damping. To address this issue, we have defined a new task called Fine-Grained Filling-Aware Impact Sound Generation. As a foundational step, we first introduce the fine-grained fill-aware dataset (FillImpact), a pioneering multi-modal collection comprising over 5,000 rigorous acoustic recordings from 88 diverse real-world objects. It captures impact interactions with varying internal contents (i.e., water, rice), a continuous range of fill levels, and distinct striker materials. Furthermore, comprehensive acoustic analysis confirms that the collected data closely aligns with established physical laws governing acoustic resonance and damping, indicating its suitability for physically grounded modeling. Building on this dataset, we propose a novel generative framework (FillGauss) that integrates 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with internal state conditioning for sound generation. By fusing 3DGS geometric features, precise 3D spatial strike coordinates, and fine-grained textual physical conditions within a latent diffusion architecture, FillGauss enables position-aware, striker-aware, and filling-aware audio generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach could generate high-fidelity impact sounds that adhere to underlying physical principles, establishing a new state-of-the-art for physically grounded cross-modal audio generation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到音乐生成模型（如基于整流流的扩散Transformer）在生成任务上表现出色，但将其扩展到编辑真实录音面临三大挑战：需要准确的确定性反演、可靠的音乐结构保持、以及反演和生成过程中数值稳定的积分。现有音乐编辑方法在这些方面存在不足。

Method: FlowSonic 基于预训练的整流流扩散Transformer，首先通过确定性ODE反演将真实录音映射到潜空间；在编辑过程中，复用反演阶段提取的交叉注意力表示以保持原始音乐结构；引入高阶ODE求解器（如RK4）替代低阶方法，系统研究不同数值积分方案对轨迹稳定性、结构保持和语义可控性的影响。

Result: 在音色迁移和风格修改任务上的综合实验表明，FlowSonic 在语义对齐、和声保持、结构一致性和感知音频质量方面一致优于现有音乐编辑方法。几何和实证分析表明，高阶数值积分策略改善了潜轨迹稳定性，带来了更可靠的音乐编辑效果。

Conclusion: FlowSonic 通过确定性反演、交叉注意力复用和高阶ODE求解器的结合，实现了稳定且高质量的零样本文本引导音乐编辑，为基于扩散模型的音频编辑提供了有效的数值稳定性解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本文本引导的音乐编辑（timbre-transfer和genre-modification），属于音频生成与编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Music editing is outside your priority subtopics, and the method's focus on audio-specific inversion and ODE solvers does not directly advance your core interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: zero-shot music editing, rectified flow diffusion transformer, high-order ODE solver, cross-attention reuse for structure preservation, timbre transfer and genre modification

Abstract: Zero-shot text-guided editing of real-world music recordings requires balancing semantic modification with faithful preservation of the original musical structure. Although recent diffusion transformers trained with rectified flow have achieved remarkable success in text-to-music generation, extending them to edit existing recordings remains challenging because editing requires accurate deterministic inversion, reliable structural preservation, and numerically stable integration throughout the inversion and generation processes. We present FlowSonic, a zero-shot music editing framework built upon a pretrained diffusion transformer trained with rectified flow. FlowSonic first deterministically inverts a real-world recording into the latent space and preserves its musical structure during editing by reusing cross-attention representations extracted during inversion. To improve the numerical reliability of inversion-based editing, we introduce a high-order ODE solver and systematically investigate how different numerical integration schemes influence trajectory stability, structural preservation, and semantic controllability. Comprehensive experiments on timbre-transfer and genre-modification tasks demonstrate that FlowSonic consistently outperforms existing music editing methods across semantic alignment, harmonic preservation, structural consistency, and perceptual audio quality. We further provide geometric and empirical analyses showing how the proposed numerical integration strategy improves latent trajectory stability and leads to more reliable music editing.

</details>

### [73] [3D FaceShell: Attribute Transfer in 3D Face Avatars as a VLM Defense Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16280)
*Weston Bondurant, Srijan Das, Hieu Le, Stephanie Schuckers*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 3D FaceShell 提出一种针对3D面部头像的VLM防御机制，通过在3D表征上添加可学习的、视觉上不明显的扰动，在保持几何保真度和面部身份的前提下，干扰VLM对敏感属性的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D面部头像被广泛部署，但VLM无需微调即可从渲染图像中推断出敏感属性（如年龄、性别等），现有防御方法主要在2D图像空间操作，无法处理3D面部表征的语义操纵问题，因此需要一种面向3D头像的隐私保护方法。

Method: 提出3D FaceShell框架，在原始3D表征上附加一个可学习的高斯壳（Gaussian shell），通过多视角嵌入对齐优化产生细微、空间分布的扰动，使扰动在视觉上不显眼但能有效重定向VLM的属性推断，且保持视角一致性。

Result: 在重建的名人面部头像和多个黑盒VLM上的实验表明，3D FaceShell显著提高了属性注入率和属性错配率，同时保持了高感知相似度和身份一致性。

Conclusion: 证明了可以在不损害人类可识别外观的前提下，操纵VLM对3D面部的语义解释，为3D数字资产的隐私保护提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出3D FaceShell，通过在3D面部表征上添加可学习的扰动来防御VLM对敏感属性的推断，属于多模态安全与攻防（防御）方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on VLM defense for 3D face avatars, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: VLM defense, 3D face avatar, attribute transfer, privacy, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Photorealistic 3D face avatars are increasingly deployed as reusable digital assets across applications such as telepresence, animation, and personalized media. At the same time, vision-language models (VLMs) can infer sensitive attributes from rendered images with open-ended semantic reasoning without any fine-tuning. This creates a new privacy challenge: once a 3D face avatar is shared, any of its renderings can be analyzed to extract high-level facial attributes. Existing defenses largely operate in 2D image space and do not address identity-preserving semantic manipulation of 3D facial representations. We propose 3D FaceShell, a framework for steering VLM interpretations of faces rendered from 3D models while preserving geometric fidelity and facial identity. 3D FaceShell augments the original 3D representation with a learnable Gaussian shell that produces subtle, spatially distributed perturbations optimized through multi-view embedding alignment. The perturbations are designed to be visually inconspicuous yet sufficient to redirect VLM-based attribute inference in a view-consistent manner. Extensive experiments on reconstructed celebrity face avatars and multiple black-box VLMs demonstrate that 3D FaceShell significantly increases attribute injection and mismatch rates while maintaining high perceptual similarity and identity consistency. Our results show that it is possible to manipulate VLM-level semantic interpretation of 3D faces without compromising their human-recognizable appearance.

</details>


### [74] [PlanFlip: Attacking Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Planning-Phase Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16199)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (51.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PlanFlip 提出针对多智能体LLM系统的规划阶段提示注入攻击框架，包含四种攻击策略（GoalSubstitution、PriorityInversion、ContextPollution、RoleConfusion），实验发现更强的模型反而更脆弱（GPT-5 ASR=0.68），同质化流水线存在相关智能体盲区，而推理增强模型（DeepSeek-R1）能完全抵抗。同时提出两种防御方法（GoalAnchorCheck 和 CrossAgentConsensus），检测率最高达1.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统日益依赖Planner将目标分解为子任务序列，但规划阶段的提示注入攻击尚未被系统研究。作者发现规划阶段是一个关键攻击面：单次注入Planner上下文即可产生级联放大效应，同时污染所有下游子任务。

Method: 提出PlanFlip框架，包含四种规划阶段提示注入攻击：GoalSubstitution（PF-1，替换目标）、PriorityInversion（PF-2，颠倒优先级）、ContextPollution（PF-3，污染上下文）、RoleConfusion（PF-4，混淆角色），每种攻击伪装成合理的工具输出以规避关键词过滤。在9个前沿LLM上进行了3,479轮实验评估。提出两种防御方法：GoalAnchorCheck（D1，目标锚点检查）和CrossAgentConsensus（D2，跨智能体共识）。

Result: (1) 能力放大脆弱性：GPT-5攻击成功率最高（ASR=0.68），反驳了更强模型更安全的假设；(2) 同质化流水线存在相关智能体盲区：GPT-4o和Llama-3.3-70B的ASR接近0但Stealth=1.00且StepShift>0，攻击重构了计划但同骨干网络的Critic报告对齐（语义偏差仅-0.20到-0.32，r=0.943）；(3) 推理增强模型抵抗注入：DeepSeek-R1在所有攻击下StepShift=0.00。防御方法在15/16个单元格中优于同骨干基线，检测率最高达1.00。

Conclusion: 异构模型多样性是多智能体系统的安全前提；同质骨干网络内的冗余无法防御规划阶段攻击。推理增强模型（如DeepSeek-R1）对提示注入具有天然抵抗力，为构建更安全的多智能体系统提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多智能体LLM系统中的规划阶段提示注入攻击（PlanFlip），属于多模态/LLM安全与攻防范畴。虽然论文聚焦于纯文本LLM系统而非多模态，但其攻击框架（提示注入、越狱、安全评测）与多模态安全攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）的核心主题高度一致，且该[CANONICAL]子主题涵盖LLM安全攻击与防御方法。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent LLM security, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multi-agent LLM security, planning-phase injection, capability amplifies vulnerability, reasoning-augmented models resist attacks, homogeneous pipeline blind spot

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on a Planner to decompose goals into sub-task sequences that downstream Executor and Critic agents execute and audit. We identify the planning phase as a critical attack surface: a single injection into the Planner's context achieves cascade amplification, corrupting all downstream sub-tasks simultaneously. We introduce PlanFlip, a framework comprising four planning-phase prompt injection attacks -- GoalSubstitution (PF-1), PriorityInversion (PF-2), ContextPollution (PF-3), and RoleConfusion (PF-4) -- each disguised as plausible tool outputs to evade keyword filters. Evaluating nine frontier LLMs across 3,479 episodes, we uncover three findings: (1) capability amplifies vulnerability -- GPT-5 achieves the highest attack success rate (ASR = 0.68), contradicting the assumption that stronger models are inherently more secure; (2) homogeneous pipelines exhibit a correlated-agent blind spot -- GPT-4o and Llama-3.3-70B show ASR near 0 yet Stealth = 1.00 and StepShift > 0, with attacks restructuring plans while the same-backbone Critic reports alignment (two independent judges confirm -0.20 to -0.32 semantic deviation, r = 0.943); (3) reasoning-augmented models resist injections -- DeepSeek-R1 achieves StepShift = 0.00 across all attacks. We propose GoalAnchorCheck (D1) and CrossAgentConsensus (D2), achieving detection rates up to 1.00 and outperforming same-backbone baselines in 15 of 16 cells. Our key insight: heterogeneous model diversity is a security prerequisite for multi-agent systems; redundancy within a homogeneous backbone provides no protection against planning-phase attacks.

</details>


### [75] [Diagnosing Correctness Probes under Self-Judgement Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16799)
*Yi-Long Lu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文发现语言模型隐藏状态中"正确性探测"信号实际上编码的是模型的自判断（self-judgement）而非客观正确性（objective correctness）。通过构造冲突案例和跨域迁移实验，证明自判断方向具有可靠的跨域迁移能力，而客观正确性方向在迁移时表现低于随机水平，揭示了正确性探测方法存在严重的语义混淆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正确性探测方法假设从隐藏状态中解码的信号反映的是客观正确性，但客观正确性通常与模型的自判断高度一致，导致解码出的信号语义模糊不清。作者希望厘清：这些探测到的信号究竟编码的是客观正确性还是模型的自判断？

Method: 构造客观正确性(OC)与自判断(SJ)冲突的案例（模型高置信度但回答错误，或低置信度但回答正确），使用线性探针对比分析。通过因子化方法估计SJ和OC各自关联的方向，并在数学推理和事实回忆两个领域间进行跨域迁移实验，评估两种方向的迁移能力。同时控制答案似然、序列长度和空方向等混淆因素。

Result: 在4个指令微调模型（最高14B参数）上，SJ关联方向在所有模型的跨域迁移中均高于随机水平，而OC关联方向在所有条件下的预期OC排序点估计均低于随机水平。这种迁移不对称性在模型中间到深层出现，并在MMLU和二值TruthfulQA上得到验证。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态中可迁移的正确性信号实际上编码的是模型的自判断而非客观正确性，仅凭迁移性不能证明探测到的是客观正确性语义。这提醒研究者在使用正确性探测方法时需要谨慎解释结果。

Classification reason: 论文研究语言模型内部表征中"正确性探测"的混淆问题——自判断(SJ)与客观正确性(OC)在隐藏状态中的信号混淆。这属于模型可靠性/忠实性范畴，与多模态安全与攻防中的"可靠性"主题相关。虽然论文主要针对语言模型而非多模态，但其核心问题（模型内部表征的语义混淆与可靠性）与多模态幻觉/可靠性方向高度契合，且该方向下已有大量关于模型可靠性、事实性的研究。

Importance reason: The paper's focus on probing correctness in LLMs is methodologically sound but falls outside the user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: probing correctness, self-judgement confounding, cross-domain transfer, LLM interpretability, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Hidden-state readouts can predict whether language-model outputs are correct, but objective correctness (OC) usually agrees with the model's own self-judgement (SJ), leaving the decoded signal semantically ambiguous. We construct conflict cases in which OC and SJ predict opposite readout orderings. On high-confidence disagreements, conventional correctness-labelled contrasts often rank incorrect/self-endorsed responses above correct/self-rejected responses, following SJ rather than OC. We estimate factorial SJ- and OC-associated directions and evaluate their polarity across mathematical reasoning and factual recall. Across four instruction-tuned models up to 14B parameters, the SJ-associated direction transfers above chance in both cross-domain directions for every model, whereas the OC-associated direction has a below-chance point estimate for the expected OC ordering in every corresponding condition. This transfer asymmetry develops across middle-to-late layers, persists under answer-likelihood, sequence-length, and null-direction controls, and extends to MMLU and binary TruthfulQA without target-domain direction fitting. Across the studied models and diagnostic subsets, the most reliably transferable component preserves SJ-associated polarity. Transferability alone therefore does not establish objective-correctness semantics.

</details>


### [76] [One Modality to Forget Them All: Enhancing Cross-Modal Unlearning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16442)
*Sudharshan Balaji, Yili Ren, Guangjing Wang, Yimin Chen, Ning Wang*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了VLM中跨模态遗忘迁移问题，发现遗忘在模态间不对称且不完整，在typographic攻击下可恢复。提出CrossInf策略，通过影响引导聚焦遗忘关键Transformer块，减少迁移差距并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要聚焦于纯文本LLM，但VLM同时处理文本和视觉输入，一个模态的遗忘是否迁移到另一模态尚不清楚。此外，typographic攻击等视觉文本操纵可能绕过遗忘机制，存在安全隐患。

Method: 在LLaVA-1.5、InstructBLIP、IDEFICS三种VLM架构上进行跨模态遗忘实验，分析遗忘迁移的不对称性。提出CrossInf策略，基于影响函数识别对跨模态泛化贡献最大的Transformer块，将遗忘集中施加于这些关键块上。

Result: 文本遗忘可部分迁移到视觉模态但不对称；typographic攻击可恢复已遗忘知识。CrossInf将跨模态迁移差距减少一半以上，同时保持模型效用；将typographic攻击成功率降至接近零。人类评估一致性κ=0.77。

Conclusion: VLM的跨模态遗忘存在显著的不对称性和浅层性问题，CrossInf通过影响引导的针对性遗忘有效缓解了这些问题，为VLM安全遗忘提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）中的跨模态遗忘（unlearning）安全性问题，包括文本遗忘是否迁移到视觉模态、typographic攻击下遗忘失效等，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM unlearning security, not aligned with user's priority on generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: VLM unlearning, cross-modal transfer, typographic attack, influence-guided mitigation, shallow unlearning analysis

Abstract: Machine unlearning is widely used to remove hazardous knowledge from large language models. Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs), however, process both text and visual inputs, raising a fundamental security question: does unlearning in one modality transfer to the other? We present the first systematic, bidirectional study of cross-modal unlearning transfer across three VLM architectures: LLaVA-1.5 (MLP projection), InstructBLIP (Q-Former), and IDEFICS (gated cross-attention). We find that unlearning transfers across modalities, but the transfer is asymmetric and incomplete. In some cases, text unlearning strongly transfers to vision. However, this robustness is not preserved under typographic attacks that manipulate the visual presentation of text. Under such attacks, previously unlearned knowledge can be readily recovered, indicating shallow unlearning. To address the transfer gap and shallow robustness, we propose \textsc{CrossInf}, an influence-guided mitigation strategy. Motivated by the observation that different model components contribute unequally to cross-modal transfer, \textsc{CrossInf} focuses unlearning on transformer blocks that most influence cross-modal generalization. It reduces the transfer gap by more than half in architectures with strong fusion, while preserving model utility. It also improves robustness under typographic attacks, reducing the attack success rate to near zero. We further conduct human evaluation with three annotators ($\kappa{=}0.77$) to validate our findings. Finally, we analyze shallow unlearning using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), providing insights into the observed transfer behavior and robustness limitations.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实消融框架CVAA，通过生成式图像修复移除前视相机图像中的单个检测对象，分析端到端自动驾驶模型（Alpamayo 1轨迹预测器）的因果响应，并结合机制可解释性技术探索模型内部表征，旨在提升自动驾驶系统的可解释性与人机信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型虽然能有效导航复杂场景，但其内部逻辑不透明，缺乏可解释性，难以建立人机信任。现有方法难以系统性地分析场景中每个对象对模型规划行为的因果影响。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Vision Action Analysis (CVAA)框架：1) 使用photorealistic生成式修复从前置相机图像中逐个移除检测到的对象，构建反事实图像集（Counter-nuScenes）；2) 比较原始图像与反事实图像下模型输出的差异，量化每个对象的因果影响；3) 结合机制可解释性技术，对比原始与修复图像在模型各层的中间表征，分析模型内部编码特征。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集210个场景上对Alpamayo 1轨迹预测器进行测试，发现车辆和行人等路径内对象具有主导因果影响，交通灯的影响与其图像占比不成比例，同时发现模型对某些人类认为无关的对象也有强烈响应。

Conclusion: CVAA框架提供了从行为审计到表征理解的可解释性路径，有助于构建可解释的自动驾驶系统，增强人机信任。同时揭示了模型可能编码了与人类可理解场景元素不同的内部特征。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自动驾驶VLM模型的可解释性与因果分析，通过反事实消融（移除检测对象）和机制可解释性分析模型内部表征，属于多模态模型的安全性与可靠性范畴，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）中关于模型可解释性、对抗分析、安全评测的主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on interpretability for autonomous driving, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Counterfactual ablation, Vision-Language-Action models, Autonomous vehicle interpretability, Generative inpainting, Mechanistic interpretability

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models are now able to navigate complex road scenarios, mapping raw sensor observations directly to observed paths for open-loop evaluation and often effective driving in closed-loop evaluation. Yet the internal logic of these safety-critical systems remains largely opaque, due to the complexity of traffic scenes. We propose a counterfactual ablation framework called Counterfactual Vision Action Analysis (CVAA) that systematically removes individual detected objects from front-camera images using photorealistic generative inpainting to prepare counterfactual sets to evaluate the difference in the model's response. This isolates the causal effect of each object's presence on the model's planning behaviour. Applied to the Alpamayo 1 trajectory predictor across 210 nuScenes driving scenes, we create a dataset Counter -nuScenes, using which we see that vehicles and pedestrians within the model's 'path' dominate causal influence as expected, while traffic lights, as expected, exert disproportionate effect relative to their image footprint. However, we also find cases where the model responds strongly to objects a human driver would consider irrelevant. This brings forth a deeper question: does the model itself view the scene as a sum of individual objects influencing the outcome, or does it encode an entirely different set of internal features that do not correspond to human-legible scene elements? To further understand this, we compare intermediate representations of original and inpainted image pairs using mechanistic interpretability techniques and examine the effect of the removal through the various model layers. Together, these two stages offer a path from behavioral auditing to representational understanding, creating explainable driving systems and solidifying human-AI trust.

</details>


### [78] [Measuring Monosemanticity in Sparse Autoencoders via Latent Activation Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17770)
*Katarzyna Filus, Sebastian Pokuciński*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (47.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Tversky Monosemanticity Score (TMS)，一种无需外部标签或嵌入模型的无标签度量，通过二值化SAE潜变量的激活集一致性来评估稀疏自编码器的单语义性。实验表明TMS比基于嵌入的替代指标更少受编码器各向异性影响，且与已有单语义性指标保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单语义性评估指标要么需要外部概念标签，要么依赖预训练嵌入模型，且对编码器的几何结构（各向异性）敏感，缺乏一种无标签、鲁棒的度量方法。

Method: 受Tversky相似性理论启发，将单语义性操作化为二值化SAE潜变量的激活集一致性（activation-set coherence），提出TMS指标。该指标无需外部嵌入编码器，通过计算每个潜变量激活样本集合的内部一致性来度量单语义性。在DINOv3、CLIP、BLIP2等预训练视觉/视觉-语言模型上训练的SAE（TopK和BatchTopK两种范式）上进行评估。

Result: TMS比基于嵌入的替代指标更少受编码器各向异性影响；与已有单语义性指标保持对齐；揭示了不同基础模型上SAE训练动态的差异；在编码器各向异性条件下，TMS对基于探针的概念删除有效性提供了更强的指示。

Conclusion: TMS是一种有效的无标签单语义性度量，对编码器几何结构更鲁棒，可作为现有指标的补充或替代，有助于更好地理解和评估SAE的可解释性。

Classification reason: 论文提出TMS指标用于评估稀疏自编码器（SAE）的单语义性（monosemanticity），属于可解释性/机械可解释性方向。在多模态理解与生成方向下，最接近的现有子主题是"多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack），因为该子主题涵盖安全评测与可靠性分析，而单语义性评估是模型可解释性与可靠性的重要组成部分。但该论文更偏向通用可解释性方法，不完全匹配任何现有子主题。考虑到现有子主题中"多模态安全与攻防"是相对最接近的（涉及模型内部表征分析与可靠性），且论文在视觉和视觉-语言模型上验证，故复用该子主题。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on mechanistic interpretability of SAEs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Novel TMS metric for monosemanticity, No external labels needed, Evaluated on vision and vision-language models, Addresses encoder anisotropy, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords

Abstract: Within Explainable Artificial Intelligence, mechanistic interpretability uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to extract more interpretable features from neural representations. However, assessing their monosemanticity, and thus explanation quality, remains challenging. Existing metrics require external concept labels or depend on pretrained embedding models, making them sensitive to encoder's geometry. We introduce the Tversky Monosemanticity Score (TMS), a label-free metric that operationalizes monosemanticity as activation-set coherence of binarized SAE latents, and does not require external embedding encoders. We evaluate TMS on SAEs trained on features from pretrained vision and vision-language models (DINOv3, CLIP, BLIP2), two common SAE regimes (TopK, BatchTopK), multiple sparsity levels, and expansion factors. Our results show that TMS is less affected by encoder anisotropy than its embedding-based alternative, while remaining aligned with established monosemanticity indicators. TMS also reveals distinct SAE training dynamics across base models. Moreover, under encoder anisotropy, TMS provides a stronger indication of probe-based concept deletion effectiveness, while being competitive otherwise.

</details>


### [79] [Simple Domain Generalization for Strong Pixel-Level Image Tampering Detection in Modern VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18230)
*Yi Tang, Xinyi Shang, Jiacheng Cui, Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat, Jiacheng Liu, Xiaohan Zhao, Tran Dinh Tien, Ahmed Elhagry, Salwa K. Al Khatib, Tianjun Yao, Yonina C. Eldar, Jing-Hao Xue, Hao Li, Salman Khan, Zhiqiang Shen*

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (70.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单但有效的域泛化训练框架，用于检测现代VLM（如ChatGPT、Gemini等）生成的图像中的像素级篡改。通过平衡小批量采样和延迟注入策略，在跨模型和分布外场景下显著优于先前SOTA方法PIXAR，平均gIoU和cIoU分别提升26.1%和26.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLM的图像生成和编辑能力日益增强，使得像素级图像篡改检测变得愈发重要且具有挑战性，尤其是在跨模型和分布外（OOD）场景下。现有方法在应对不同VLM生成的多样化篡改分布时泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出一个域泛化训练框架，包含两个核心策略：(1) 平衡小批量采样（balanced minibatch sampling），在每个小批量中策略性地采样篡改图像和真实图像，防止优化偏向篡改伪影或干净图像先验，避免训练崩溃；(2) 延迟注入策略（late-injection strategy），检测器先在大规模基础数据上训练至稳定收敛，再暴露于少量来自新兴VLM分布的新支持数据，提高适应性而不过拟合。

Result: 在GPT-Images-2.0、Gemini-3.1、FLUX.2和Seedream 4.5等OOD VLM上，相比先前SOTA方法PIXAR，平均gIoU提升26.1%，平均cIoU提升26.8%。

Conclusion: 所提出的简单域泛化框架在像素级篡改定位和OOD鲁棒性方面取得了显著提升，验证了平衡采样和延迟注入策略的有效性，为VLM生成图像的篡改检测提供了实用且强健的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究现代VLM生成图像的像素级篡改检测（tampering detection），属于多模态安全与防御范畴，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）这一CANONICAL子主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM tampering detection, not aligned with user's priority on generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: VLM security, domain generalization, tampering detection, not generation or world model, no continuous token or flow matching

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) have significantly improved image generation and editing capabilities, making pixel-level image tampering detection increasingly important yet challenging under cross-model and out-of-distribution shifts. This work studies domain generalization for pixel-level image tampering detection in modern VLMs like ChatGPT, Gemini, Qwen-Image, etc., aiming to learn tampering localization models that remain robust across diverse VLM-generated manipulation distributions. We propose a simple yet effective domain-generalized training framework built on two practical strategies. First, we introduce a balanced minibatch sampling scheme that strategically samples tampered and real images in each minibatch, preventing biased optimization toward either manipulated artifacts or clean-image priors and avoiding training collapse, ensuring that each optimization step receives proper sampled gradient signals. Second, we adopt a simple late-injection strategy, where the detector is first trained on large-scale base data until stable convergence, and then exposed to a small amount of newly selected supporting data from emerging VLM distributions, improving adaptability without overfitting to limited new domains. Together, these components provide a simple yet strong recipe for improving pixel-level tampering localization and OOD robustness across modern VLMs. Despite the conceptual simplicity, our framework outperforms the prior state-of-the-art PIXAR by a large margin of 26.1% and 26.8% relative improvement in average gIoU and cIoU, respectively, across OOD VLMs of GPT-Images-2.0, Gemini-3.1, FLUX.2, and Seedream 4.5. Our code is available at

</details>

### [80] [Look Clearly Before Answering: Mitigating Hallucinations in LVLMs via Saliency-Driven Perceptual Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16841)
*Pengxu Chen, Yao Zhu, Guangming Zhu, Jun Sheng, Jincai Huang, Xiangyang Ji, Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SDPR（Saliency-Driven Perceptual Realignment），一个无需训练的训练-free框架，通过显著性驱动的注意力重分配、KV缓存对齐和先验约束对比解码，缓解LVLM在推理过程中视觉感知逐渐退化导致的幻觉问题，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在推理过程中存在视觉感知逐渐退化的问题（包括注意力被非语义sink token劫持、KV缓存空间失真、语言先验主导），导致幻觉生成。现有方法未充分探索这种渐进式退化。

Method: SDPR包含三个组件：(1) 显著性驱动的注意力重分配，释放被非语义sink token劫持的注意力，恢复关键视觉证据；(2) 显著性驱动的KV缓存对齐，在生成过程中保留与查询相关的视觉特征；(3) 先验约束对比解码，惩罚由主导语言先验导致的不忠实预测。

Result: 在多个LVLM架构上的实验表明，SDPR在幻觉基准和通用基准上均优于现有最先进方法，无需额外训练且运行时开销极小。

Conclusion: SDPR通过在整个生成轨迹中对视觉意识进行整体对齐，有效缓解了LVLM的幻觉问题，是一种轻量级、即插即用的训练-free解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于LVLM的幻觉缓解问题，提出无需训练的视觉感知对齐框架SDPR，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses LVLM hallucination via training-free visual realignment, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous/multimodal models or generation. Signals: training-free hallucination mitigation, saliency-driven attention redistribution, KV cache alignment, contrastive decoding, LVLM reliability

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in multimodal understanding. However, they remain prone to hallucinations, generating responses that are inconsistent with the visual evidence. Existing mitigation methods largely address language-prior bias or cross-modal imbalance, while progressive visual degradation across perception and memory remains underexplored. In this work, we propose Saliency-Driven Perceptual Realignment (SDPR), a training-free framework that mitigates the degradation of visual awareness throughout inference. Specifically, we first introduce saliency-driven attention redistribution to release attention hijacked by non-semantic sink tokens, thereby recovering critical visual evidence. Second, we identify spatial distortion in the KV cache and propose saliency-driven cache alignment to preserve query-relevant visual features during generation. Finally, we introduce prior-constrained contrastive decoding to penalize unfaithful predictions induced by dominant language priors. Our proposed SDPR is robust against hallucinations due to its holistic alignment of visual awareness across the entire generative trajectory. Extensive experiments across diverse LVLM architectures show that SDPR outperforms state-of-the-art methods on both hallucination and general-purpose benchmarks, requiring no additional training and incurring minimal runtime overhead. The code is available \href{ }{\color{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [81] [Diversity-Oriented Fine-Tuning for Uncertainty-Based Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16643)
*Qiuyuan Li, Hongliang Dai, Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

TL;DR: 本文提出多样性导向微调策略（SFT和DPO），通过鼓励模型生成更多样化的输出，使幻觉回答不再具有低语义熵，从而提升基于语义熵的幻觉检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法通常在推理阶段进行，不修改模型本身。作者发现基于语义熵的检测方法中，许多错误输出因模型多次生成几乎相同的错误答案而无法被检测到，因此希望通过微调策略增强模型输出的可检测性。

Method: 提出多样性导向微调方法，包含两种策略：(1) 基于SFT的多样性微调，鼓励模型生成多样化的回答；(2) 基于DPO的多样性微调，通过偏好优化使模型避免产生低语义熵的幻觉回答。两种策略均旨在降低模型对幻觉答案生成低语义熵输出的倾向。

Result: 实验表明，经过多样性微调后，模型对幻觉答案产生低语义熵响应的可能性降低，幻觉检测效果得到提升，最终结果优于或与最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: 通过微调模型本身来增强幻觉的可检测性是可行且有效的方向，多样性导向微调能够显著改善基于语义熵的幻觉检测性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于语义熵的幻觉检测方法，通过多样性微调（SFT/DPO）提升幻觉的可检测性，属于多模态幻觉缓解与可靠性提升方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Focuses on hallucination detection via fine-tuning, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: hallucination detection, semantic entropy, diversity-oriented fine-tuning, SFT, DPO

Abstract: Existing hallucination detection methods are typically conducted at the inference stage, without making any modifications to the model itself. In this paper, we are interested in exploring fine-tuning strategies that enhance the detectability of hallucinations in the resulting model. Focusing on semantic-entropy-based detection, we observe that many erroneous outputs remain undetected because the model produces nearly identical incorrect answers across multiple runs. To address this, we propose diversity-oriented fine-tuning to encourage more varied generations. We introduce two specific strategies: one based on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and the other on Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Extensive experiments are conducted to evaluate our approach and analyze the behavior of the models before and after fine-tuning. We find that after adopting our fine-tuning methods, the models become less likely to produce low semantic entropy responses for hallucinated answers, thereby improving the effectiveness of hallucination detection, eventually yielding results better than or comparable with state of the art methods. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [82] [Zero Hallucination, by Construction: Hallucination-Aware Layered Oversight for Trustworthy Enterprise AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17883)
*Bogdan Raduta, Horia Velicu, Alexandru Preda, Serban Chiricescu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO（幻觉感知分层监督）架构，通过六层防御机制（基于检索的接地生成、约束确定性执行、多信号验证、校准弃权、完全可追溯性、持续监督）将幻觉视为可控制的故障模式而非可消除的属性，在受监管的理赔提取工作负载上验证了该架构的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业无法信任会幻觉的AI代理，而幻觉是大语言模型固有的构造性缺陷——模型本质上就是生成未受支持文本的机器，规模扩展无法消除这一可能性。现有方法（如事后真实性判断、检索增强生成）只能捕获部分错误，无法实现零幻觉。因此需要重新定义目标：零幻觉不是模型属性，而是系统强制执行的属性。

Method: 提出HALO架构，包含六层防御：(1) 接地生成——在检索并审批过的内容上生成；(2) 约束确定性执行——限制模型可能出错的边界；(3) 多信号验证——使用LLM判断器和基于源文本的证据检查对每个输出进行接地性和幻觉评分；(4) 校准弃权——当接地性不足时系统拒绝回答而非猜测；(5) 完全可追溯性——记录每次检索、工具调用和生成；(6) 持续监督——检测漂移、阈值告警、闭环再生与统计验证。特别关注基于证据的置信度（验证提取内容与源文档的一致性，而非信任模型自报的置信度）。

Result: 在受监管的理赔提取工作负载上展示了HALO架构的有效性，通过多层防御机制实现了系统级的零幻觉保证。

Conclusion: 零幻觉不应被视为模型的内在属性，而应通过系统架构来强制执行。HALO通过将幻觉视为可控制的故障模式，而非可消除的缺陷，为企业级AI代理提供了可信赖的保障框架。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于大语言模型的幻觉问题，提出HALO架构通过多层防御机制实现零幻觉。这与多模态理解与生成方向下的[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）高度相关，该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性与可靠性提升。

Importance reason: Focuses on enterprise hallucination mitigation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Enterprise AI, hallucination as system property, layered oversight, claims extraction, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Enterprises will not deploy AI agents they cannot trust, and the most-cited reason for distrust is hallucination: confident, fluent output that is simply not true. The common response is to wait for a model that does not hallucinate. We argue that this is the wrong target. Large language models are, by construction, capable of generating unsupported text, and no amount of scale removes the possibility; a faithfulness judge bolted onto a raw model catches some errors but still ships others, and even well-curated retrieval pipelines have been shown to fabricate citations. We reframe the goal: "zero hallucination" is not a property a model possesses but a property a system enforces. We present HALO (Hallucination-Aware Layered Oversight), an assurance architecture which treats hallucination as a containable failure mode rather than an eliminable one. HALO composes six layers of defense: grounded generation over retrieved, approved content; constrained, deterministic execution that bounds where the model can err; multi-signal verification that scores every output for groundedness and hallucination using both an LLM judge and evidence-based checks against the source text; calibrated abstention, so the system declines rather than guesses when grounding is insufficient; total traceability of every retrieval, tool call, and generation; and continuous oversight that detects drift, alerts on threshold breaches, and closes the loop by regenerating and statistically validating improved agents. We detail each layer, give particular attention to evidence-based confidence (which verifies extractions against the source document rather than trusting the model's self-reported certainty), and illustrate the architecture on a regulated claims-extraction workload

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态系统在检测否定等高层语义概念时表现不佳，但根本原因尚不明确。作者希望从表征学习的角度分析VLM潜空间中否定信号的存在性，并据此设计更有效的跨模态否定检测方法。

Method: 1) 对标准VLM（如CLIP）的潜空间进行线性/非线性可分性分析，证明否定信号不可分；2) 提出跨模态注意力架构（Cross-Modal Attention），显式建模视觉与文本模态间的依赖关系；3) 利用Qwen2.5-VL自动标注3,222个政治视频-文本对的否定标签；4) 结合JEPA2自监督视频表征进行时序否定建模。

Result: 所提跨模态注意力方法相比单模态基线在F1上提升最高达+7.03%。统计分析验证了视觉否定在语义上依赖语言上下文的关键不对称性。

Conclusion: 标准VLM的潜空间缺乏可泛化的否定信号，需要显式的跨模态依赖建模来弥补这一缺陷。该工作为多模态系统中学习鲁棒、语义对齐的表征提供了新方法和新见解。

Classification reason: 该论文研究跨模态否定检测（negation detection），核心问题是多模态系统中语义概念（否定）的表示可靠性问题，属于多模态理解中关于语义忠实性与可靠性的范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）涵盖事实性、可靠性、忠实性提升，与本论文的否定信号缺失、语义对齐问题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on negation detection in VLMs, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: negation detection, cross-modal attention, VLM latent space analysis, political video-text dataset, no continuous token or generation focus

Abstract: Detecting high-level semantic concepts like negation across modalities remains a challenge for current multimodal systems. We analyze this as a fundamental representation learning problem, providing the first evidence that negation does not form a linearly or non-linearly separable class in the latent spaces of standard vision-language models (VLMs). We demonstrate that pretrained embeddings primarily encode modality-specific features, lacking a generalizable negation signal. To overcome this, we propose a novel cross-modal attention architecture that explicitly models inter-modal dependencies, achieving performance gains of up to +7.03% F1 over unimodal baselines. Our analysis reveals a key asymmetry: while textual negation often appears independently, visual negation is semantically dependent on linguistic context, a finding validated through our statistical analysis of 3,222 political video-text pairs automatically annotated via \textsc{Qwen2.5-VL}. By combining this analysis with self-supervised video representations (JEPA2), we advance the modeling of temporal negation. This work provides new methods and insights for learning robust, semantically-aligned representations in multimodal systems.

</details>

### [84] [Rater State Bias in RLHF Preference Data: An Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16195)
*Elena Kopteva, Vitaliy Hlynianyi-Zhuk*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

TL;DR: 本文识别了 RLHF 偏好数据中一种结构性的混淆因素：标注者的情绪/压力状态会随时间变化，导致偏好标签不仅反映输出质量，还编码了标注者状态。这种偏差不同于随机噪声，可能通过奖励建模和策略优化传播。论文提出了标注者状态偏移的定义、可测量的响应模式、五个可证伪预测以及审计框架和试点研究计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLHF 研究主要关注标注者之间的分歧或随机标签噪声，但忽略了标注者自身状态（如压力、情绪）随时间变化对偏好标签的系统性影响。这种结构化偏差可能被聚合过程保留并传播到奖励信号中，影响模型对齐效果，因此需要系统性地识别和审计这一偏差来源。

Method: 本文提出了一个理论框架和审计协议：(1) 定义了 rater state shift（标注者状态偏移）、rater state confound（标注者状态混淆）和 correlated rater state bias（相关标注者状态偏差）三个概念；(2) 定义了 survival level emotional authenticity（生存级情绪真实性）作为可测量的响应模式，利用词汇、语用、话语和安全相关特征进行检测；(3) 推导了五个可证伪预测及效应量阈值；(4) 设计了审计协议和试点研究计划，可应用于公开的指令微调模型。

Result: 本文是理论性工作，尚未进行实证验证。主要产出包括：对 RLHF 偏好数据中标注者状态偏差的形式化定义、五个可证伪预测（如：在高压标注时段，偏好分布会系统性偏移；标注者状态特征与偏好标签之间存在统计显著的相关性等）、效应量阈值建议，以及一个完整的审计协议和试点研究计划。

Conclusion: 标注者状态偏移是 RLHF 偏好数据中一种合理且可测试的结构化偏差来源，值得社区关注。该偏差不同于随机噪声，可能通过奖励建模影响策略优化。本文提供的审计框架为后续实证研究奠定了基础，有助于提升 RLHF 数据的质量和对齐系统的可靠性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 RLHF 偏好数据中的标注者状态偏差问题，属于 RLHF/偏好学习/对齐的核心议题，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper addresses RLHF bias from rater state, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: RLHF bias, rater state shift, audit framework, preference data, structured confound

Abstract: We identify a structured confound in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). Pairwise preference labels are intended to reflect the compared outputs, but they may also reflect the rater's state during annotation. Under sustained stressful or distressing conditions, raters' preferences may shift over time. As a result, preference data can encode rater state alongside judgments about response quality. These shifts differ from ordinary disagreement or random label noise. They are state dependent, can be shared across annotators working under similar conditions, and can propagate through reward modeling and policy optimization. We therefore propose rater state shift as a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data. This paper develops a hypothesis and an audit framework for studying this source of bias. We define rater state shift, rater state confound, and correlated rater state bias. We also define survival level emotional authenticity as a measurable response pattern using lexical, pragmatic, discourse, and safety related features. We analyze how correlated rater state bias can survive aggregation and enter learned reward signals. We derive five falsifiable predictions and effect size thresholds for an initial audit. Finally, we present an audit protocol and pilot study plan that can be applied to publicly available instruction tuned models. We do not infer the training history of any specific deployed model. Our goal is to isolate a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data.

</details>


### [85] [Group Entropy-Controlled Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16850)
*Guangran Cheng, Chengqi Lyu, Songyang Gao, Wenwei Zhang, Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

TL;DR: 本文提出GEPO（Group Entropy-Controlled Policy Optimization），一种轻量级的GRPO扩展方法，通过组级熵估计进行熵条件化的非对称优势塑造，解决异构任务混合训练中全局/词元级熵调控不足以及GRPO归一化优势导致的熵依赖偏差问题，在数学、物理、科学、代码生成和指令跟随等13个基准上一致优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO等方法在异构任务混合训练时，不同任务在相同策略下会呈现不同的熵状态，全局或词元级的熵调控无法满足异构探索需求；同时GRPO的组内优势归一化在不同熵组间产生统计不可比的偏差。需要一种能够感知组级熵差异的自适应优势调控方法。

Method: GEPO利用GRPO已有的分组样本估计组级熵，基于组熵进行条件化的非对称优势塑造：在低熵组中衰减正优势以减少过度利用，在高熵组中衰减负优势以保留探索空间；自适应阈值从历史熵统计中推导得出，无需额外计算开销。

Result: 在两种基础模型上，覆盖数学、物理、科学、代码生成和指令跟随等13个基准的实验中，GEPO一致优于GRPO及近期熵控制方法，实现了跨任务的均衡提升，同时在整个训练过程中保持了任务特定的探索水平。

Conclusion: GEPO通过组级熵感知的优势塑造有效解决了异构任务混合训练中的熵调控问题，是一种轻量且有效的GRPO改进方案，为LLM对齐中的探索-利用平衡提供了更精细化的控制手段。

Classification reason: 该论文提出GEPO方法，针对GRPO在异构任务上的熵控制问题进行改进，属于LLM强化学习对齐方法。虽然论文主要聚焦于语言模型，但其方法（GRPO的扩展）与多模态理解与生成方向下的"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度相关，该子主题涵盖RLHF、DPO、偏好学习等对齐方法，GEPO作为GRPO的改进属于该范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL entropy control for LLM alignment, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: GRPO extension, entropy-conditioned advantage shaping, heterogeneous task training, LLM alignment, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Entropy control has become an effective tool in reinforcement learning (RL) of large language models (LLMs), helping balance exploration-exploitation trade-off during alignment process. Such RL paradigm is often conducted on mixtures of heterogeneous tasks, which induce distinct entropy regimes under the same policy, making global or token-level entropy regulation insufficient to corresponding heterogeneous needs of exploration. This heterogeneity further makes GRPO-style normalized advantages induce an entropy-dependent bias, making advantage signals across prompt groups statistically non-comparable. To address this issue, we propose Group Entropy-Controlled Policy Optimization (GEPO), a lightweight extension to GRPO that uses group entropy, estimated from existing grouped samples to perform entropy-conditioned asymmetric advantage shaping. GEPO attenuates positive advantages in low-entropy groups to reduce over-exploitation, and negative advantages in high-entropy groups to preserve exploration, with adaptive thresholds derived from historical entropy statistics. Extensive experiments on two base models across thirteen benchmarks spanning mathematics, physics, science, code generation, and instruction following show that GEPO consistently outperforms GRPO and recent entropy-controlled methods, delivering balanced cross-task improvements while preserving task-specific exploration levels throughout training.

</details>


### [86] [Med-OPD: Improving Medical Vision-Language Models via Evidence-Aware On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16303)
*Yunhang Qian, Jiaquan Yu, Jiawei Liu, Meng Wang, Hongwei Bran Li, Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: Med-OPD 提出了一种证据感知的在线蒸馏框架，通过 Medical Evidence Advantage (MEA) 信号在 token 和轨迹级别重分配蒸馏权重，增强医学 VLM 对关键视觉证据的依赖，提升多模态医学推理的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学 VLM 可能依赖语言先验或医学模板生成看似合理的临床答案，而非真正关注诊断关键区域。标准在线蒸馏（OPD）均匀处理所有 token，导致稀疏的证据依赖 token 被大量临床叙述 token 稀释。因此需要一种能区分证据依赖 token 与叙述 token 的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出 Med-OPD 框架，核心是 Medical Evidence Advantage (MEA)：通过比较原始图像和证据退化图像下教师模型的似然差异，衡量每个 token 对医学视觉证据的依赖程度。基于 MEA，在 token 级别和轨迹级别重新分配蒸馏信号，强调诊断关键 token 和证据依赖的 rollout。

Result: 在 OmniMedVQA 子集上的实验表明，Med-OPD 在 CT、MRI、疾病诊断和病变分级等任务上一致优于 SFT 和标准 OPD 方法。

Conclusion: 证据感知的在线蒸馏能够有效增强医学 VLM 对关键视觉证据的依赖，提升可靠的多模态医学推理能力，为 Med-VLM 的后训练提供了统一框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Med-OPD，一种基于证据感知的在线蒸馏（On-Policy Distillation）方法，用于改进医学视觉语言模型（Med-VLM）的视觉证据依赖性和推理可靠性。核心方法涉及教师模型对学生生成轨迹的 token 级和轨迹级蒸馏信号重分配，属于多模态模型的对齐与偏好优化范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM distillation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Medical VLM, On-policy distillation, Evidence-aware, Token-level supervision, Multimodal reasoning

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) require reliable reasoning from fine-grained visual evidence, yet existing models can produce plausible clinical answers by relying on language priors or medical templates rather than truly attending to diagnosis-critical regions. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers dense token-level supervision on student-generated trajectories and provides a privacy-compatible means of capability transfer without requiring the redistribution of raw patient data. However, standard OPD uniformly distills all tokens, causing sparse evidence-dependent tokens to be diluted by abundant clinical narrative tokens. Inspired by the success of OPD in the large language model community, we propose \textbf{Med-OPD}, to our knowledge the first unified post-training framework that integrates on-policy distillation with medical evidence-aware supervision for Med-VLMs. We introduce \textbf{Medical Evidence Advantage} (MEA), a teacher-grounded counterfactual signal that uses an answer-aware hint to focus teacher scoring on evidence supporting the target diagnosis, and measures each token's dependence on medical visual evidence by comparing teacher likelihoods under the original and evidence-degraded imaging modalities. Based on MEA, Med-OPD redistributes the distillation signal at both the token and trajectory levels, emphasizing diagnosis-critical tokens and evidence-reliant rollouts. Experiments on OmniMedVQA subsets show that Med-OPD consistently outperforms SFT and standard OPD across CT, MRI, Disease Diagnosis, and Lesion Grading. These results demonstrate that evidence-aware distillation can better strengthen medical VLMs' reliance on key visual evidence and improve reliable multimodal medical reasoning. The source code and data is publicly available at:

</details>


### [87] [CADENCE: Closing the Reasoning Gap via Coverage-Adaptive On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16955)
*Satyam Kumar, Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (83.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CADENCE 提出了一种覆盖自适应的策略蒸馏框架，通过 DRIFT 机制（逐 token 前向/反向 KL 混合代理目标）及六个扩展组件（COVA、FTB、CCD、LAP、EMR、BSD），解决了冷启动崩溃、状态无关调度和二元奖励稀疏三个问题。在 GSM8K 和 MATH-500 上，将 0.5B 学生模型从 1.5B 教师蒸馏至 69.8% 准确率，在单台 Apple Mac Studio 上即可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略蒸馏方法存在三个复合失效模式：(1) 冷启动崩溃——新初始化的学生模型对教师偏好的 token 赋予接近零的概率；(2) 状态无关的散度调度——仅依赖时间步的前向/反向 KL 插值，忽略了学生模型的覆盖状态；(3) 二元奖励稀疏——pass/fail 信号丢弃了部分正确轨迹中的有用信息。本文旨在统一解决这些问题。

Method: 提出 CADENCE 框架，核心是 DRIFT 机制——在学生采样轨迹上使用逐 token 的前向 KL 和反向 KL 凸混合代理目标（而非序列级 KL 梯度估计器）。六个扩展组件：(A) COVA：覆盖自适应 β 调度，加速前向到反向 KL 的过渡；(B) FTB：分叉 token 增强，通过全局归一化熵参考在高熵位置集中梯度；(C) CCD：密集奖励，为不正确但接近的轨迹提供数值邻近部分信用；(D) LAP：简洁偏好正确轨迹强化；(E) EMR：熵匹配校准正则化；(F) BSD：自举自蒸馏阶段。

Result: 在 GSM8K 和 MATH-500 上（修正的 512 token 协议，5 个随机种子，报告标准差），CADENCE 将 0.5B 学生从 1.5B 教师蒸馏到 69.8±0.5% GSM8K pass@1（预训练基线 48.7%，关闭了 63.2% 的教师差距）；从 3B 教师蒸馏到 72.1±0.4%（关闭 76.2% 差距），超过最强匹配计算量的标签使用基线（DRIFT+二元奖励）4.4±0.7 个百分点。所有实验在单台 Apple Mac Studio（M 系列，64GB 统一内存）上运行。

Conclusion: CADENCE 证明了原则性的蒸馏方法可以在不依赖数据中心级硬件的情况下达到强推理质量。覆盖自适应的调度、密集奖励和自蒸馏等设计有效弥补了策略蒸馏中的关键缺陷，为资源受限场景下的推理模型蒸馏提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于策略的蒸馏（on-policy distillation）方法，通过改进KL散度调度、密集奖励和自蒸馏等机制，将大模型推理能力迁移至小模型。这属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与偏好优化范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度相关，因为论文核心涉及奖励设计、策略优化和蒸馏对齐。

Importance reason: Strong distillation method but not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models or video generation. Signals: On-policy distillation, cold-start collapse fix, DRIFT per-token KL mixture, GSM8K/MATH-500 gains, single Mac Studio run

Abstract: On-policy knowledge distillation transfers reasoning from large teachers to compact students, but existing approaches suffer three compounding failure modes: (i) cold-start collapse, where a fresh student assigns near-zero mass to teacher-preferred tokens; (ii) state-agnostic divergence scheduling, where time-only forward/reverse-KL interpolation ignores the student's coverage state; and (iii) binary reward sparsity, where pass/fail signals discard information from partially correct traces. We present CADENCE, a unified framework with a targeted fix for each. Its DRIFT mechanism schedules a per-token convex mixture of forward-KL and reverse-KL surrogate objectives on student-sampled trajectories (per-token surrogates, not sequence-level KL gradient estimators). Six components extend it: (A) COVA, a coverage-adaptive $\beta$ schedule accelerating the forward-to-reverse transition; (B) FTB, a forking-token boost concentrating gradient at high-entropy positions via a globally-normalized entropy reference; (C) CCD, a dense reward adding numerical-proximity partial credit for incorrect-but-close traces; (D) LAP, brevity-preferential correct-rollout reinforcement; (E) EMR, an entropy-matching calibration regularizer; (F) BSD, a bootstrapped self-distillation phase. On GSM8K and MATH-500 (corrected 512-token protocol, 5 seeds, reported std), CADENCE distills a 0.5B student from a 1.5B teacher to 69.8 $\pm$ 0.5% GSM8K pass@1 (from 48.7% pretrained; 63.2% of the teacher gap closed) and to 72.1 $\pm$ 0.4% with a 3B teacher (76.2% closed), beating the strongest matched-compute label-using baseline (DRIFT+binary reward) by +4.4 $\pm$ 0.7 points. All experiments run on a single Apple Mac Studio (M-series, 64GB unified memory), showing principled distillation reaches strong reasoning quality without datacenter-scale hardware.

</details>


### [88] [Can Experts Adapt Without Training? On Test-Time Modality Generalization in MVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16726)
*Raza Imam, Darakshan Rashid, Yutong Xie, Dwarikanath Mahapatra, Brejesh Lall, Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (51.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MoBE（Mixture-of-Bayesian-Experts），一种完全无需优化的测试时模态泛化框架，通过熵引导的动态路由和贝叶斯专家自适应，使医学视觉语言模型在未见模态和域上实现鲁棒泛化，无需梯度更新即可在线调整专家置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型（MVLM）在遇到未见过的模态和域时性能急剧下降，而临床场景对鲁棒性要求极高。现有测试时适应（TTA）方法需要优化更新，计算成本高且不适用于资源受限的医疗场景。作者希望探索能否在不进行任何训练/优化的情况下，通过 Mixture-of-Experts 的路由与自适应机制实现测试时的模态泛化。

Method: 提出 MoBE（Mixture-of-Bayesian-Experts）框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 熵引导的动态路由（Entropy-guided Dynamic Routing），在测试时根据输入模态的不确定性动态选择最合适的专家；(2) 专家级贝叶斯自适应（Expert-wise Bayesian Adaptation），利用在线统计信息更新专家置信度，无需梯度更新。整个框架完全无需优化，直接增强静态 MVLM 的测试时泛化能力。

Result: 在医学多模态基准上，MoBE 在已见模态、未见模态和异质模态上分别比最先进的 TTA 方法平均提升 +4.72、+7.17 和 +4.3 的准确率，证明了无需训练的专家自适应在模态泛化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文验证了在测试时无需参数更新即可实现有效的模态泛化，MoBE 通过熵引导路由和贝叶斯自适应解决了专家混合中的"专业化-泛化"困境，为资源受限的医疗多模态场景提供了一种轻量级、高效的鲁棒性增强方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态视觉语言模型（MVLM）在测试时的模态泛化问题，提出无需训练的专家路由与自适应方法（MoBE），属于多模态模型在测试时的对齐与自适应优化范畴，与 canonical 子主题"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的测试时适应、模型鲁棒性方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical domain and test-time adaptation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Test-time modality generalization, Mixture-of-Bayesian-Experts, No gradient updates, Medical vision-language models, Entropy-guided routing

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (MVLMs) promise broad zero-shot generalization, yet their reliability collapses when confronted with unseen modalities and domains, precisely where clinical robustness matters most. To address this gap, we revisit test-time modality generalization from the perspective of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and ask: can experts route-and-adapt without any optimization during inference? We identify a fundamental specialization-generalization dilemma at test time, where blindly aggregating modality experts dilutes modality-specific knowledge, while selecting one highly confident expert risks mismatch under shift. To address this, we propose MoBE: a fully optimization-free framework that performs dynamic expert selection and adaptation at test time. MoBE combines entropy-guided dynamic routing in MoE settings with expert-wise Bayesian adaptation, enabling experts to update their confidence and adapt online without gradient updates. Without parametric updates, MoBE augments a static MVLM with test-time routing and online statistics, achieving average accuracy gains of +4.72, +7.17, and +4.3 over state-of-the-art TTA methods across seen, unseen, and heterogeneous medical benchmarks, highlighting the effectiveness of training-free expert adaptation for robust modality generalization.

</details>


### [89] [PRiSM: Prototype Regularization for Few-Shot VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17820)
*Ghassen Baklouti, Omprakash Chakraborty, Jose Dolz, Ismail Ben Ayed*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PRiSM，一种用于少样本VLM的原型正则化方法。针对现有少样本适应基准中类别平衡等不现实假设，作者引入基于Dirichlet采样的更真实基准，发现现有方法在类别不平衡和高类别数下性能显著下降。PRiSM通过最大化类间原型距离、支持特征对齐和保真度正则化的多目标损失，并采用基于块Majorize-Minimize的高效优化器，显著提升了多种无训练少样本基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本VLM适应方法在标准基准上表现良好，但这些基准假设适应数据类别平衡，与真实场景不符。作者发现当引入类别不平衡和更多类别时，现有方法性能大幅下降，甚至随着标注样本增多反而更差。因此需要一种更鲁棒的少样本适应方法。

Method: 提出PRiSM框架，包含：(1) 多目标损失函数，包括最大化类间原型距离的正则项、支持特征与原型对齐项、以及保真于基线原型的项；(2) 基于块Majorize-Minimize（MM）的高效优化器，通过Gershgorin圆盘定理推导出块级Lipschitz常数（Hessian谱范数上界），实现高效优化。

Result: 在多种VLM（CLIP等）和多个数据集上，PRiSM显著提升了多种无训练少样本基线方法的性能，尤其在严重类别不平衡和高类别数场景下增益明显。实验验证了各损失项和优化器的有效性。

Conclusion: PRiSM作为一种即插即用的原型正则化模块，能有效提升VLM在真实少样本场景（类别不平衡、多类别）下的适应性能，且计算高效。该工作揭示了现有少样本VLM基准的不现实假设，并为更鲁棒的少样本适应提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在少样本场景下的原型正则化方法，属于多模态对齐与适应范畴。虽然不直接涉及RLHF/DPO，但论文关注VLM的少样本适应、类别不平衡下的性能提升，与多模态对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）中偏好学习、适应优化的主题高度相关，且该[CANONICAL]条目是现有最匹配的宽泛主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on few-shot VLM adaptation with class imbalance, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong overlap with user's key interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Few-shot VLM, prototype regularization, class imbalance benchmark, Dirichlet sampling, block Majorize-Minimize optimizer

Abstract: Training-free few-shot adaptation methods have gained significant attention recently in the context of Vision-language Models (VLMs). Yet, current benchmarks rely on strong assumptions about the statistics of the adaptation data, e.g., class balance. We question these simplifying assumptions and introduce a more realistic benchmark that varies both the levels of class balance and the effective number of classes in few-shot tasks via Dirichlet sampling. Surprisingly, under our setting, we observe substantial drops in the performances of state-of-the-art methods, more so when the number of labeled samples increases. To mitigate this, we introduce PRiSM, a class-prototype regularization that can be deployed as a plug and play module on top of any existing baseline method, significantly improving performances. Our method optimizes a novel multi-term loss, which includes a regularizer maximizing inter-class pairwise distances, along with additional terms promoting support-feature alignment and fidelity to the baseline prototypes. Furthermore, we introduce an effective and computationally efficient block Majorize-Minimize optimizer for our objective. More specifically, we derive a valid blockwise Lipschitz constant (i.e., a bound on the Hessian's spectral norm), which can be computed efficiently via the Gershgorin circle theorem. Extensive experiments show that PRiSM improves several training-free baselines, with large gains when dealing with severe class imbalance and high numbers of classes.

</details>
