### [90] [Though Language Models Err While They Strive: Conformal Prediction for Self-Correcting Scientific Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16704)
*Mingqiao Mo, Yunlong Tan, Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Scientific Feasibility Control (SFC)，一种基于图结构共形预测的框架，通过渐进式绝对-一致-事实性验证为科学推理提供统计保证，在 PhyX 物理推理等基准上超越 DeepSeek-R1 和 GPT-4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成科学内容时经常违反基本科学原理，且早期错误会级联污染后续推理步骤。现有方法缺乏对科学推理有效性的形式化统计保证。

Method: 将科学推理分解为原子化的绝对-一致-事实性单元，构建近似可推导性图来建模逻辑依赖关系，通过实时验证和动态分支（检测到科学违规时切换到替代生成路径）实现共形预测框架 SFC。

Result: 在 PhyX 物理推理上达到 50.1% 准确率，超越 DeepSeek-R1 (49.8%) 和 GPT-4 (45.8%)；在 α=0.10 置信水平下提供 91.7% 的科学有效性保证；跨多种模型架构减少 73% 的科学定律违反。

Conclusion: SFC 框架能有效为科学推理提供形式化的统计保证，显著减少科学错误，且具有跨模型架构的泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SFC 框架，利用共形预测对科学推理（含多模态物理推理 PhyX、数学推理等）提供统计保证，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴，该 canonical 子主题涵盖科学推理与数学推理。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel conformal prediction framework for scientific reasoning validity, but is not directly aligned with your priority subtopics on continuous models or generation. Signals: conformal prediction, scientific reasoning, self-correction, statistical guarantees, PhyX benchmark

Abstract: Large language models frequently violate fundamental scientific principles when generating technical content, undermining their reliability in scientific applications. We introduce Scientific Feasibility Control SFC, a graph-structured conformal prediction framework that provides statistical guarantees for scientific reasoning validity through progressive absolute-coherent-factuality validation. Our approach decomposes scientific reasoning into atomic absolute-coherent-factuality units requiring both individual correctness against physical laws and logical substantiation from preceding context, addressing the cascade effect where early scientific errors contaminate subsequent reasoning steps. Unlike independence-based methods that treat claims in isolation, SFC models logical dependencies as approximate deducibility graphs and operates through real-time validation with dynamic branching when scientific violations are detected, the system branches to alternative generation paths using verified context as foundation. We demonstrate SFC across established scientific reasoning benchmarks including PhyX multimodal physics, MATH, ScienceQA, and ARC Challenge, achieving 50.1 percent accuracy on PhyX physics reasoning, substantially outperforming recent reasoning models including DeepSeek-R1 49.8 percent and GPT-4 45.8 percent while providing 91.7 percent scientific validity with formal conformal coverage guarantees at alpha equals 0.10 confidence level and reducing scientific law violations by 73 percent across multiple model architectures.

</details>


### [91] [Constraint-Anchored Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16727)
*Zehua Cheng, Wei Dai, Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CART（Constraint-Anchored Reasoning Traces），一种神经符号框架，通过在多模态思维链推理中插入可机器检查的符号约束断言，并利用约束传播模块实时检测矛盾、触发回溯，从而有效缓解 MLLM 推理中的错误级联（snowballing）问题。在五个基准上，CART 将雪球率从 0.65 降至 0.14，GQA 准确率提升 4.6 个百分点，POPE 达到 89.1 F1，推理开销不超过 18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归多模态大语言模型在思维链推理中存在严重的错误级联问题：早期的一个错误推理会污染后续所有推理步骤。现有方法（如多次采样、事后自验证、完整程序合成）要么缺乏符号基础，要么发现错误太晚，要么牺牲了自然语言推理的灵活性。因此需要一种能在推理过程中实时检测并阻止错误传播的机制。

Method: CART 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 约束锚定推理轨迹——训练 MLLM 在自然语言推理步骤中穿插轻量级符号约束断言（如 count(red_objects)=3）；(2) 双管齐下的约束传播模块——结合学习的神经接地头（neural grounding head）与布尔约束传播（BCP），持续验证锚定约束与提取的视觉特征是否一致并检查逻辑一致性；(3) 回溯控制器——检测到矛盾时暂停生成并回退到最后一个一致检查点。此外，变频率发射机制允许模型自适应控制锚定密度，避免轨迹膨胀。通过增强 GQA、CLEVR-CoGenT 和 VCR 数据集构建 218K 训练实例，使用 LoRA 微调 LLaVA-NeXT 和 Qwen2-VL。

Result: 在五个基准上，CART 将雪球率从 0.65 降至 0.14，GQA 准确率相比仅训练基线提升 4.6 个百分点，POPE-all 上达到 89.1 F1，推理开销最多仅增加 18%。

Conclusion: CART 通过神经符号融合的方式，在保持自然语言推理灵活性的同时引入了符号约束的可验证性，有效缓解了 MLLM 推理中的错误级联问题。该方法在多个基准上取得了显著改进，且推理开销可控，为提升多模态推理的可靠性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于多模态大语言模型在思维链推理中的错误级联问题，提出约束锚定推理轨迹（CART）这一神经符号框架来缓解推理错误传播。核心贡献属于多模态推理与思维链（CoT）范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "multimodal_reasoning_cot" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses error snowballing in MLLM reasoning with a neuro-symbolic framework, highly relevant to multimodal understanding and reasoning. Signals: multimodal reasoning, chain-of-thought, error mitigation, neuro-symbolic, constraint propagation

Abstract: Autoregressive multimodal large language models (MLLMs) suffer from error snowballing: a single incorrect inference early in a chainof-thought (CoT) trace corrupts all downstream reasoning. We find that in state-of-the-art open-source MLLMs, once the first error occurs, the reasoning cascades into failure across all remaining steps in 65% of such cases (a metric we term the snowball rate). Existing mitigations-sampling multiple chains, post-hoc self-verification, or full program synthesis-either lack symbolic grounding, catch errors too late, or sacrifice the flexibility of natural language reasoning. We propose Constraint-Anchored Reasoning Traces (CART), a neuro-symbolic framework that trains MLLMs to interleave natural language reasoning steps with symbolic constraint assertions: lightweight, machine-checkable statements about visual content (e.g., count(red_objects) = 3). A dual-pronged Constraint Propagation Module-combining a learned neural grounding head with Boolean Constraint Propagation-continuously verifies these anchors against extracted visual features and checks their mutual logical consistency. When a contradiction is detected, a backtrack controller halts generation and reverts to the last consistent checkpoint, preventing error propagation. A variable-frequency emission mechanism allows the model to adaptively control anchor density, avoiding trace bloat. We construct 218K training instances by augmenting GQA, CLEVR-CoGenT, and VCR with ground-truth constraint annotations derived from scene graphs, and fine-tune open-source MLLMs (LLaVA-NeXT, Qwen2-VL) via LoRA. On five benchmarks, CART reduces the snowball rate from 0.65 to 0.14, improves GQA accuracy by +4.6 percentage points over trainingonly baselines, and achieves 89.1 F1 on POPE-all with at most 18% inference overhead.

</details>


### [92] [FUSAR-R1: A Large-Scale Reasoning Model for Intelligent Interpretation of SAR Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16819)
*Yi Yang, Xiaokun Zhang, Yuxuan Li, Ruyi Zhang, Xinpeng Zhou, Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (74.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FUSAR-R1，一个面向 SAR 图像智能解译的大规模推理模型。通过模拟人类专家解译过程构建显式思维链推理数据指导指令学习，再引入强化学习优化输出，使模型具备逐步分析、逻辑判断和自我纠错能力。实验表明，FUSAR-R1 在目标检测、目标计数与分类、地物类别识别等 SAR 解译任务上持续优于现有多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SAR 视觉语言模型受相干成像机制、复杂散射特性、散斑噪声干扰等因素影响，不具备人类专家的逐步分析、逻辑判断和自我纠错能力，难以在复杂场景下支持可靠的智能解译。

Method: 1) 通过模拟人类专家解译过程构建显式思维链推理数据；2) 利用该数据指导指令学习，赋予模型基础推理能力；3) 引入强化学习策略，基于推理结果优化模型输出，实现自我纠错和更可靠的推理。

Result: 在目标检测、目标计数与分类、地物类别识别等多种 SAR 解译任务上，FUSAR-R1 持续优于现有多模态大模型。

Conclusion: 通过思维链推理数据构建与强化学习优化相结合的方法，能够有效提升 SAR 图像解译模型的推理能力和可靠性，为 SAR 智能解译提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SAR 图像智能解译的大规模推理模型 FUSAR-R1，通过构建显式思维链推理数据指导指令学习，并引入强化学习优化推理结果，实现逐步分析、逻辑判断与自我纠错。这属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴，因此复用该 canonical 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on remote sensing SAR interpretation, which is a negative keyword, and does not align with priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: remote sensing, SAR image interpretation, chain-of-thought reasoning, reinforcement learning, multimodal large language model

Abstract: In recent years, large-scale vision-language models have been driving a paradigm shift in intelligent remote sensing image interpretation. By incorporating textual semantic information, the cognitive expression, semantic understanding, and human-computer interaction capabilities of interpretation models have been significantly improved, achieving initial progress in the field of Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) image interpretation. However, SAR images are affected by factors such as coherent imaging mechanisms, complex scattering characteristics, speckle noise interference, and target-background coupling, resulting in complex and variable image features with significant uncertainties and specializations. Existing SAR vision-language models do not yet possess the step-by-step analysis, logical judgment, and self-correction capabilities of human experts, making it difficult to support reliable intelligent interpretation in complex scenarios. To address this issue, this paper proposes a large-scale reasoning model, FUSAR-R1, for intelligent interpretation of SAR images. The model first constructs explicit chain-of-thought reasoning data by simulating the interpretation process of human experts and uses this data to guide instruction learning, thereby endowing the model with basic reasoning capabilities. Subsequently, a reinforcement learning strategy is introduced to optimize the model's outputs based on inference results, enabling self-correction and more reliable reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that FUSAR-R1 consistently outperforms existing multimodal large-scale models across various SAR interpretation tasks, including target detection, target counting and classification, and land-cover category recognition.

</details>


### [93] [WeedExpert-R1: Incentivizing Botanical Reasoning in MLLMs with Reinforcement Learning for Precision Weed Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16492)
*Zonglin Yang, Wei-Zhen Liang, Nevin Lawrence, Xin Qiao, Benjamin Riggan, Chi-En Chiang, Fuchen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (70.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WeedExpert-R1 提出了一种结合领域特定 Chain-of-Thought 合成管道与 GRPO 强化学习的多模态大模型方法，用于精细杂草物种识别与实例级定位。在 37 个杂草物种、6 个数据集上，4B 参数模型以 75.82% 的精确集精度超越 GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Pro 等闭源模型及 Qwen3-VL-30B 等更大开源模型，并展现出开放词汇的跨区域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标检测器使用封闭词汇集，难以跨区域部署，且无法在复杂农业场景中解释其预测。MLLM 虽具备视觉定位与推理能力，但缺乏足够的植物学知识，导致细粒度杂草识别中出现幻觉。因此需要一种能学习视觉定位的植物学推理的多模态模型。

Method: 提出 WeedExpert-R1 框架：(1) 构建领域特定的 Chain-of-Thought 合成管道，结合人工整理的植物学特征词典与 Auditor-Synthesizer LLM 工作流，生成推理数据用于监督微调；(2) 应用 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，对格式、准确性、实例数量和响应长度设置奖励信号进行强化学习训练。

Result: 在 37 个杂草物种、6 个数据集上，WeedExpert-R1-4B 在 IoU=0.5 阈值下达到 75.82% 精确集精度、89.30% 精确率、87.81% 召回率，超越 GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Pro 等闭源模型及 Qwen3-VL-30B-Instruct、Gemma-4-31B-it 等更大开源模型。在未见物种上展现出开放词汇能力。

Conclusion: WeedExpert-R1 证明了通过领域特定 CoT 数据合成与 GRPO 强化学习相结合，可以有效提升 MLLM 在细粒度农业视觉定位任务中的推理能力与泛化性能，为跨区域、跨作物的精准杂草控制提供了无需重新训练的开放词汇解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 MLLM 进行精细杂草识别与定位，通过领域特定的 Chain-of-Thought 合成管道生成推理数据，并用 GRPO 强化学习训练模型进行视觉定位推理。核心贡献在于多模态推理（CoT + 强化学习）在农业视觉定位任务上的应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Focuses on agricultural domain-specific MLLM reasoning, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: domain-specific MLLM, reinforcement learning for grounding, agricultural application, closed-vocabulary limitation, open-vocabulary generalization

Abstract: Precision weed control requires species-level identification and instance-level localization. However, conventional object detectors use a closed vocabulary, limiting their deployment across regions, and cannot explain their predictions in complex agricultural scenes. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer visual grounding and reasoning capabilities, but insufficient botanical knowledge can cause hallucinations in fine-grained weed identification. This study introduces WeedExpert-R1, a multimodal model that learns visually grounded botanical reasoning through verifiable rewards. A domain-specific Chain-of-Thought synthesis pipeline combines a human-curated botanical trait dictionary with an Auditor-Synthesizer LLM workflow to generate reasoning data for supervised fine-tuning. Group Relative Policy Optimization is then applied with rewards for format, accuracy, instance count, and response length. Across 37 weed species from six datasets, WeedExpert-R1-4B achieved 75.82 percent exact-set precision at an IoU threshold of 0.5, 89.30 percent precision, and 87.81 percent recall. It outperformed proprietary models, including GPT-5.4 and Gemini-3.1-Pro, and larger open-source models, including Qwen3-VL-30B-Instruct and Gemma-4-31B-it. Results on unseen species further demonstrate its open-vocabulary capability and potential for deployment across diverse regions and crops without retraining.

</details>


### [94] [Is Your Model Thinking or Just Stagnating? PUMA: Diagnosing Reasoning Pathology via Phase-Momentum Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17188)
*Cheng Yan, Guangyang Ye, Wuyang Zhang, Fan Xu, Zhijun Fan, Xiang Xia, Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PUMA框架，通过相位-动量对齐假设诊断大推理模型（LRM）在测试时扩展中的"过度思考"问题，利用几何认知努力和熵认知不确定性两个正交维度，实现自适应截断或纠正干预，在1.5B到32B模型上取得更优的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型通过长思维链进行测试时扩展时，常出现"过度思考"悖论——冗余推理增加计算开销却不保证准确性。现有方法（信息论方法易受"欺骗性收敛"影响，潜空间分析缺乏实时敏感性）无法有效区分主动探索与被动停滞。

Method: 提出相位-动量对齐假设，认为推理正确性取决于几何动量与不确定性消解之间的时间同步。构建认知能量模型，通过两个正交维度刻画推理动态：几何认知努力（潜速度与曲折度量化）和熵认知不确定性。PUMA框架采用分层诊断架构，结合轻量级相位监控与事件触发的几何分析，实现自适应截断或纠正干预。

Result: 在1.5B到32B规模的大推理模型上，PUMA在多个基准上持续优于现有最优方法，实现了更优的准确率-效率权衡和鲁棒的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: PUMA通过相位-动量对齐假设有效诊断了推理病理，为测试时计算效率优化提供了无需训练的新范式，能够区分主动探索与被动停滞，实现精准干预。

Classification reason: 论文研究大语言模型/推理模型在测试时计算中的"过度思考"问题，提出诊断推理病理的方法，属于推理时计算扩展与思维链效率优化，与多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Addresses reasoning efficiency in LRMs, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: reasoning pathology diagnosis, test-time scaling efficiency, phase-momentum alignment, cognitive-energy model, adaptive truncation

Abstract: Test-time scaling empowers Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) to tackle complex tasks via extensive Chain-of-Thought (CoT). However, this often induces the "overthinking" paradox, where redundant reasoning increases computational overhead without guaranteeing accuracy. Existing test-time efficiency optimization methods primarily fall into two categories: information-theoretic approaches, which are prone to "deceptive convergence" where low uncertainty masks hallucinations, and latent representation analyses, which are often post-hoc, lacking the real-time sensitivity for dynamic reasoning. To bridge this gap, we first posit the Phase-Momentum Alignment Hypothesis, asserting that reasoning correctness hinges on the temporal synchronization between geometric momentum and uncertainty resolution. We then theoretically formulate the Cognitive-Energy Model to characterize these dynamics through two orthogonal dimensions: Geometric Cognitive Effort, quantified by latent velocity and tortuosity, and Entropic Cognitive Uncertainty. To operationalize this, we introduce PUMA (Phase-Uncertainty Momentum Alignment), a training-free framework employing a tiered diagnostic architecture. By coupling lightweight phase monitoring with event-triggered geometric analysis, PUMA effectively distinguishes active exploration from passive stagnation, enabling precise interventions through adaptive truncation or corrective measures. Extensive experiments on LRMs spanning 1.5B to 32B demonstrate that PUMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off and robust cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [95] [LenGuard-GPC: Length Guarding with Guided-Prompt Consistency for Spatial Reasoning Reinforce Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17243)
*Xingjian Tao, Yiwei Wang, Yujun Cai, Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LenGuard-GPC 提出一种密集奖励框架，通过引导提示一致性（Guided-Prompt Consistency）的 KL 散度作为密集奖励信号，并结合分阶段长度奖励，在多视图空间推理任务上提升 GRPO 强化学习的准确率并缩短推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 GRPO 强化学习在多视图空间推理中仅提供稀疏的结果级奖励，无法指示推理轨迹中具体出错的位置，也无法控制推理长度。而 CoT 推理在此类任务中容易变得冗长却未必更准确，因此需要一种既能提供密集反馈又能控制推理长度的奖励机制。

Method: 提出 LenGuard-GPC 框架：(1) 对每个采样轨迹，比较标准提示和引导提示下的逐 token 预测分布，计算 token 级 KL 散度作为密集奖励信号；(2) 引入分阶段长度奖励（staged length bonus），将推理长度控制在合理范围内，避免单纯鼓励短回答而牺牲质量。

Result: 在六个多视图空间推理基准上，LenGuard-GPC 相比标准 GRPO 提升了准确率，同时降低了平均回答长度。

Conclusion: 通过密集奖励和长度控制相结合，可以有效提升多视图空间推理中强化学习的效果，为视觉推理任务中的奖励设计提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多视图空间推理（multi-view spatial reasoning），属于视觉推理范畴，并采用强化学习（GRPO）结合密集奖励来改进推理过程。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配，该子主题涵盖多模态推理、视觉推理及推理时计算扩展。

Importance reason: Focuses on spatial reasoning and RL reward design, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: spatial reasoning, reinforcement learning, dense reward, GRPO, length control

Abstract: Multi-view spatial reasoning requires vision-language models to compare visual evidence across images, align object correspondences, and infer spatial relations over long visual contexts, a setting where chain-of-thought reasoning tends to grow verbose without becoming more accurate. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is a natural fit for this task, but standard GRPO reward relies on sparse outcome-level feedback and gives no signal about where a reasoning trajectory goes wrong, nor any control over its length. We propose LenGuard-GPC, a dense reward framework that addresses both problems together. For each sampled trajectory, it compares the token-wise predictive distributions under a standard prompt and a guided prompt, and uses the resulting token-sum KL divergence as a dense reward signal. Since this KL penalty accumulates over tokens and would otherwise reward shorter responses regardless of their quality, we introduce a staged length bonus that keeps reasoning length within a controlled range without simply encouraging brevity. On six multi-view spatial reasoning benchmarks, LenGuard-GPC improves accuracy over vanilla GRPO while reducing average response length.

</details>


### [96] [ST-Veto: Spatio-Temporal Token Veto for Diffusion MLLMs via Taylor Prediction and Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17884)
*Keuntae Kim, Beomseok Lee, Hyunwoo Kim, Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-Veto，一种无需训练的时空Token否决方法，利用泰勒二阶预测和视觉注意力定位，在扩散多模态大模型的每一步推理中过滤不稳定和弱对齐的token，从而提升多模态推理准确率（最高提升9%），且不增加额外训练或生成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM使用CoT推理存在顺序生成成本高、错误累积和缺乏自纠正能力的问题。扩散MLLM虽然通过无序去噪实现了高效和迭代优化，但其推理机制及如何增强推理能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: ST-Veto利用扩散MLLM每一步可观测所有token位置的特点，提出两种否决机制：(1) 基于二阶泰勒预测的时序稳定性否决——预测token置信度的动态变化趋势，否决不稳定的token；(2) 基于图像注意力质量的视觉定位否决——通过注意力质量过滤弱对齐的token，并用更安全的候选token替换被否决的token。

Result: 在多个dMLLM和多模态推理基准上，ST-Veto一致优于标准解码策略和先前的VLM推理方法，准确率最高提升9%，且无需额外训练或生成成本。

Conclusion: ST-Veto通过引导生成走向更高置信度、更好对齐的路径，有效增强了扩散MLLM的推理能力，是一种轻量级且高效的训练-free推理增强方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散多模态大模型(dMLLM)的推理增强方法，通过时空token否决机制提升多模态推理准确性，属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，因此复用[CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_reasoning_cot"。

Importance reason: Novel training-free token veto method for diffusion MLLMs improves reasoning accuracy, aligning with user's interest in continuous multimodal models and efficient inference. Signals: diffusion MLLM, token veto, Taylor prediction, visual grounding, training-free

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong reasoning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting but incur high sequential-generation cost, error accumulation, and limited self-correction. Diffusion Multimodal Large Language Models (dMLLMs) unmask tokens in an order-agnostic process, improving efficiency and enabling iterative refinement, yet their reasoning and how to enhance it remain underexplored. We propose a training-free method, Spatio-Temporal Token Veto (ST-Veto), which leverages the ability to observe all token positions at each diffusion step. Rather than relying only on current-step confidence, ST-Veto vetoes temporally unstable tokens via second-order Taylor prediction of confidence dynamics and filters weakly grounded tokens using image-attention mass, swapping them with safer candidates. Across multiple dMLLMs and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, ST-Veto consistently outperforms standard decoding policies and prior VLM reasoning methods, improving accuracy by up to 9% with no additional training or generation cost. Analyses show that ST-Veto steers generation toward higher-confidence, better-grounded paths.

</details>


### [97] [Can We Break LLMs Out of Self-Loops? Fine-Grained Reasoning Control with Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18100)
*Sheldon Yu, Tong Yu, Xunyi Jiang, Rohan Surana, Gagan Mundada, Sungchul Kim, Lina Yao, Julian McAuley, Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SOPHIA方法，通过激活干预（activation steering）对LLM的推理过程进行细粒度控制。该方法将推理轨迹建模为隐状态序列，构建状态对索引的转向向量库，在线检测并干预自循环（self-loop）失败，从而提升推理质量和token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM扩展推理（extended reasoning）方法虽然提升了模型能力，但推理轨迹不可控，失败轨迹常陷入自循环（self-loop）耗尽token预算。基于prompt的方法无法提供对推理过程的细粒度控制，因此需要一种能在推理时干预、打破自循环的方法。

Method: SOPHIA方法将推理轨迹视为隐状态序列而非无结构文本。首先对每个前缀分类到隐状态，记录步级状态转移；然后构建由状态对索引的转向向量库（steering vector bank）。推理时，控制器推断当前状态，结合目标状态检索对应转向向量进行干预，同时在线检测自循环结构以防止模型陷入推理黑洞。

Result: 实验表明，SOPHIA能可靠地干预自循环失败，转向向量可泛化到不同状态对。最终任务准确率和token效率均表明细粒度可控性带来了更好的推理质量。

Conclusion: 通过激活干预对LLM推理过程进行细粒度控制是可行的，能够有效打破自循环、提升推理质量和效率。该方法为推理过程的可控性提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM推理过程中的细粒度控制，通过激活干预打破推理自循环（self-loop），属于推理过程控制与推理时计算扩展范畴。虽然论文聚焦于纯文本LLM推理而非多模态，但其核心关注点——推理轨迹控制、推理状态转移、推理质量提升——与多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一CANONICAL子主题高度相关，该子主题涵盖推理时计算扩展和推理过程控制。

Importance reason: Focuses on reasoning control via activation steering, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: activation steering, reasoning control, self-loop detection, LLM inference, token efficiency

Abstract: Extended reasoning has become standard for frontier Large Language Models (LLMs), yet the trajectories these models produce remain largely uncontrollable. Existing methods for shaping how a model reasons are prompt based approaches and operate at the input level, offering no fine-grained control over the reasoning process itself. Related work analyzes and discovers latent transition dynamics in the reasoning traces from Large Language Models. Building on this, we statistically characterize these states, and show that failure trajectories get stuck in self-loops, exhausting the token budget without progress toward the final answer. To intervene on these failures, We propose SOPHIA: Steering Of reasoning Processes via Hidden-state Intervention and Activations. We treat each reasoning trace as a sequence of latent states rather than an unstructured texts, and investigate whether inference time interventions can provide fine-grained control over the self-looping reasoning process. We classify every prefix to a latent state, record step level transitions, and use them to construct a bank of steering vectors indexed by state pairs. At inference time, a controller infers the current state and, given a target state, retrieves the corresponding vector and can also detect self-loops online from the transition structure to prevent the model from sinking into a reasoning black hole. Through extensive experiments, our method reliably intervenes on self-loop failures, with steering vectors that generalize to different state pairs. End task accuracy and token efficiency indicate that fine-grained controllability results in better reasoning quality.

</details>

### [98] [From Modalities to Propositions: A Language-Centric Framework for Multimodal Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16560)
*Nadine Chang, Maying Shen, Shizhe Diao, Jialiang Wang, Jingde Chen, Thomas Breuel, Pavlo Molchanov, Rafid Mahmood, Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM, cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一种以语言为中心的多模态智能框架，将图像、视频、文本等观测数据表示为原子命题（atomic propositions）的集合，通过全局语义码本将不同模态统一到共享的规范命题词汇空间，实现可解释推理、跨模态理解与检索、组合式多模态理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型通常将不同模态映射到独立的嵌入空间，缺乏统一的、可解释的语义表示，难以实现细粒度的跨模态推理与组合式理解。作者希望构建一个语言中心化的表示框架，使所有模态都能在同一个可解释的命题空间中表达和推理。

Method: 提出一个语言中心化框架，核心包括：(1) 将任意模态的观测（图像、视频、文本）表示为原子命题（atomic propositions）的集合，每个命题是关于场景中实体、动作和关系的简单陈述；(2) 构建全局语义码本（global semantic codebook），将不同模态的命题统一到共享的规范命题词汇空间；(3) 该表示支持从细粒度事实到高层概念的层次化组合，实现可解释推理、跨模态理解与检索、复杂结构化检索与数据策展。在自动驾驶和开放世界数据上验证。

Result: 在自动驾驶场景和开放世界数据上展示了框架的有效性，包括跨模态理解、结构化检索、数据策展等任务，验证了基于原子命题的统一表示能够实现可解释的跨模态推理与组合式多模态理解。

Conclusion: 提出了一种语言中心化的多模态表示框架，通过原子命题和全局语义码本将不同模态统一到可解释的共享空间，为多模态理解、检索与组合推理提供了一种新的范式，在自动驾驶和开放世界数据上展示了潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于原子命题的语言中心化多模态表示框架，将图像、视频、文本等不同模态统一映射到共享的规范命题词汇表，实现跨模态理解、检索与组合推理。核心贡献在于跨模态统一表示与检索，因此映射到 canonical 子主题「多模态检索」。

Importance reason: Proposes a language-centric multimodal framework with interpretable atomic propositions, but focuses on retrieval and autonomous driving, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Multimodal retrieval, interpretable reasoning, autonomous driving, atomic propositions, cross-modal understanding

Abstract: We propose a language representation for multimodal data in which any observation, whether image, video, or text, is expressed as a bag of atomic propositions, simple statements about the entities, actions, and relations in a scene. A global semantic codebook unifies these into a shared vocabulary of canonical atomic propositions, placing every modality and observation into one interpretable space that spans fine grained facts to high level concepts and composes into richer ones. This brings interpretability with reasoning, cross-modal understanding and retrieval, and compositionality that enables complex multimodal understanding, rich data curation and complex structured retrieval. We demonstrate the framework on autonomous driving and open-world data.

</details>


### [99] [ColGraphRAG: Late-Interaction Evidence Retrieval for Multimodal GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16208)
*Seonok Kim*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ColGraphRAG 提出在多模态 GraphRAG 中，将图链接图像的候选排序从单向量双编码器相似度替换为 ColBERT/ColPali 风格的 late-interaction MaxSim 多向量评分，在 MultimodalQA 上提升了检索和下游 QA 性能，尤其在视觉证据重要的场景中效果更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图增强多模态问答（Graph-grounded multimodal QA）中，图链接图像的候选排序依赖单向量双编码器相似度，这忽略了图像 patch 级和 token 级的结构信息，导致细粒度对齐不足，影响下游推理的准确性。

Method: 在保持离线图构建、文本/表格侧检索、结构化提取和下游推理不变的前提下，将图链接图像节点的视觉候选排序算子替换为 ColBERT/ColPali 系列的 late-interaction MaxSim 多向量评分机制，利用多向量交互实现更细粒度的图像-查询对齐。

Result: 在 MultimodalQA 数据集上，该方法在检索阶段对图链接图像候选的点估计有所提升，下游 QA 也获得增益；在视觉证据重要的场景中提升更大，而在文本主导的问题上趋势混合。

Conclusion: Late-interaction 多向量评分能够改善图链接视觉证据的检索质量，为多模态 GraphRAG 中视觉证据的纳入提供了机制层面的证据，但更广泛的验证和更细粒度的图级诊断仍是未来工作。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态图增强检索（GraphRAG）中的视觉证据检索，使用ColBERT/ColPali风格的late-interaction多向量评分改进图链接图像的候选排序，属于多模态检索范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索" (multimodal_retrieval)。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal retrieval for GraphRAG, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models or video/image generation. Signals: MultimodalQA benchmark, late-interaction scoring, graph-grounded QA, visual evidence retrieval, ColBERT/ColPali style

Abstract: Graph-grounded multimodal question answering organizes text, tables, and images in a structured evidence graph, yet end-to-end accuracy depends on which multimodal assets are ranked highly enough to enter downstream reasoning; for graph-linked images, single-vector bi-encoder similarity can discard patch- and token-level structure needed for fine-grained alignment. We evaluate replacing the visual candidate-ranking operator over graph-linked image nodes with late-interaction MaxSim-style multi-vector scoring in the ColBERT/ColPali lineage, while keeping offline graph construction, text- and table-side retrieval, structured extraction, and downstream reasoning unchanged. On MultimodalQA, this change is associated with improved retrieval-stage point estimates for graph-linked image candidates and downstream QA gains, with larger movement where visual evidence matters most and mixed trends on text-dominant questions; we interpret the pattern as mechanism-level evidence for graph-linked visual evidence inclusion, while broader validation and finer graph-level diagnostics remain important future work.

</details>


### [100] [Eddy-VL 1.9B: Structural Pruning and Layered Distillation for Edge-Deployable Multimodal Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16316)
*HanYeong Cho, Changwoo Kim, Taeuk Chu, Jimin Park*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (64.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Eddy-VL 1.9B 是一个基于 Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B 剪枝蒸馏得到的压缩多模态嵌入模型，专为离线边缘部署（如法医取证场景）设计。通过探针驱动的结构化剪枝（移除4层文本解码器）和分层知识蒸馏（含空洞覆盖映射、中间层注意力1-CKA、最终层MSE+余弦损失及Matryoshka维度），模型参数量减少约9.5%，在MMEB-V2基准上保留教师模型91.7%的性能（63.2 vs 68.9），前向延迟降低约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能多模态嵌入模型（如Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B）参数量大、推理延迟高，难以部署在无云端API可用的离线边缘设备（如法医取证场景）。需要一种既能保持检索质量又能显著压缩模型大小和推理延迟的方法。

Method: 采用两步压缩策略：(1) 探针驱动的结构化剪枝——通过相邻层线性CKA相似度排序，移除4个冗余文本解码器层（28→24层）；(2) 分层知识蒸馏——使用空洞覆盖的教师-学生映射（hole-covering mapping），中间层采用注意力图1-CKA损失，最终层采用MSE和余弦损失，并配合Matryoshka维度（{128,256,512,1024,2048}）进行多粒度嵌入学习。

Result: 模型参数量1.93B（bf16 3.85GB），比教师模型2.13B减少约9.5%。在MMEB-V2（78个任务，VLM2Vec协议）上总体得分63.2（教师68.9），保留91.7%性能；剪枝后仅56.8分，蒸馏恢复6.4分。组合推理任务接近教师（SugarCrepe 86.1 vs 86.4，MR2-Bench 24.5 vs 24.7，ARO 59.5 vs 60.4）。前向延迟从150.0ms降至136.4ms（约10%）。

Conclusion: Eddy-VL 1.9B 证明了结构化剪枝结合分层知识蒸馏可以有效压缩多模态嵌入模型，在显著降低参数量和推理延迟的同时保持较高的检索性能，适合离线边缘部署场景。Winoground组性能（6.8 vs 8.5）仍是主要局限。

Classification reason: 该论文构建面向边缘部署的多模态嵌入模型，用于视觉-语言检索（vision-language retrieval），属于多模态检索方向。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on edge deployment of multimodal embedding via pruning and distillation, but the subtopic (multimodal retrieval) and compression method are not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: Structural pruning, layered distillation, edge deployment, multimodal embedding, MMEB-V2 benchmark

Abstract: In this report, we introduce Eddy-VL 1.9B, a compressed multimodal embedding model built on Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B for offline, edge-deployable vision-language retrieval. Eddy-VL targets air-gapped forensic and investigative settings where cloud APIs are unavailable and low latency is essential. Compression combines (i) probe-driven structural pruning that removes four redundant text-decoder layers (28 to 24) ranked by adjacent-layer linear CKA, and (ii) layered knowledge distillation with hole-covering teacher-student mappings, mid-layer attention-map 1-CKA, and final-layer MSE and cosine losses with Matryoshka dimensions {128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048}. The released model contains 1,926,188,032 parameters (3.85 GB bf16), representing approximately 9.5% fewer parameters than the 2.13B teacher model. Empirical evaluations on MMEB-V2 (78 tasks, VLM2Vec protocol) show that Eddy-VL achieves an overall score of 63.2 compared with 68.9 for the teacher, retaining 91.7% of the teacher's performance while recovering 6.4 of the 12.1 points lost through pruning alone (56.8). Compositional reasoning performance remains close to the teacher on SugarCrepe (86.1 vs. 86.4), MR2-Bench (24.5 vs. 24.7), and ARO (59.5 vs. 60.4), while Winoground group performance (6.8 vs. 8.5) remains the primary limitation. Depth pruning also reduces forward latency by approximately 10% (150.0 to 136.4 ms per image on NVIDIA DGX Spark using FlashAttention-2). We present the architecture, compression methodology, training procedures, and evaluation results, demonstrating the effectiveness of Eddy-VL for multimodal retrieval under constrained edge deployment. Model weights and inference code are publicly available on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [101] [COLIP-2: Olfaction-Vision-Language Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17559)
*Kordel Kade France*

Main category: cs.ET, cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (48.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COLIP-2 是一个将嗅觉（分子结构、气体传感器读数、气味描述）与视觉、语言对齐到统一嵌入空间的多模态模型，旨在让机器人能够将检测到的气味与场景中的物体进行概率性定位，并针对边缘端实时机器人应用进行了优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏 ImageNet 规模的气味-图像配对数据集，且嗅觉在机器人多模态感知中尚未被充分整合。作者希望展示仅用开源嗅觉数据所能达到的极限，从而论证收集新数据集和开发新方法的必要性，以推动面向嗅觉的机器人感知能力。

Method: COLIP-2 采用对比学习（Contrastive Learning）将四种模态——分子结构、气体传感器读数、气味描述文本和图像——映射到共享嵌入空间。模型针对边缘端实时机器人应用进行了必要的优化，使其能够在机器人平台上运行。

Result: 内部测试验证了 COLIP-2 架构的有效性，模型能够在边缘设备上实时运行，实现气味到场景物体的概率性定位。

Conclusion: COLIP-2 证明了在有限开源嗅觉数据条件下构建多模态嗅觉-视觉-语言嵌入的可行性，为机器人嗅觉感知提供了基础，同时呼吁更大规模的数据集和方法创新以推动该领域发展。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了嗅觉-视觉-语言多模态嵌入空间，实现跨模态检索与对齐（分子结构、气体传感器读数、气味描述文本、图像之间的检索），属于多模态检索范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题「多模态检索」。

Importance reason: Novel multimodal embedding for olfaction, but far from user's core focus on continuous language/video generation and world models. Signals: olfactory-vision-language alignment, robotics edge optimization, no paired image-scent dataset, novel modality integration, low overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: The Contrastive Olfaction-Language-Image Pre-training 2 (COLIP-2) model is a multimodal embeddings space that places olfaction as a first-class citizen among vision and language. Molecular structure, gas-sensor readings, odor-descriptor language, and images are all trained into a single shared representation space, so that a robot can localize a detected aroma to objects in a scene probabilistically. No ImageNet-scale datasets of paired image-scent examples exists which warrants the need for their collection. Our intent with the release of COLIP-2 is to demonstrate the limit of what can be built for robotics with open-sourced olfactory data in order to ground the argument for why new methodologies and datasets are necessary in order to enable advanced olfactory-oriented perception capabilities. We enumerate results from internal testing of the COLIP-2 architecture and make necessary optimizations to run the model at the edge for real-time robotics applications. While developed with robotics in mind, the design of COLIP-2 has been influenced by experts across many disciplines of science in academia and industry, and we hope that the model can be useful in any multimodal domain requiring olfactory intelligence.

</details>


### [102] [Pailitao-MMSearch: Building Native E-Commerce Multimodal Search Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17499)
*Xiaohan Ye, Xu Chen, Zihan Gong, Jian Ding, Lianyu Du, Baicheng Chen, Yunmeng Shu, Jingqian Zhao, Zhixiang Zhao, Shuaiqi Jia, Chong Ma, Shuwen Xiao, Xiangheng Kong, Yuan Gao, Jun Song, Jinsong Lan, Xiaoyong Zhu, Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Pailitao-MMSearch 是一个面向电商场景的原生多模态搜索基础模型，通过混合语义ID（HybSID）、两阶段持续预训练和混合推理后训练流水线，在淘宝拍立淘平台上实现了GMV提升+13.61%、交易量提升+8.21%的显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商搜索面临两难困境：单模态专家模型（文本检索、视觉搜索、语音识别）各自独立部署，无法处理跨模态查询；而通用视觉语言模型缺乏电商领域知识，难以进行细粒度商品理解、用户行为建模和商业意图推理。

Method: 提出三个关键创新：(1) HybSID（混合语义ID）——将商品ID、文本语义和视觉语义统一编码为混合语义表示；(2) 两阶段持续预训练策略——先在电商数据上持续预训练，再针对搜索任务进行领域适配；(3) 混合推理后训练流水线——结合监督微调和强化学习优化搜索推理能力。模型基于Qwen架构，部署在淘宝拍立淘平台。

Result: 在线A/B测试中，相比传统多模态搜索流水线，GMV提升最高达+13.61%，交易量提升+8.21%，证明了原生电商多模态搜索大语言模型的有效性。

Conclusion: Pailitao-MMSearch 通过原生多模态搜索基础模型的设计，有效弥合了单模态专家模型与通用VLM之间的鸿沟，在真实电商场景中取得了显著的商业指标提升，验证了领域专用多模态搜索模型的巨大潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出电商多模态搜索基础模型，核心任务是跨模态检索（图像+文本组合查询），属于多模态检索范畴。现有 canonical subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）完全匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on e-commerce multimodal search, which is not aligned with your priority subtopics on continuous language models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: E-commerce application, Multimodal search, Hybrid Semantic ID, Continual pre-training, GMV improvement

Abstract: The evolution of e-commerce has fundamentally transformed how users search for products, shifting from simple text-based keyword queries to complex multimodal interactions that seamlessly combine product images, natural language descriptions, and mixed-intent instructions. However, existing approaches face a critical dilemma: single-modal specialist models, deployed independently for text retrieval, visual search, and voice recognition, operate in isolation and cannot handle cross-modal queries, while general-purpose vision-language models lack the domain-specific knowledge necessary for fine-grained product understanding, user behavior modeling, and commercial intent reasoning. In this work, we present Pailitao-MMSearch, one native e-commerce multimodal search foundation model designed to bridge this gap. Our approach introduces three key innovations: (1)HybSID (Hybrid Semantic ID);(2)a two-stage continual pre-training strategy; and (3)a hybrid reasoning post-training pipeline. Built upon Qwen and deployed on Taobao's Pailitao multimodal search platform, Pailitao-MMSearch achieves substantial improvements in online A/B testing, including up to +13.61\% in Gross Merchandise Volume (GMV) and +8.21\% in transaction volume compared to traditional multi-modal search pipeline, demonstrating the effectiveness of our native e-commerce multimodal search large language models.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_benchmark'></div>

## 多模态评测与基准 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态评测、新基准、评测协议、自动评估。

### [103] [RobustMAD: Evaluating Real-World Robustness of Multimodal Small Language Models for Deployable Anomaly Detection Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16243)
*Anushiya Arunan, Xin Li, Yan Qin, U-Xuan Tan, Nhu Khue Vuong, Xiaoli Li, Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (69.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RobustMAD 是首个面向工业异常检测场景的多模态小语言模型（MSLM）鲁棒性评测基准，涵盖物体理解、异常检测、不可回答问题及视觉质量退化等多样化开放查询。实验发现顶级 MSLM 甚至超越 GPT-5 Nano，但仍存在多模态接地脆弱、回答不全面、幻觉等关键鲁棒性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型因计算开销大和云端推理的隐私风险，难以在工业现场部署。多模态小语言模型（MSLM）是可行的替代方案，但缺乏全面的鲁棒性分析和反映真实工业条件的评测基准。

Method: 构建 RobustMAD 基准，设计涵盖物体理解、异常检测、不可回答问题、视觉质量退化四类开放查询的评测体系，系统评估多个 MSLM 在工业异常检测场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 顶级 MSLM 展现出有前景的能力，甚至超越更大的 GPT-5 Nano，但仍未达到安全关键要求。RobustMAD 揭示了三个关键失败模式：细粒度区分或退化视觉条件下的多模态接地脆弱、回答不够全面、对不可回答或不当查询的逻辑接地薄弱导致幻觉。

Conclusion: RobustMAD 为下一代多模态工业检测助手的设计提供了可操作的指导，强调了在保持 MSLM 优势的同时解决其鲁棒性缺陷的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 RobustMAD，一个面向多模态小语言模型（MSLM）在工业异常检测场景下的鲁棒性评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on robustness evaluation of small multimodal models for industrial anomaly detection, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous language models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: Multimodal small language model robustness, Industrial anomaly detection benchmark, GPT-5 Nano comparison, Fragile multimodal grounding, Hallucination in unanswerable queries

Abstract: Multimodal industrial anomaly inspection assistants are a critical component of next-generation smart factories, enabling interactive vision-language-based querying. However, multimodal large language models remain impractical for on-site deployment due to prohibitive computational demands and privacy risks from cloud-based inference. Compact multimodal small language models (MSLMs) offer a deployable alternative, yet progress is constrained by the lack of comprehensive robustness analyses and meaningfully challenging benchmarks that reflect real-world industrial conditions. To address this gap, we develop RobustMAD, the first deployment-motivated benchmark, designed to comprehensively evaluate model robustness through diverse open-ended queries spanning object understanding, anomaly detection, unanswerable problems, and visual quality degradations. Contrary to conventional assumptions, top-performing MSLMs exhibit promising capabilities, surprisingly outperforming even the larger GPT-5 Nano. However, they still fall short of safety-critical requirements, and RobustMAD reveals critical robustness gaps that pose operational risks. In particular, three recurring failure modes emerge: (i) fragile multimodal grounding under fine-grained distinctions or degraded visual conditions, (ii) insufficiently comprehensive responses, and (iii) weak logical grounding on unanswerable or ill-posed queries, leading to hallucinated outputs. Grounded in these insights, we provide actionable guidance for the design of next-generation multimodal industrial inspection assistants that leverage their promising competence. Code is available at .

</details>


### [104] [MAC 2026: Advancing Micro-Action Analysis Towards Fine-Grained Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16284)
*Kun Li, Dan Guo, Jihao Gu, Pengyu Liu, Xiaobai Li, Haoyu Chen, Yanbin Hao, Guoying Zhao, Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (68.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文介绍了第三届微动作分析挑战赛（MAC 2026），在ACM Multimedia 2026上举办。该届挑战赛从传统的识别与检测扩展到细粒度微动作理解，引入借助多模态大语言模型评估的新任务，总结了数据集、任务设置、评估协议、竞赛结果及优胜方案，并讨论了微动作分析的未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微动作（Micro-Actions）是社交互动和情感交流中重要的非语言线索，但由于其持续时间短、运动模式微弱、语义差异细粒度，导致标注、建模和评估困难。前两届MAC建立了标准化的识别与检测评估设置，第三届旨在推动从识别走向细粒度微动作理解。

Method: 论文提出了第三届微动作分析挑战赛（MAC 2026），在原有识别和检测任务基础上，新增细粒度微动作理解任务，借助多模态大语言模型（MLLM）评估模型捕捉细粒度语义线索和解释微妙人体微动作的能力。论文总结了数据集、任务设置、评估协议、竞赛结果及优胜团队的解决方案。

Result: 介绍了MAC 2026的竞赛结果和代表性优胜方案，展示了多模态大模型在细粒度微动作理解中的潜力，并指出了当前方法的局限性和未来方向。

Conclusion: 微动作分析正从识别与检测迈向细粒度理解，多模态大语言模型为这一方向提供了新的评估范式和研究机遇。未来工作可进一步探索更深层次的微动作语义理解和更广泛的人为中心视频理解应用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了第三届微动作分析挑战赛（MAC 2026），包含新的细粒度微动作理解任务，并借助多模态大模型进行评估。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，是[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on a niche micro-action benchmark, not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: micro-action analysis, fine-grained understanding, multimodal large language model evaluation, ACM Multimedia 2026 challenge, not autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: Micro-Actions (MAs) are subtle and spontaneous human behaviors that provide important non-verbal cues in social interaction and affective communication. However, their short duration, weak motion patterns, and fine-grained semantic differences make them difficult to annotate, model, and evaluate in a standardized manner. To promote academic research on micro-action analysis, we proposed and have annually organized the Micro-Action Analysis Grand Challenge (MAC) as a public benchmark platform for this emerging field. The first two editions of MAC established standardized evaluation settings for micro-action recognition and detection, providing publicly accessible datasets and protocols. Building upon these editions, this paper presents the 3rd MAC, held in conjunction with ACM Multimedia 2026. Under the theme of moving from recognition to fine-grained micro-action understanding, this edition further expands the scope of the challenge beyond conventional recognition and detection. In particular, we introduce a new task named fine-grained micro-action understanding, evaluated with the assistance of multimodal large language models, aiming to assess models' ability to capture fine-grained semantic cues and interpret subtle human micro-actions at a deeper level. We summarize the datasets, task settings, evaluation protocols, competition results, and representative solutions from top-performing teams. Finally, we discuss future directions for micro-action analysis and its broader role in human-centric video understanding.

</details>


### [105] [Seeing What Is Actually There: PriVE-Bench and PriVE-Tools for Counterfactual Evaluation of Agentic Visual Evidence in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16311)
*Jingyu Sun, Jiachen Tu, Yuyang Xue, Yaoxin Jiang, Guoyi Xu, Zhengtao Yao, Rui Qian, Yizheng Sun, Hongpeng Zhou, Jingyuan Sun, Yan Lin*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PriVE-Bench，一个基于反事实图像对（原始 vs 反事实）的 VLM 视觉接地评测基准，用于检测模型是否依赖语言/类别先验而非图像像素进行回答。同时提出 PriVE-Tools，扩展评估工具增强视觉证据（边界框、裁剪、缩放、轮廓）能否帮助模型克服先验偏差。实验表明视觉工具在某些场景下有效，但并非通用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 常依赖语言和类别先验而非图像本身来回答问题，反事实图像可诊断此问题。但现有基准仅检测先验跟随行为是否存在，未进一步探究：在工具增强和智能体视觉系统兴起的背景下，额外的视觉证据视图能否帮助 VLM 对抗先验偏差。

Method: 构建 PriVE-Bench：使用配对的原图与反事实图像（如将"狗叼报纸"反事实改为"猫叼报纸"），评估模型是否给出视觉接地答案。进一步提出 PriVE-Tools：在反事实评测中引入工具派生的视觉证据（边界框、裁剪图、缩放面板、轮廓图），比较原始输入、配对图像输入和工具条件输入下的准确率、先验跟随错误率等指标。

Result: 在多个开源和闭源 VLM 上的实验表明：视觉证据工具在某些设置下能帮助模型提升接地能力（尤其是当模型能有效利用局部化证据时），但并非通用解决方案——多个模型即使在明确提供相关视觉证据的情况下，仍然会跟随语言和类别先验。

Conclusion: 反事实评测揭示了 VLM 中先验跟随问题的普遍性；工具增强的视觉证据有一定帮助但有限，不能完全消除先验偏差。这为未来构建更可靠的视觉接地模型和评测方法提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 PriVE-Bench，一个用于评估 VLM 是否基于视觉证据而非语言/类别先验进行回答的反事实评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。同时论文还引入了 PriVE-Tools 工具扩展来评估工具增强的视觉证据对模型接地能力的影响。该工作核心贡献是评测基准，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM evaluation and prior bias, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM evaluation benchmark, counterfactual images, visual grounding, tool-augmented vision, prior-following bias

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often answer visual questions using learned language and category priors rather than grounding their predictions in the image itself. Counterfactual images provide a natural diagnostic setting for this failure mode: when visible evidence contradicts what is usually true, a grounded model should answer from the pixels, while a prior-following model will produce a canonical but visually incorrect response. However, existing counterfactual benchmarks mainly ask whether such prior-following behavior exists. In this paper, we ask a further question motivated by the rise of tool-augmented and agentic vision systems: can additional visual evidence views help VLMs reason against their priors? We introduce PriVE-Bench, a Prior-vs-Visual Evidence Benchmark that uses paired original and counterfactual images to distinguish visually grounded answers from prior-consistent errors. We further introduce PriVE-Tools, a controlled agentic-vision-inspired extension that evaluates whether tool-derived visual evidence -- including bounding boxes, crops, zoom panels, and contours -- improves grounding under the same counterfactual conflicts. Across open- and closed-source VLMs, we compare raw, paired-image, and tool-conditioned inputs using accuracy, prior-following error rate, and other-response rate. Our results show that visual evidence tools can help in some settings, especially when models can use localized evidence effectively, but they are not a universal remedy: several models continue to follow language and category priors even when relevant visual evidence is explicitly provided.

</details>


### [106] [Local Brushstroke Quality Assessment via Vision-Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16330)
*Mio Mitamura, Hirokatsu Kataoka*

TL;DR: 本文探究多模态大模型（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Flash）能否评估书法局部笔画质量并生成教育性自然语言反馈。构建了五点评分框架，与三位书法专家对比，发现 GPT-4o 绝对准确率最佳（MAE=0.885），但所有模型与人类专家的秩相关性均不显著。RAG 可提升秩相关但降低绝对准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 书法教育中，局部笔画质量的自动评估和反馈生成具有重要应用价值，但现有研究多关注整体作品评分，缺乏对局部笔画质量的细粒度评估，且多模态大模型在此领域的潜力尚未被系统探索。

Method: 构建评估框架：使用三个多模态大模型（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Flash）对书法作品的前后对比图像对进行五点评分，并与三位书法专家的评分进行对比。同时探索了 Claude 的 RAG 变体。使用 MAE 评估绝对准确率，Kendall's tau 评估秩相关性，并对生成理由进行词汇分析。

Result: GPT-4o 绝对准确率最佳（MAE=0.885），但所有模型与人类专家的整体秩相关性均不显著。词汇分析揭示了各模型的评价偏差特征。RAG 提升了秩相关性但降低了绝对准确率，表明基于文本的规则注入存在重要负面结果。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型在书法局部笔画质量评估中能达到一定绝对准确率，但尚无法与人类专家的排序判断一致。RAG 的引入存在权衡，提示需要更精细的评估策略。该工作为 AI 辅助书法教育提供了有价值的基准和方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型在书法笔画局部质量评估上的表现，构建了评估框架并与人类专家评分对比，属于多模态评测范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on calligraphy quality assessment, which is far outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks any boost keywords. Signals: No overlap with priority subtopics, No boost keywords present, Negative keywords not triggered but irrelevant, Low general research value for the user's focus

Abstract: This paper investigates whether multimodal LLMs can evaluate local brushstroke quality in calligraphy and generate educationally useful natural language feedback. We construct an evaluation framework in which three multimodal LLMs (GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4, and Gemini 2.5 Flash) assess before-after image pairs of calligraphic works using a five-point ordinal scale, and compare their outputs against scores assigned by three expert calligraphers. We additionally examine a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) variant of Claude as a preliminary condition. Results show that all models achieve useful levels of absolute score accuracy (MAE), with GPT-4o performing best (MAE = 0.885). However, none of the models produce statistically significant overall rank correlations with human experts (Kendall's tau). Vocabulary analysis of generated rationales reveals characteristic evaluative biases in each model, and RAG is shown to improve rank correlation while worsening absolute accuracy, constituting an important negative result for text-based rule injection.

</details>


### [107] [Otap:Structure-Aware Optimal Transport for Evaluating Planning and Execution in Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17082)
*Babak Barazandeh, Subhabrata Majumdar, George Michailidis*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (47.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出OTAP，一种基于结构感知最优传输的智能体轨迹评估方法。将Agent执行轨迹建模为依赖图，通过非平衡融合Gromov-Wasserstein距离计算轨迹与有效解图集之间的距离，得到对依赖保持重排序不变、对冗余步骤有界敏感、能处理缺失/幻觉步骤的伪度量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent轨迹评估方法存在严重缺陷：二值成功标志无法区分"碰巧成功"与"真正正确"的方案，也无法解释失败原因；精确匹配则惩罚有效但顺序不同的合理方案。需要一种能捕捉轨迹结构、容忍合理变体、诊断失败原因的细粒度评估指标。

Method: 将Agent轨迹建模为属性依赖图（节点为步骤/工具调用，边为依赖关系），将评估问题转化为计算轨迹图与一组有效解图之间的非平衡融合Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输距离。该方法支持软匹配（允许不同粒度）、非平衡边际（处理缺失/幻觉步骤）、对依赖保持重排序不变。

Result: 在受控扰动和三个公开基准上，OTAP在语义指标低于随机水平的场景中能有效区分有效与无效轨迹。当依赖图精确恢复时准确率最高，从自由文本轨迹启发式推断依赖图时性能有所下降但仍优于基线。

Conclusion: OTAP提供了一种结构感知的Agent轨迹评估新范式，弥补了二值成功标志和精确匹配的不足，为Agent系统的细粒度诊断和比较提供了理论上有保证的度量工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种基于最优传输的智能体轨迹评估方法（OTAP），属于对LLM Agent规划与执行过程的评测方法。虽然论文不直接涉及多模态输入，但其评估框架适用于多模态Agent轨迹（包含工具调用、中间结果等），且"评测"主题明确对应[多模态评测与基准]这一CANONICAL subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent trajectory evaluation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with boost keywords. Signals: Agent trajectory evaluation, Optimal transport, Dependency graph, Unbalanced fused Gromov-Wasserstein, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Large language model agents solve tasks by generating trajectories that interleave planning, tool calls, and intermediate results. Current evaluation metrics reduce such a trajectory to a binary success flag or compare it against a reference by exact matching. A success flag cannot distinguish a sound solution from one that succeeds by luck, and says nothing about why a failed run went wrong. Exact matching penalizes plans that are valid but reordered or decomposed differently from the reference. We reframe trajectory evaluation as a distance between the agent's execution graph and a set of valid solution graphs, and instantiate it via an unbalanced fused Gromov-Wasserstein transport problem over attributed dependency graphs. The resulting score, termed \otap{} (Optimal Transport for Agentic Planning), is a pseudo-metric that is provably invariant to dependency-preserving reorderings and has bounded sensitivity to redundant steps. Its unbalanced marginals handle missing or hallucinated steps without forcing a match, and its soft coupling accommodates variation in plan granularity. On controlled perturbations and three public benchmarks, \otap{} separates valid from invalid trajectories in a regime where semantics-only metrics score below chance. Its accuracy is highest when the dependency graph is recovered exactly, and drops only when the graph is inferred heuristically from free-text traces.

</details>


### [108] [Can Multimodal Large Language Models Understand OCT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16609)
*Baochen Fu, Wenzhi Deng, Baihao Jin, Yang Li, Zihan Nie, Kailin Jiang, Yuntao Du, Weiye Song*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (62.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出OCT-Bench，一个包含10,076道多选题、覆盖20个细粒度任务的OCT图像理解基准，系统评估了20个多模态大模型，发现现有模型在OCT理解上仍远未达到可靠水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCT理解评测大多局限于粗粒度疾病分类或孤立的视觉问答，缺乏对从视觉感知到临床推理完整认知过程的评估。

Method: 构建OCT-Bench基准，包含10,076道高质量多选题，来自7个公开数据集的4,137张OCT图像。按照临床解读流程，建立包含感知、认知、推理三个维度共20个细粒度任务的分层能力分类体系。系统评估20个代表性MLLM（包括闭源、开源通用和医学领域模型）。

Result: 当前模型在OCT理解上远未达到可靠水平；医学领域适配和模型规模扩大均不能一致地提升各能力层次的表现。

Conclusion: OCT-Bench为MLLM提供了全面细粒度的评测框架，有助于识别能力瓶颈并推动临床级OCT理解的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了OCT-Bench，一个针对OCT图像理解的多模态大模型评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_benchmark"。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow medical benchmark for OCT, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Medical diagnosis benchmark, OCT image understanding, MLLM evaluation, Not in priority subtopics, Contains negative keyword "medical diagnosis"

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography (OCT) imaging is essential for the diagnosis and treatment of retinal diseases. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated considerable potential in medical image analysis, existing benchmarks largely reduce OCT understanding to coarse-grained disease classification or isolated visual question answering, leaving the complete cognitive process from visual perception to clinical reasoning insufficiently evaluated. To address this limitation, we introduce OCT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark dedicated to OCT image understanding. OCT-Bench comprises 10,076 high-quality multiple-choice questions constructed from 4,137 OCT images across seven public datasets. Following the real-world clinical interpretation workflow, we establish a hierarchical capability taxonomy consisting of 20 fine-grained tasks across three dimensions: Perception, Cognition, and Reasoning. These tasks cover a broad range of capabilities, including imaging attributes, retinal anatomy, lesion characteristics, spatial relationships, disease assessment, therapeutic decision-making, and prognostic management. We systematically evaluate 20 representative MLLMs, including proprietary models, open-source general-purpose models, and medical-domain models. Experimental results demonstrate that current models remain substantially short of reliable OCT understanding. Moreover, neither medical-domain adaptation nor increased model scale consistently improves performance across capability levels. OCT-Bench enables comprehensive and fine-grained evaluation of MLLMs, providing a foundation for identifying capability bottlenecks and advancing clinically grounded OCT understanding.

</details>


### [109] [EvoGUI: An Evolution-Aware Benchmark for GUI State-Transition Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17050)
*Yaohan Yang, Minglei Shi, Borui Zhang, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EvoGUI是一个用于评估GUI智能体状态转换理解能力的诊断性基准，通过将GUI轨迹转化为时序排序、逆动作/值预测和对比单步后继判别三类VQA探针，对28个VLM模型进行零样本评测，发现最强模型仅达60.4% EvoGain，揭示了该任务仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体评估依赖端到端成功率，无法区分状态转换理解能力与感知、定位、规划、恢复等其他能力的贡献。作者希望构建一个诊断性框架，专门解耦并评测智能体对GUI状态转换的理解能力。

Method: 将归一化的GUI轨迹转化为三类互补的VQA探针：(1) 时序排序——判断多个状态的先后顺序；(2) 逆动作/值预测——根据前后状态推断中间动作；(3) 对比单步后继判别——从候选状态中识别正确的后继状态。标签直接从轨迹顺序和记录的动作中推导，无需额外标注。基于Mind2Web和WebLINX构建了包含3000个实例、覆盖120个领域的EvoGUI-Bench。

Result: 在28个VLM配置的零样本评测中，最强模型仅达到60.4% EvoGain。模型规模和GUI专业化程度并不能可靠预测性能，表明当前VLM在GUI状态转换理解方面存在显著不足。

Conclusion: EvoGUI-Bench可作为端到端GUI智能体评估的可扩展诊断补充，揭示了当前VLM在状态转换理解任务上的巨大提升空间，为未来GUI智能体的能力解耦评估提供了新工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个针对GUI智能体状态转换理解的诊断性评测基准EvoGUI-Bench，属于多模态评测与基准方向。论文构建了基于GUI轨迹的VQA探针，评估了28个VLM模型，属于多模态评测范畴，因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Provides a diagnostic benchmark for GUI state-transition understanding, but is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: GUI agent evaluation, diagnostic benchmark, VQA probes, zero-shot VLM evaluation, limited top performance

Abstract: GUI agents must reason about how actions transform interface states, but end-to-end success rates entangle this ability with perception, grounding, planning, and recovery. We introduce EvoGUI, a diagnostic framework that converts normalized GUI trajectories into three complementary visual question answering probes: temporal ordering, inverse action/value prediction, and contrastive one-step successor discrimination. Their labels are derived from trajectory order and logged actions, requiring no additional task-label annotation after trajectory normalization. We instantiate EvoGUI-Bench from Mind2Web and WebLINX, yielding 3,000 instances across 120 domains, and evaluate 28 vision-language model configurations zero-shot. The strongest model reaches only 60.4 EvoGain, while model scale and GUI specialization do not reliably predict performance. These results establish EvoGUI-Bench as a scalable diagnostic complement to end-to-end GUI-agent evaluation while exposing substantial headroom in state-transition understanding. The source code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [110] [ProEvent: An Event-centric Benchmark for Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17701)
*Guanzhen Li, Liangming Pan, Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ProEvent 是首个面向主动智能体的事件中心评测基准，通过合成但真实的即时通讯聊天场景，评估智能体主动维护用户时间表的能力。实验表明，当前 LLM 作为主动智能体表现不佳，GPT-5.1 也仅在 26.7% 的场景中正确反应，存在过度反应和无法处理事件取消等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动智能体研究大多忽略了事件中心的辅助能力，且主动辅助的开放性质使得可靠评估面临挑战。作者希望填补这一空白，提供一个标准化的评测基准来评估智能体在动态聊天环境中主动维护用户时间表的能力。

Method: 构建 ProEvent 基准，包含合成但真实的即时通讯聊天数据，考虑用户间的动态交互、并发聊天线程和现实世界噪声。评估维度包括响应时机、单步响应正确性和多步响应正确性。在 8 个 LLM 和流水线上进行实验。

Result: 当前智能体经常过度反应，难以处理事件取消。GPT-5.1 仅在 26.7% 的场景中正确反应。进一步定性分析揭示了当前 LLM 作为主动智能体的根本局限性，特别是在检测隐式事件和从用户第一人称视角推理方面。

Conclusion: ProEvent 揭示了当前 LLM 在主动事件中心辅助方面的显著不足，为未来主动智能体的研究提供了有价值的评测工具和方向指引。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 ProEvent，一个面向主动智能体的事件中心评测基准，评估智能体基于即时通讯聊天维护用户时间表的能力。这属于多模态/智能体评测基准，可归入 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark），该 subtopic 涵盖新基准与评测协议。

Importance reason: Proactive agent benchmark is tangentially related to multimodal understanding but lacks direct connection to user's core continuous multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: event-centric benchmark, proactive agents, LLM evaluation, low performance on event tracking, no multimodal or generation focus

Abstract: Proactive agents are expected to anticipate user needs and provide autonomous assistance by perceiving environmental context without explicit instructions. A fundamental capability of such agents is to identify and track users' upcoming events, enabling continuous and event-specific assistance. For example, by recording the time and location of a planned hike, an agent can deliver weather reminders in advance or provide navigation support before departure. However, existing works on proactive agents largely overlook event-centric assistance, and the open-ended nature of proactive assistance poses challenges for reliable evaluation. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ProEvent, the first event-centric benchmark designed to assess an agent's ability to proactively maintain a user's timetable based on ongoing instant messaging chats. ProEvent provides synthesized yet realistic chats that consider the dynamic interaction among users, concurrent chat threads, and noise in the real world, and evaluates proactive agents on response timing, single-step response correctness, and multi-step response correctness. Experiments on eight LLMs and pipelines reveal that current agents frequently overact and struggle with event cancellation. Notably, even GPT-5.1 only reacts correctly in 26.7% of scenarios. Further qualitative analysis reveals fundamental limitations of current LLMs as proactive agents, particularly in detecting implicit events and reasoning from the user's first-person perspective.

</details>


### [111] [WorldCupArena: Fine-Grained Evaluation of Language Models and Deep-Research Agents on Football Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18084)
*Zhaokai Wang, Tianlin Gui, Jiayuan Rao, Shangzhe Di, Yihong Tang, Dingli Liang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WorldCupArena 是一个动态基准，用于评估语言模型和深度研究代理在足球比赛预测上的细粒度能力。以2026年世界杯为首次评测，模型需在赛前基于给定或自行搜索的信息预测比分、球员、事件、统计数据等，赛后与实际结果对比。在104场比赛和13个系统上的实验表明，结果准确率相近的模型在细粒度预测上差异更大，最佳系统相比博彩市场和人类基线仅有小幅优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评测多关注静态知识或通用推理，缺乏对动态、时效性信息利用能力的评估，尤其是在需要赛前预测且答案事后才可知的场景下。足球预测需要模型整合不断变化的信息并做出明确预测，现有基准难以衡量模型在这类动态预测任务上的细粒度能力。

Method: WorldCupArena 构建了一个可复用的动态评测流程：每场比赛前，模型接收通用证据包或自行搜索信息，预测比赛结果、精确比分、可能球员和事件、比赛统计数据以及赛事最终结果。赛后将这些预测与实际记录对比，计算结果准确率、精确比分准确率、比分接近度分数（Scoreline）以及其他预测任务的得分。评测流程可扩展至未来赛事，避免数据泄露。

Result: 在104场比赛和13个系统上的评测显示：结果准确率相近的模型在细粒度预测（如精确比分、球员事件）上差异更明显；最佳系统相比博彩市场和人类基线在结果和精确比分准确率上仅有小幅提升，但在Scoreline分数上有更明显的优势。

Conclusion: WorldCupArena 提供了一个动态、细粒度的评测框架，能够更全面地评估语言模型在足球预测任务上的能力，揭示了模型间在细粒度预测上的显著差异，并指出当前最佳系统相比传统基线优势有限，仍有较大提升空间。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于评估语言模型和深度研究代理在足球预测任务上的动态基准（WorldCupArena），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然该基准聚焦于足球预测这一特定领域，但其核心贡献是构建评测框架、评估协议和指标，属于评测基准类工作。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on a narrow football prediction benchmark, which is far from the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, video generation, and world models. Signals: narrow domain benchmark, no continuous multimodal or video generation, no autoregressive or diffusion methods, no world model focus, low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Predicting a football match before kickoff requires more than knowing past results: a model must use changing information and make a clear prediction before the answer is available. We present WorldCupArena, a dynamic benchmark for language models and deep-research agents. The 2026 FIFA World Cup is its first evaluation, and the same process can be reused for future leagues and cups. Before each match, a model either receives a common evidence package or searches for information itself. It predicts the result and score, likely players and events, match statistics, and the outcome of the competition. After the match, these predictions are compared with the recorded result. We report result accuracy, exact-score accuracy, and a scoreline score that gives some credit when a predicted score is close but not exact, together with scores for the other prediction tasks. Across 104 matches and 13 systems, models with similar result accuracy differ more clearly on detailed predictions. Compared with betting-market and human-fan baselines, the best system shows only small gains in result and exact-score accuracy, but a clearer gain in Scoreline. New schedules can be added as they begin, allowing the benchmark to evaluate future models without using outcomes that are already known. Code, prompts, predictions, and evaluation scripts are open sourced at .

</details>


### [112] [BanClickThumb: A Multimodal Dataset and Transformer Fusion Benchmarks for Clickbait Detection in Bengali YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17182)
*Md. Ariful Islam, Md Tanvirul Islam, Md. Maruf Hossain Miru, Md Khalid Syfullah*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (47.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了BanClickThumb，一个包含7,147个孟加拉语YouTube缩略图-标题对的多模态数据集，用于点击诱饵检测。作者标注了五个内容领域的数据，并评测了文本、图像和多模态融合方法。提出的BanClickFusionFormer（ViT + XLM-RoBERTa中间融合）达到0.84的最佳准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语视频平台上的点击诱饵检测缺乏公开可用的多模态数据集，现有研究主要针对英语等资源丰富的语言，低资源语言的多模态内容分析研究不足。

Method: 构建了7,147个孟加拉语YouTube缩略图-标题对数据集，由10名标注者标注，Cohen's Kappa为0.83-0.93。提出了BanClickFusionFormer模型，通过中间融合（intermediate fusion）结合ViT图像编码器和XLM-RoBERTa文本编码器进行点击诱饵二分类检测。

Result: 文本模型BanClickTextFormer（XLM-RoBERTa）准确率0.82，图像模型BanClickImageFormer（SwiftFormer）准确率0.68，多模态融合模型BanClickFusionFormer达到最佳准确率0.84。错误分析表明密集缩略图文字、比喻性语言和文化特定俚语仍是挑战。

Conclusion: 多模态融合对孟加拉语点击诱饵检测有效，BanClickFusionFormer优于单模态方法。该数据集和基准为低资源多模态内容分析提供了公开基准。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了孟加拉语YouTube视频的点击诱饵检测数据集BanClickThumb，并进行了多模态融合基准评测。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖新数据集构建与多模态评测基准，与本论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on Bengali clickbait detection, which is far from the user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video generation, and world models. Signals: Low-resource language, clickbait detection, multimodal fusion benchmark, no autoregressive or diffusion generation, no continuous representation

Abstract: Clickbait, where video titles and thumbnails exaggerate or misrepresent content, reduces user trust, wastes attention, and promotes misinformation on video-sharing platforms. Detecting Bengali clickbait remains challenging because publicly available multimodal datasets are limited. To address this gap, we introduce BanClickThumb, a curated dataset of 7,147 Bengali YouTube thumbnail-title pairs from five content domains, annotated by ten annotators with high agreement (Cohen's Kappa: 0.83-0.93). Using this dataset, we benchmark text-only, image-only, and multimodal approaches. Among unimodal models, BanClickTextFormer (XLM-RoBERTa) achieves 0.82 accuracy, while BanClickImageFormer (SwiftFormer) reaches 0.68. Our proposed multimodal model, BanClickFusionFormer, combines ViT and XLM-RoBERTa through intermediate fusion and achieves the best accuracy of 0.84. Error analysis shows that dense thumbnail text, figurative language, and culturally specific slang remain challenging. Our findings demonstrate the effectiveness of multimodal fusion for Bengali clickbait detection and provide a publicly available benchmark to support future research on low-resource multimodal content analysis.

</details>


### [113] [SpEmoC: A Balanced Speaker-Segment Multimodal Emotion Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18109)
*Sania Bano, Shahzad Ahmad, Santosh Kumar Vipparthi, Sukalpa Chanda, Subrahmanyam Murala*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpEmoC 是一个面向对话场景的多模态情感识别基准数据集，包含从3100部影视作品中筛选出的3万条高质量、类别均衡的音视频+文本片段，采用严格的影视级数据划分以避免内容泄露，并通过混合标注流程（预训练模型+人工验证）标注7种情感。实验表明，均衡的数据分布和严格的数据划分有助于提升跨数据集泛化能力和少数类情感建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别数据集在规模、情感分布、模态对齐和数据划分策略上差异较大，导致跨数据集泛化评估不可靠，且少数类情感（如恐惧、厌恶）建模困难。作者旨在构建一个类别均衡、划分严格的多模态情感基准，以支持更可靠的模型评估和迁移学习研究。

Method: 从3100部英语影视作品中提取306,544个原始片段，通过预训练模型初筛+人工验证的混合标注流程，筛选出3万条高质量、类别均衡的片段。每个片段包含同步的视觉、音频和文本模态，标注7种情感（包括Fear和Disgust等少数类）。采用严格的影视级数据划分（同一影视作品的所有片段归入同一子集），防止内容泄露。

Result: 在域内基准测试、跨数据集迁移、低数据训练、类别不平衡分析和模态迁移等实验中，SpEmoC 的均衡数据分布和严格划分策略使模型在各情感类别上表现更稳定，跨数据集泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 数据集设计（尤其是类别均衡和严格的数据划分）对鲁棒且可迁移的多模态情感识别至关重要。SpEmoC 为情感计算领域提供了一个更可靠、更公平的评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向对话中多模态情感识别的新基准数据集 SpEmoC，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。该主题是 [CANONICAL] 条目，直接匹配。

Importance reason: This is a solid benchmark paper for multimodal emotion recognition, but it does not align with the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models, video generation, or world models. Signals: Multimodal emotion benchmark, Balanced dataset design, Cross-dataset generalization, No priority subtopic match, No boost keywords present

Abstract: Understanding human emotions in spoken conversations is a key challenge in affective computing, with applications in empathetic AI, human computer interaction, and mental health monitoring. However, existing datasets vary in scale, emotion distribution, modality alignment, and data partitioning strategies, which can influence reliable cross-dataset generalization and minority-emotion modeling. We introduce SpEmoC a Speaking segment Emotion for Conversations comprising 306,544 raw clips from 3,100 English language movies and TV series. From these, 30,000 high quality, class balanced clips are curated, featuring synchronized visual, audio, and textual modalities annotated for seven emotions through a hybrid pipeline that integrates pretrained models with human validation. SpEmoC uses strict movie- and series-level splits to prevent content overlap between split sets, allowing more reliable evaluation of model generalization. The dataset also maintains a near-balanced distribution across seven emotions, including minority classes such as Fear and Disgust, which supports more balanced learning across categories. Extensive experiments, including in-domain benchmarking, cross-dataset transfer, low-data training, class-imbalance analysis, and modality transfer show that balanced data and careful splitting lead to more stable performance across emotions when models are evaluated on other datasets. These results highlight the importance of dataset design for robust and transferable multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>
