### [114] [CRISP: Pre-LLM Yet Text-Driven Visual Token Pruning for Efficient LVLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16326)
*Xu Li, Yi Zheng, Mengyang Zhao, Yuxuan Liang, Zhe Liu, Rui Zhu, Xiaolei Chen, Wei Zhou, Baoquan Zhao, Juncen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (70.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CRISP 提出了一种在 LLM 之前、由文本驱动的视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过两阶段流水线（文本对齐识别 + 语义多样性增强）在保持高精度的同时大幅降低 LVLM 推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 需要处理大量视觉 token，导致推理开销巨大。已有的视觉 token 剪枝方法要么在 LLM 之前使用与文本无关的启发式方法，要么在 LLM 内部剪枝但带来效率损失和跨模态注意力噪声。需要一种既高效又能保留指令相关证据和场景上下文的剪枝方法。

Method: CRISP 采用两阶段流水线：Stage 1 通过文本-视觉对齐识别与指令相关的视觉 token；Stage 2 通过语义多样性增强来补充场景上下文信息，确保剪枝后保留的 token 既包含任务关键信息又保持场景完整性。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5 和 LLaVA-NeXT 上的实验表明，CRISP 在激进剪枝比例下仍能保持高达 99.5% 的准确率，同时将推理成本和延迟降低 2 倍以上。

Conclusion: CRISP 是一种实用的预 LLM 文本驱动视觉 token 剪枝方案，在资源受限场景下能有效平衡 LVLM 推理效率与性能，为高效多模态推理提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉 token 剪枝方法以加速 LVLM 推理，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal) 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient LVLM inference via text-driven visual token pruning, aligning with user's model efficiency priority and boosting inference speed. Signals: efficient inference, visual token pruning, text-driven pruning, LVLM acceleration, resource-constrained scenarios

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) typically require processing hundreds to thousands of visual tokens, leading to substantial inference overhead. Existing visual token pruning methods either operate before the LLM using text-agnostic heuristics or prune inside the LLM at the cost of efficiency and noisy cross-modal attention. To address these limitations, we propose CRISP, a pre-LLM yet text-driven visual token pruning framework that preserves both instruction-relevant evidence and essential scene context. CRISP works in a two-stage pipeline: Stage 1 first identifies text-aligned visual tokens, and Stage 2 enhances contextual completeness through semantic diversity. Extensive experiments on LLaVA-1.5 and LLaVA-NeXT demonstrate that CRISP achieves superior performance retention under aggressive pruning ratios, maintaining up to 99.5% accuracy while reducing inference cost and latency by more than 2 times. CRISP serves as a practical solution for efficient LVLM inference, especially in resource-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [115] [Searching for Task-Specific Vision Paths: Evolutionary Block Pruning Across Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17052)
*Tarun Tomar*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (52.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究能否在不微调的情况下，为VLM跳过固定数量的视觉编码器块。提出源平衡进化搜索方法，在Qwen2.5-VL和SmolVLM2上实验，发现搜索能可靠地找到更好的块剪枝路径，但能力标签（OCR、计数等）并不能定义通用可迁移的视觉通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型对每个问题都执行完整的视觉编码器，但OCR、计数、物体、属性、空间等不同查询可能不需要相同的计算量。本文研究能否在固定预算下跳过部分视觉块而不进行微调，以提升推理效率。

Method: 提出源平衡进化搜索（source-balanced evolutionary search）来寻找最优的K个视觉块剪枝路径。定义了两种策略：共享K块路径（对所有问题使用同一组跳过的块）和能力特定K块策略（根据已知的能力标签选择不同路径）。与独立排序、连续移除和随机路径等基线进行比较。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct和SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct上实验。搜索的共享四块路径在SmolVLM2上比独立构造高4.91个百分点。六块能力策略在Qwen上比共享路径高2.17点（OCR增益7.10点），但在IIIT5K上SmolVLM2的OCR特定路径比共享路径低13.6点。

Conclusion: 组合搜索能可靠地改进路径构建，但能力标签并不能定义通用可迁移的视觉通路。搜索方法跨架构可迁移，但能力特定路径的稳定性不足。

Classification reason: 该论文研究在VLM中跳过视觉编码器块以节省计算，属于多模态模型高效化/轻量化方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM efficiency via block pruning, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM efficiency, evolutionary block pruning, no fine-tuning, capability-specific routes, limited transferability

Abstract: Vision-language models normally execute the same complete vision encoder for every question, even when OCR, counting, object, attribute, and spatial queries may not require identical computation. We study whether fixed-budget combinations of vision blocks can be skipped without fine-tuning. A shared K-block route skips one searched set of exactly K blocks for every question, while a capability-specific K-block policy selects one same-size route using a known capability label. We introduce a source-balanced evolutionary search and compare it with independent ranking, contiguous removal, and random routes at matched budgets. Experiments use Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct, SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct, and an 876-example image-disjoint selection split. Search transfers across architectures: on SmolVLM2, the searched shared four-block route beats independent construction by 4.91 percentage points. Capability specialization is less stable. On Qwen, the six-block capability policy beats the shared route by 2.17 points, driven by a 7.10-point OCR gain. On sealed IIIT5K, however, the SmolVLM2 OCR-specific route trails its shared route by 13.6 points. Combinatorial search reliably improves route construction, but capability labels do not define universally transferable vision pathways.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-spatial_3d_reasoning'></div>

## 空间与 3D 多模态推理 [[Back]](#toc)

空间推理、3D 视觉定位、具身推理、几何推理。

### [116] [OrientSAM: Mitigating Camera-Centric Shortcut in Multimodal Spatial Reasoning via Orientation-Aware Spatial Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17657)
*Wenxiao Fan, Hang Yin, Kan Li*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OrientSAM 提出一种朝向感知的空间对齐框架，通过注入显式朝向信息（朝向感知 token + 傅里叶角度编码）和课程学习策略，缓解多模态大模型在空间推理中依赖相机中心线索的捷径行为，在 Spatial-MM、ViewSpatial 和 3DSRBench 上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在需要视角变换的空间推理任务中表现不佳，往往依赖相机中心线索而非从参考物体视角进行推理，导致在非相机参考设置下出现系统性错误。论文发现物体朝向是这种相机中心捷径行为的关键因素。

Method: 提出 OrientSAM 框架：1）通过朝向感知 token 和傅里叶角度编码将显式朝向信息注入多模态表示；2）采用课程学习策略逐步提升视角感知推理能力；3）构建空间数据生成管线，从大规模图像中生成朝向感知的空间监督数据。

Result: 在 Spatial-MM、ViewSpatial 和 3DSRBench 三个基准上，OrientSAM 一致优于强基线方法，尤其在非相机视角、以人为中心和朝向敏感的任务上提升显著。

Conclusion: 显式的朝向建模对于缓解相机中心捷径行为、实现更鲁棒的异中心（allocentric）空间推理至关重要，为多模态模型的空间推理能力提升提供了有效方向。

Classification reason: 论文关注多模态大模型的空间推理（spatial reasoning），特别是视角变换和朝向感知推理，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与 3D 多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses camera-centric shortcut in spatial reasoning but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: spatial reasoning, multimodal large language model, orientation-aware token, camera-centric shortcut, curriculum learning

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still struggle with spatial reasoning that requires perspective transformation. In particular, they often rely on camera-centric cues rather than reasoning from the reference object's viewpoint, leading to systematic errors in non-camera reference settings. In this paper, we first analyze this failure mode and show that object orientation is a key factor underlying such camera-centric shortcut behavior. To address this issue, we propose OrientSAM, an orientation-aware spatial alignment framework for multimodal models. OrientSAM injects explicit orientation information into multimodal representations through orientation-aware tokens and Fourier-based angle encoding, and further adopts a curriculum learning strategy to progressively improve perspective-aware reasoning. In addition, we build a spatial data construction pipeline to generate orientation-aware spatial supervision from large-scale images. Experiments on Spatial-MM, ViewSpatial, and 3DSRBench show that OrientSAM consistently outperforms strong baselines, especially on non-camera-view, person-centric, and orientation-sensitive tasks. The results further demonstrate that explicit orientation modeling is important for mitigating camera-centric shortcut behavior and enabling more robust allocentric spatial reasoning in multimodal models.

</details>


### [117] [SaaF: Scene-Specific Ambiguity-Aware 3D Language Fields towards Interactive Real-World Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16309)
*Yuga Yano, Daiju Kanaoka, Hakaru Tamukoh, Yasutomo Kawanishi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (67.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SaaF 提出了一种基于3D高斯泼溅的场景特定模糊感知语言场，通过度量学习构建实例判别且模糊感知的统一特征空间，用于交互式真实世界物体检索，在提升检索精度的同时能够检测用户查询的模糊性并主动请求澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在复杂真实环境中进行交互式物体检索时，现有3D语言场方法存在两个局限：(1) 特征压缩导致相似物体判别性下降；(2) 对模糊查询处理能力差，检索结果不稳定或错误。因此需要一种既能精细区分物体又能感知查询模糊性的方法。

Method: SaaF 基于3D高斯泼溅构建3D语言场，引入度量学习策略：(i) 将同一物体多视角图像特征拉近，增强实例级视觉判别性；(ii) 对每个跟踪物体生成多个文本标签（包括模糊描述），联合训练以学习模糊与具体特征之间的语义关系，使系统能估计查询模糊度并在需要时主动请求澄清。

Result: 实验结果表明，SaaF 在开放词汇设置下相比先前方法提升了检索准确率，并能稳健地检测和处理用户文本查询中的模糊性。

Conclusion: SaaF 通过度量学习和模糊感知训练，有效解决了3D语言场中物体判别性不足和模糊查询处理困难的问题，为服务机器人在复杂真实环境中的交互式物体检索提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于3D高斯泼溅的语言场用于交互式物体检索，涉及3D场景理解、自然语言查询与空间定位，属于空间与3D多模态推理方向。该论文与[空间与 3D 多模态推理]（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on 3D scene-specific object retrieval, which is not a core priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with the user's high-weight continuous language/multimodal or video generation directions. Signals: 3D language fields, Gaussian Splatting, interactive object retrieval, ambiguity-aware, metric learning

Abstract: We propose Scene-specific Ambiguity-aware 3D Language Fields (SaaF), a novel Gaussian Splatting-based 3D language field designed for interactive object retrieval in a given real-world scene. Interactive object retrieval using natural language is a crucial capability for service robots operating in complex real-world environments. While recent 3D language field methods for object retrieval establish associations between rendered pixels and autoencoder-compressed CLIP features, they suffer from two limitations: (1) reduced discriminability among similar objects due to feature compression, and (2) poor handling of ambiguous queries, often resulting in unstable or incorrect retrieval. To address these limitations, SaaF introduces a metric learning strategy to construct a unified feature space that is both instance-discriminative and ambiguity-aware. (i) To enhance instance-level visual discrimination, SaaF employs metric learning that pulls image features from multiple viewpoints of the same object closer together in the feature space. (ii) To establish ambiguity awareness, the model jointly trains on multiple text labels generated by the proposed method from each tracked object image sequence, including ambiguous descriptions, to learn the semantic relationships between ambiguous and specific features in a target scene. This feature space enables fine-grained visual understanding while allowing the system to estimate query ambiguity and interactively request clarification when needed. Experimental results demonstrate that SaaF not only improves retrieval accuracy over previous methods but also robustly detects and handles ambiguity in the user text queries under open-vocabulary settings.

</details>


### [118] [ConsiSpace: Learning Geometric Consistency Matters for Video Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17599)
*Ting Huang, Zhenyu Zhang, Wenyuan Huang, Jian Yang, Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ConsiSpace 提出了一种几何一致性感知框架，通过构建几何一致性记忆（GCM）和统一一致性自监督强化学习（UC-SSRL），增强多模态大模型在视频空间推理任务中对空间关系的稳定推理能力，在三个基准上平均提升12.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在视频空间推理中偏向语义理解，难以从冗余的视频观测中聚合一致的空间证据，导致推理不稳定或低效。需要一种显式利用几何一致性的方法来提升跨视角的空间推理能力。

Method: 提出ConsiSpace框架，包含：(1) 几何一致性记忆（GCM），融合隐式证据token和显式几何线索，通过高效组织策略紧凑保留任务相关空间证据；(2) 统一一致性自监督强化学习（UC-SSRL），在SFT后使用答案一致性、度量一致性和拓扑一致性奖励来提升跨视角稳定性。

Result: 在VSI-Bench、OSI-Bench和MMSI-Video-Bench三个空间推理基准上，ConsiSpace相比最强基线平均提升12.6分，验证了几何一致性对视频空间推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 将空间一致性同时作为证据组织原则和显式学习信号，能够显著提升MLLM在视频空间推理中的表现，为导航感知和长视频问答等任务提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注视频空间推理（video spatial reasoning），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与3D多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，该子主题涵盖空间推理、几何推理等，与论文的几何一致性空间推理高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong geometric consistency method for video spatial reasoning, but only tangentially touches priority continuous multimodal and world model topics. Signals: video spatial reasoning, geometric consistency memory, self-supervised reinforcement learning, multimodal large language model, VSI-Bench

Abstract: Video spatial reasoning is essential for navigation-oriented perception and long-video question answering, where models must infer spatial relations across long horizons under changing viewpoints. However, existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain largely semantic-centric, and often fail to reliably aggregate consistent spatial evidence from redundant video observations, leading to inefficient or unstable reasoning. To address these issues, we propose ConsiSpace, a geometry-consistency-aware framework for geometry-sensitive video spatial reasoning that turns spatial consistency into both an evidence organization principle and an explicit post-SFT learning signal. We build a geometry-consistent memory (GCM) including implicit evidence tokens and explicit geometric cues, and leverage efficient organization strategies to compactly preserve task-related spatial evidence. Furthermore, we utilize unified consistency self-supervised reinforcement learning (UC-SSRL) after supervised fine-tuning to improve cross-view stability, with answer-, metric-, and topology-consistency rewards. Extensive experiments on three spatial-reasoning benchmarks, VSI-Bench, OSI-Bench, and MMSI-Video-Bench, show consistent gains, improving the average score by 12.6 points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [119] [Anticipate Before Acting: Future-State-Conditioned Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18042)
*Lingfeng Zhang, Zhanguang Zhang, Liheng Ma, Tongtong Cao, Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出未来状态条件视觉语言导航（FSC-VLN），通过在训练时引入未来视觉潜空间对齐作为额外监督信号，使因果VLM在推理时无需未来图像即可受益于未来信息，在R2R基准上提升了导航性能，尤其在长程任务中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的行为克隆训练仅监督下一步动作，没有显式训练策略状态去预测未来的视觉结果。作者首先通过诊断实验验证了未来图像作为特权输入有助于当前动作选择，进而提出在推理时无法获取未来图像的情况下，能否通过压缩的未来视觉潜空间作为训练监督来利用未来信息。

Method: 提出FSC-VLN框架，在因果VLM中引入一个未来查询（future-query）token，通过一个仅在训练时使用的目标分支，将该token的隐藏状态对齐到Δ步之后的冻结视觉embedding。训练完成后移除该目标分支，推理时仅保留未来查询token及其训练好的表征能力。同时采用双查询设计，将未来查询与动作查询分离。

Result: 在R2R val-unseen上，FSC-VLN在两种训练数据设置下均提升了SR/OSR/SPL指标，长程任务上增益更大。消融实验验证了双查询设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过训练时对齐未来视觉潜空间，可以在不依赖未来图像的情况下提升VLN策略的导航性能，为具身导航中利用未来信息提供了一种有效且实用的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言导航（VLN），属于具身智能中的空间推理与视觉定位任务，与 [CANONICAL] 空间与 3D 多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper's focus on future-state conditioning for VLN is tangentially related to continuous multimodal models but lacks direct alignment with your top-weighted priority subtopics. Signals: vision-language navigation, future-state conditioning, causal VLM, privileged input, long-horizon tasks

Abstract: End-to-end vision-language navigation (VLN) with causal vision-language models can map instructions and egocentric observations directly to actions, but standard behavior cloning supervises only the next action and does not explicitly train the policy state to be predictive of future visual outcomes. We first ask a diagnostic question: if the policy is given an expert-trajectory future image as privileged input at training and testing time, is that additional visual evidence useful for choosing the current action? (These expert-trajectory future images are unavailable at test time in real deployment, so we use this setting only as a privileged-input diagnostic.) The answer is yes; this sanity check shows that future observations can provide rich, actionable cues. We then ask a deployable question: without accessing future images at inference, can we still benefit from future information by using a compressed future visual latent only as training supervision? We propose Future-State-Conditioned VLN (FSC-VLN), which adds a future-query token and aligns its hidden state to a frozen visual embedding $\Delta$ steps ahead via a training-only target branch that is removed after training. On R2R val-unseen, FSC-VLN improves SR/OSR/SPL over a StreamVLN-style baseline under two training-data regimes, with larger gains on long-horizon episodes; ablations further support the dual-query design (separating future and action queries).

</details>


### [120] [VGOcc: Learning Visual-Geometric Gaussians for Vision-Centric 3D Driving Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18078)
*Junhong Lin, Xianda Guo, Kangli Wang, Yuqi Ye, Xiaoyu Liang, Yanlun Peng, Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VGOcc 提出了一种视觉-几何高斯表示方法，利用基础模型提取的视觉和几何线索进行高斯原语初始化与细化，实现基于环视图像的3D驾驶场景语义占用预测，在nuScenes上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯原语的3D占用预测方法主要依赖图像域特征，缺乏显式的几何信息用于体素推理。作者观察到有效的高斯占用建模不仅需要稀疏原语，还需要更丰富的几何和语义学习线索。

Method: 提出VGOcc框架：(1) Visual-Geometric Gaussian Birth：从射线深度假设中形成空间平衡的高斯中心，视觉语义特征初始化原语属性；(2) Pose-Aware Feature Learning：将基础模型token与相机嵌入和校准射线信息结合，聚合相邻视图特征用于高斯细化；(3) Gaussian Decoder：用位姿感知特征细化初始高斯并渲染为语义占用。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，VGOcc在视觉-only 3D占用预测任务中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 融合基础模型的视觉和几何线索能够显著提升高斯原语在3D语义占用预测中的表现，验证了视觉-几何联合建模的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于环视图像的3D驾驶场景语义占用预测（occupancy prediction），属于空间3D推理与视觉感知范畴，与[空间与 3D 多模态推理]（spatial_3d_reasoning）高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on 3D driving occupancy prediction, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with the user's key interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: 3D occupancy prediction, vision-centric driving, Gaussian primitives, foundation model cues, nuScenes SOTA

Abstract: Vision-only occupancy prediction requires recovering a semantic 3D occupancy field from calibrated surround-view images, where each view provides observations with ambiguous depth along camera rays. Existing methods have progressed from dense structured representations to sparse Gaussian primitives, improving the efficiency of 3D scene representation. However, Gaussian learning still relies primarily on image domain features, which provide limited explicit geometric information for volumetric reasoning. Our key observation is that effective Gaussian occupancy modeling requires not only sparse primitives, but also richer geometric and semantic learning cues. In this paper, we propose VGOcc, which learns visual and geometric cues from foundation models for Gaussian modeling. VGOcc incorporates these cues into primitive initialization and refinement, yielding a representation termed Visual-Geometric Gaussians tailored to semantic occupancy prediction. Specifically, we propose Visual-Geometric Gaussian Birth to form spatially balanced Gaussian centers from ray depth hypotheses, while visual semantic features initialize primitive attributes. Next, we design Pose-Aware Feature Learning to combine foundation tokens with camera embeddings and calibrated ray information. Features from neighboring views are then aggregated at projected 3D locations for each Gaussian refinement stage. Finally, Gaussian decoder refines birth Gaussians with pose-aware features and renders them into semantic occupancy. Experiments on nuScenes demonstrate that VGOcc achieves state-of-the-art performance in vision-only 3D occupancy prediction. Codes will be available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-unified_multimodal_model'></div>

## 统一多模态模型 [[Back]](#toc)

理解与生成一体化、统一 token 表征、Any-to-Any 多模态模型。

### [121] [Think, Plan, Paint: Layout-Aware Reasoning for Controllable Image Generation in Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16409)
*Junhao Liu, Jian-Wei Zhang, Tao Huang, Miles Yang, Zhao Zhong, Liefeng Bo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ATLAS 是一个统一 MLLM 框架，通过"思考-规划-绘制"三阶段范式实现可控图像生成。它使用布局作为共享表征连接推理、规划与渲染三个阶段，并通过强化学习对齐提升规划到图像的忠实度。在 7B 和 80B 规模上均达到 SOTA，并引入了 ATLAS-Reasoning 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一 MLLM 在可控图像生成中难以遵循复杂的空间指令和逻辑约束，缺乏对空间需求的推理能力和显式的布局规划能力。

Method: 提出 ATLAS 框架，采用"Think, Plan, Paint"三阶段范式：(1) Think 阶段：MLLM 推理空间需求并生成布局描述；(2) Plan 阶段：将布局描述转化为显式的物体排列（bounding boxes）；(3) Paint 阶段：基于布局生成最终图像。使用布局作为三阶段间的共享表征。引入基于强化学习的布局对齐（reinforcement-learning-based layout alignment）提升 plan-to-image 忠实度。在 7B 和 80B 两个规模上实现。

Result: 在图像生成基准上达到统一 MLLM 中的 SOTA，相比现有基于布局的统一 MLLM 平均提升 65.31%；在空间相关任务上相比基模型平均提升 23.06%。同时支持指令引导编辑和多模态定位。

Conclusion: ATLAS 通过显式的"思考-规划-绘制"范式和布局共享表征，有效提升了统一 MLLM 在可控图像生成中的空间指令遵循能力，验证了推理-规划-生成三阶段分离设计的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ATLAS，一个统一多模态大模型（MLLM），将视觉理解与可控图像生成统一在"思考-规划-绘制"框架下，属于理解与生成一体化方向。该论文同时涉及多模态生成（可控图像生成），因此匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。在 subtopic 层面，论文属于"统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model），该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下为 [CANONICAL] 条目，且论文核心贡献正是统一理解与生成。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable generation in unified MLLMs with a novel reasoning-planning-rendering paradigm and layout alignment, highly relevant to your priority subtopics. Signals: unified multimodal model, controllable generation, layout-aware reasoning, reinforcement learning alignment, ATLAS-Reasoning benchmark

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer a promising paradigm for unifying visual understanding and generation, yet they still struggle to follow complex spatial instructions and logical constraints in controllable image generation. To address this gap, we present ATLAS, a unified framework that equips MLLMs with a human-like "Think, Plan, and Paint" paradigm. We adopt layout as the shared representation that connects the three stages, enabling the model to reason about spatial requirements, plan explicit object arrangements, and render the final image. We further improve plan-to-image fidelity with reinforcement-learning-based layout alignment. We instantiate ATLAS at 7B and 80B scales, achieving state-of-the-art performance among MLLMs on image generation benchmarks and an average 65.31% improvement over existing layout-based unified MLLMs. On spatially related tasks, ATLAS obtains an average 23.06% gain over the base models. Through the same layout interface, ATLAS also supports instruction-guided editing and multimodal grounding. We further introduce ATLAS-Reasoning, a benchmark for evaluating generation under complex spatial instructions.

</details>


### [122] [STBridge: Shared-Target Alignment for Bridging Understanding and Generation in UMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17140)
*Ye Wang, Hongjun Wang, Hao Fang, Tongyuan Bai, Zuwei Long, Peixian Chen, Wei Liu, Weibo Gu, Xing Sun, Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现统一多模态模型（UMM）存在理解-生成对齐鸿沟：模型能正确描述目标但生成不一致的编辑结果。为此提出 STBridge，一种共享目标对齐框架，通过"先对齐后优化"策略（SFT + 强化学习）使理解与生成共享同一目标语义信息流，在图像编辑任务上显著缩小了描述与生成之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型虽然实现了架构上的统一，但理解与生成之间仍存在语义不一致问题——模型可能正确描述目标图像，却生成与描述不符的编辑结果。这种"理解-生成对齐鸿沟"表明仅靠架构统一不足以实现真正的语义统一，需要专门的对齐策略。

Method: 提出 STBridge（Shared-Target Alignment）框架，核心思想是通过共享目标状态连接理解与生成。具体采用"align-then-optimize"两阶段策略：第一阶段使用监督微调（SFT）建立共享目标通道，使目标描述与视觉生成共享同一信息流；第二阶段使用序列强化学习进一步优化以目标为中心的协调。在图像编辑任务中，目标描述表达期望的视觉结果，编辑图像实现该结果，替代了分离的任务特定路径。

Result: 在视觉理解、图像生成和图像编辑等多个基准上，STBridge 相比初始模型取得了一致的性能提升。对齐分析证实 STBridge 缩小了模型描述与生成之间的差距，特别是在细粒度实体、属性、空间关系和局部细节方面改进显著。

Conclusion: 共享目标对齐是一种有效的后训练策略，能够桥接统一多模态模型中理解与生成之间的鸿沟。架构统一本身不足以实现语义统一，需要专门的对齐训练来确保理解与生成在语义上保持一致。

Classification reason: 该论文研究统一多模态模型（UMM）中理解与生成的对齐问题，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）这一现有子主题，该子主题在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 两个方向下均有收录。论文聚焦于通过共享目标对齐桥接理解与生成，完全符合该子主题的定义。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the critical understanding-generation alignment gap in unified multimodal models, a top priority subtopic. Signals: unified multimodal model alignment, shared-target alignment framework, align-then-optimize strategy, image editing consistency, post-training for UMMs

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to integrate visual understanding and generation within a single architecture, but architectural unification alone does not ensure semantic consistency. A model may describe the intended target correctly while generating an inconsistent edit. This exposes an understanding-generation alignment gap: linguistic and visual outputs live in different spaces, yet should be governed by the same target semantics. We study this gap in image editing, where an instruction defines a target state that can be both described and visually realized. Given a source image and an edit instruction, we compare a UMM's target caption with its edited image to test whether the two outputs converge on the same result. Our analysis shows that existing UMMs remain weakly aligned, especially for fine-grained entities, attributes, spatial relations, and local details, indicating that semantic unification is not achieved by architecture alone. To bridge this gap, we propose STBridge, a shared-target alignment framework that connects understanding and generation through a common target state. Here the target caption expresses the desired visual result, while the edited image realizes it visually, replacing separate task-specific paths with a shared information flow from target expression to target realization. STBridge follows an align-then-optimize strategy: supervised fine-tuning first establishes the shared-target channel, and sequential reinforcement learning further refines target-centered coordination. Across visual understanding, image generation, and image editing benchmarks, STBridge consistently improves over the initialization model. Alignment analysis confirms that STBridge narrows the gap between what the model describes and what it generates, demonstrating shared-target alignment as an effective post-training strategy for bridging understanding and generation in UMMs.

</details>

### [123] [LookME: Lookup-Based Multimodal Embeddings for Layer Injection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16305)
*Zeyu Xu, Xingzhong Hou, Pengkai Guo, Siling Lin, Xiao Xu, Menghua Zhai, Haoyu Chen, Yunke Zhang, Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (83.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LookME 是首个将基于查找的嵌入增强方法扩展到多模态 VLM 的框架，通过层级化粗到细的查找策略和稀疏注入机制，在 ROM 中存储大规模嵌入表并支持按需加载，在资源受限场景下提升了 VLM 的性能与效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Per-Layer Embedding (PLE) 架构通过外部嵌入表扩展模型容量，但仅支持文本 ID 检索，无法有效处理多模态嵌入中丰富的视觉信息。同时，稠密或稀疏 MoE 模型在资源受限环境中面临高内存占用或高延迟的困境。

Method: 提出 LookME 框架：(1) 层级化两级查找方法，从场景级到场景内基元级进行粗到细的嵌入检索；(2) 稀疏注入策略，自适应地优先选择关键多模态嵌入，并支持相邻层间嵌入表复用。

Result: 在多个视觉基准上，LookME 优于纯文本 PLE 风格方法，验证了基于查找的多模态嵌入增强的有效性。

Conclusion: LookME 首次将查找式嵌入增强引入多模态 VLM，通过层级查找和稀疏注入实现了更好的效率-模型大小-性能权衡，为资源受限环境下的 VLM 部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LookME，一种面向 VLM 的基于查找的多模态嵌入增强框架，属于视觉语言模型架构改进方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel lookup-based embedding injection for VLMs aligns with efficient inference and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: Efficient inference, Multimodal embedding lookup, Resource-constrained VLM, Coarse-to-fine retrieval, Sparse injection

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong progress in multimodal understanding. However, scaling dense or sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models to improve performance limits deployment in resource-constrained environments due to the trade-off between high memory usage from full loading and increased latency from on-demand loading. Recently, the Per-Layer Embedding (PLE) architecture addresses this by scaling models with large external embedding tables stored in ROM and performing lightweight lookup to retrieve relevant embeddings to enhance token representations. Nevertheless, existing PLE-style methods are primarily designed for text embeddings due to the convenience of ID-based retrieval, limiting their effectiveness in VLMs where multimodal embeddings contain richer information for visual tasks. In this paper, we propose LookME, the first framework that enables lookup-based enhancement for multimodal embeddings in VLMs while supporting partitioned storage and on-demand loading. To efficiently lookup arbitrary continuous multimodal embeddings from large-scale embedding tables, we propose a hierarchical two-level lookup method employing a coarse-to-fine strategy that performs lookups from the scene-level to the intra-scene primitive-level. Furthermore, we integrate the lookup method with a sparse injection strategy, which adaptively prioritizes critical embeddings over voluminous multimodal embeddings within layers, and facilitates embedding table reuse across neighboring layers, improving the trade-off among efficiency, model size, and performance. Experiments on multiple visual benchmarks show that LookME outperforms text-only PLE-style methods, validating the effectiveness of lookup-based multimodal embedding enhancement.

</details>


### [124] [PGN: Design and Implementation of a Vision-Language Navigation System Based on Pangu Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17806)
*Li Xian, Mingxi Li, Yizheng Wang, Yiming Shen, Qi Chen, Zhuoling Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (69.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出PGN（Pangu Navigator），一个基于OpenPangu-7B的离线视觉语言导航动作预测系统。采用两阶段训练：第一阶段通过Q-Former和MLP投影器对齐冻结的视觉编码器与语言骨干；第二阶段使用专家导航轨迹微调，结合五帧观测窗口、epoch相关时序采样和推理-动作输出格式。在500条专家轨迹上达到62.29%的归一化动作匹配率和100%的非空率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多模态大语言模型应用于视觉语言导航(VLN)任务面临视觉-语言对齐、紧凑时序输入、动作空间接地和目标硬件上稳定训练等挑战，本文旨在设计一个实用的VLN动作预测系统来解决这些问题。

Method: 两阶段训练方法。第一阶段(PGMM)：冻结EVA-ViT-G/14视觉编码器和OpenPangu-7B语言骨干，仅训练Q-Former和两层MLP投影器实现视觉-语言对齐。第二阶段(PGN)：冻结对齐后的视觉通路，使用五帧观测窗口、epoch相关时序采样和推理-动作输出格式，在专家导航轨迹上更新三个结构token嵌入和LoRA适配器。实现上采用混合精度计算、选择性FP32计算和DeepSpeed ZeRO-2在八块Ascend 910B NPU上训练。

Result: 在500条专家轨迹的教师强制开环评估中，V9版本达到62.29%的归一化动作匹配率(NAM)和100.00%的非空率(NER)。

Conclusion: PGN成功构建了一个基于盘古多模态基础模型的VLN动作预测系统，在离线评估中取得了良好效果。但当前评估仅衡量离线专家动作对齐而非闭环导航成功率，误差累积、路径效率和目标完成度的评估留待未来工作。

Classification reason: 该论文基于OpenPangu-7B构建视觉语言导航(VLN)系统，属于视觉语言模型在具体任务上的应用，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a specific VLN system implementation with limited novelty and no closed-loop evaluation, far from user's priority subtopics. Signals: Vision-Language Navigation, OpenPangu-7B, offline action prediction, no closed-loop results, low alignment with continuous language/multimodal models or video generation

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to interpret a natural-language instruction and predict actions from temporally ordered visual observations. Adapting a multimodal large language model to VLN requires visual-language alignment, compact temporal inputs, action-space grounding, and stable training on the target hardware. This technical report presents PGN (Pangu Navigator), an offline VLN action-prediction system built on OpenPangu-7B. Training proceeds in two stages. First, PGMM aligns a frozen EVA-ViT-G/14 vision encoder with the frozen language backbone by training a Q-Former and a two-layer MLP projector. Second, PGN adapts the aligned model to expert navigation trajectories using five-observation windows, epoch-dependent temporal sampling, and a reasoning-then-action output format; this stage freezes the aligned visual pathway and updates three structural-token embeddings and LoRA adapters. The implementation combines mixed-precision computation, selective FP32 computation, and DeepSpeed ZeRO-2 on eight Ascend 910B NPUs. Under teacher-forced, open-loop evaluation on 500 held-out expert trajectories, V9 reports a 62.29% Normalized Action Match (NAM) and a 100.00% Non-empty Rate (NER). These metrics quantify offline expert-action alignment rather than closed-loop navigation success; evaluating error accumulation, path efficiency, and goal completion remains future work.

</details>

### [125] [Localization-Infused Vision-Language Semantic Fusion for Text-Guided Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16327)
*Songyue Han, Mingye Zou, Shuchang Ye, Lei Bi, Mingyuan Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 LoG 框架，通过多尺度目标定位任务显式提取文本报告中的位置信息，并在特征、注意力、损失三个层面实现定位引导的视觉-语言语义融合，用于文本引导的医学图像分割，在三个基准数据集上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导的医学图像分割方法通常仅通过预训练文本编码器隐式提取文本语义，并通过简单的图像-文本特征融合进行集成，未能显式捕获文本报告中包含的目标位置等关键信息，也缺乏多层级信息融合策略，限制了关键文本语义的提取与整合。

Method: 提出 LoG（Localization-infused vision-language fusion framework），包含三个核心模块：(i) 定位引导的特征融合——将位置相关语义直接注入视觉特征；(ii) 定位门控注意力融合——利用多尺度定位预测重加权关键区域；(iii) 定位约束的损失融合——基于目标定位的空间一致性监督分割。三者联合实现三级定位引导的语义融合。

Result: 在 QaTa-COV19、MosMedData+ 和 Kvasir-SEG 三个基准数据集上分别达到 91.59%、80.71% 和 94.59% 的 Dice 分数，一致优于现有最先进的医学图像分割方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模文本报告中的目标位置信息并实现多层级视觉-语言语义融合，能有效提升文本引导的医学图像分割性能，验证了定位信息在跨模态医学图像理解中的关键作用。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本引导的医学图像分割，属于视觉语言理解与推理任务，与多模态理解方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）相关，因为医学图像分割可视为一种细粒度的视觉理解任务，且涉及文本语义与视觉特征的融合。该子主题是[CANONICAL]条目，优先选用。

Importance reason: Medical segmentation is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords, and the method does not align with the user's focus on continuous/multimodal models or generation. Signals: Medical image segmentation, text-guided segmentation, localization-infused fusion, not autoregressive or generative, not continuous language model

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is essential for modern computer-aided medicine. Recently, text-guided segmentation has shown promise by incorporating clinician-formulated textual reports as semantic guidance for image segmentation. These reports describe target appearance, location, and neighboring anatomy, providing explicit guidance for localization and delineation. Existing text-guided segmentation methods typically extract textual semantics implicitly through a pretrained text encoder and then integrate vision-language semantics via straightforward image-text feature fusion. However, these methods do not explicitly capture target-oriented information embedded in textual reports, particularly target location, and do not explore multi-level information fusion strategies beyond basic feature-level fusion, limiting the extraction and integration of critical textual semantics. In this study, we propose LoG, a localization-infused vision-language fusion framework for text-guided medical image segmentation. By jointly performing multi-scale target localization tasks, LoG explicitly captures target-oriented vision-language semantics and enables three-level localization-infused semantic fusion: (i) localization-guided feature fusion that directly infuses location-relevant semantics into visual features, (ii) localization-gated attention fusion that redirects multi-scale localization predictions to reinforce critical regions, and (iii) localization-constrained loss fusion that supervises segmentation based on spatial consistency with target localization. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets, involving three medical imaging modalities with paired textual reports, demonstrate that LoG achieves Dice scores of 91.59%, 80.71%, and 94.59% on QaTa-COV19, MosMedData+, and Kvasir-SEG, respectively, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art medical image segmentation methods.

</details>


### [126] [O-VAD: Industrial Video Anomaly Detection through Object-Centric Tracking and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18142)
*Mei Yuan, Qi Long, Qifeng Wu, Zhenyang Li, Yizhou Zhao, Lei Wang, Yang Liu, Min Xu*

Main category: cs.MA, cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出O-VAD，一个无需训练、无需领域知识的智能体框架，用于工业视频异常检测。通过目标跟踪捕获物体时空动态和状态演化，再对物体级时序轨迹进行推理以识别异常物体和事件，在三个IVAD数据集上超越现有VLM和传统VAD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在通用领域异常检测中表现良好，但在工业场景中因物体变换复杂、物理约束严格而性能下降。传统VAD方法需要针对正常片段重训练或注入领域知识，缺乏泛化性。受人类质检员观察物体状态演化的方式启发，作者希望构建一个无需训练、无需领域知识的通用工业异常检测框架。

Method: O-VAD是一个训练无关的智能体框架，包含两个核心阶段：(1) 目标跟踪阶段——利用VLM检测和跟踪视频中的物体，捕获物体随时间变化的空间-时间动态和状态演化；(2) 推理阶段——对物体级时序状态轨迹进行推理，识别异常物体并在对应帧中定位。整个流程无需在正常片段上重训练，也无需注入领域知识作为上下文。

Result: 在三个IVAD数据集上的实验表明，O-VAD在异常检测性能上超越了前沿VLM、其他智能体框架以及在各自数据集上微调的传统VAD方法，同时能提供关于异常过程和类型的可解释报告。

Conclusion: O-VAD通过目标跟踪和时序推理实现了无需训练、无需领域知识的工业视频异常检测，验证了基于VLM的智能体框架在工业场景中的有效性，为工业质检提供了可解释的异常检测方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于VLM的工业视频异常检测框架，核心是目标跟踪与时序推理以识别异常，属于视觉问答与视频理解范畴。虽然涉及视频时序推理，但更侧重于对工业场景中物体状态演化的问答式异常检测（检测异常物体和事件），与multimodal_vqa_doc（视觉问答）方向最为匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on industrial video anomaly detection, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: Training-free agentic framework, Object-centric tracking and reasoning, Outperforms VLM and VAD methods, Industrial domain specific, No domain knowledge required

Abstract: Industrial Video Anomaly Detection (IVAD) aims to identify anomalous objects and events in an industrial process, which is crucial for modern manufacturing and quality control systems. Existing VLM-based anomaly reasoning methods are capable of detecting open-ended anomalies in general domains. However, their performance declines in industrial settings characterized by intricate object transformations, strict physics, and procedural constraints. To tackle the complexity of such interaction-intensive detection, we introduce a training-free agentic framework for anomaly detection free of domain-specific knowledge, emphasizing object state evolution like humans inspectors. It is designed to track spatial-temporal dynamics and underlying transformations of detected objects over time, and then reason over the object-wise temporal state trajectories to identify abnormal objects in grounded frames. Our method overcomes limitations of prior approaches that rely on retraining on normal clips or injecting domain knowledge as context for test-time inference. Extensive experiments on three IVAD datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms frontier VLMs, agentic frameworks, and traditional VAD methods fine-tuned on the respective datasets, while providing interpretable reports over anomaly processes and types.

</details>


### [127] [Measuring and Improving Complex-Atomic Answer Consistency in Endoscopic VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17834)
*Yuhao Liu, Cheng Zhao, Guanghui Yue*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (47.46) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 EndoCA，一个用于评估内窥镜 VQA 中复杂-原子答案一致性的基准，并引入 ASR（原子支持调和）机制，通过模型生成的原子答案作为上下文前提进行答案修正和一致性引导的选择性回答，无需训练即可提升复杂-原子答案一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜 VQA 中越来越多地出现组合多个内窥镜答案组件的复杂问题，但现有评测仅关注复杂答案的准确性，忽略了复杂答案与对应原子答案之间的一致性。模型可能在复杂问题上答对，却在相关的原子问题上答错，暴露出答案一致性问题。

Method: 1) 构建 EndoCA 基准，包含 EndoCA-Core（评估常见复杂-原子模式）和 EndoCA-Diagnostic（控制问题复杂度进行诊断分析）两个子集。2) 提出 Atomic-Support Reconciliation (ASR)，一种无需训练的机制：ASR-Revise 利用模型生成的原子答案作为上下文前提来修正复杂答案；ASR-Selective 通过一致性引导让模型在不可靠情况下选择弃答。

Result: 在 11 个 VLM 上的评估显示，部分模型在复杂答案上准确率较高，但原子答案准确率和复杂-原子一致性显著较低。ASR-Revise 在四个公开模型上提升了复杂-原子一致性，同时复杂答案准确率变化不大；ASR-Selective 通过允许模型弃答，提升了已答案例的准确率。

Conclusion: EndoCA 揭示了内窥镜 VLM 中复杂-原子答案不一致的问题，ASR 提供了一种无需训练的轻量级解决方案，为内窥镜 VQA 的可靠性评估和改进提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究内窥镜视觉问答（Endoscopic VQA），属于视觉问答（VQA）范畴，与现有 canonical 子主题 "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）直接相关。

Importance reason: Niche medical VQA consistency task with no overlap with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: endoscopic VQA, complex-atomic consistency, training-free reconciliation, medical domain, no continuous language model or video generation

Abstract: Endoscopic visual question answering (VQA) increasingly asks complex questions that combine several endoscopic answer components rather than isolated factual queries. Such complex answers may be scored as correct even when the same model fails on associated atomic questions. We introduce EndoCA, a paired complex-atomic answer consistency benchmark for evaluating whether complex answers remain consistent with same-image atomic answers. EndoCA contains two suites: EndoCA-Core evaluates compact question-complexity patterns commonly seen in practical endoscopic VQA, and EndoCA-Diagnostic supports controlled analysis across increasing question complexity. We evaluate 11 VLMs spanning open, medical, endoscopy-adapted, and closed-source models on EndoCA. Some VLMs achieve high complex-answer accuracy, yet their atomic-answer accuracy and complex-atomic answer consistency remain substantially lower. To reduce this complex-atomic inconsistency, we introduce Atomic-Support Reconciliation (ASR), a training-free mechanism that uses model-generated atomic answers as contextual premises for answer revision and consistency-guided selective answering. On four selected publicly available models, ASR-Revise improves paired complex-atomic correctness with modest changes in complex-answer accuracy, while ASR-Selective improves accuracy on answered cases by allowing the model to abstain from less reliable cases. Together, EndoCA and ASR provide a consistency-aware benchmark and a training-free mechanism for answer reconciliation and selective answering in endoscopic VQA.

</details>


### [128] [Sparse Evidence Can Suffice: Agentic Evidence Seeking for Multimodal Video Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18080)
*Haochen Zhao, Yongxiu Xu, Xinkui Lin, Dong Xie, Jiarui Lu, Yuqi Qian, Yubin Wang, Hongbo Xu, Gaopeng Gou*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SIEVE框架，将多模态视频虚假信息检测解耦为证据获取与验证两个阶段：一个证据搜索智能体主动收集稀疏但关键的证据，构建紧凑的证据包，再由验证器判断真伪。通过监督轨迹学习和证据感知强化学习训练智能体，在多个基准上取得最优性能，并提供可解释的证据链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的视频虚假信息往往具有稀疏且组合式的证据结构——可靠判断仅依赖少数几个耦合线索，而大部分视频内容贡献有限。现有方法对整个视频进行一次性全量推理，引入了大量冗余信息，反而可能掩盖关键证据。因此需要将证据获取与验证解耦，先识别稀疏的决策相关线索，再基于获取的证据判断真伪。

Method: 提出SIEVE框架，包含两个核心组件：1）证据搜索智能体（Evidence-Seeking Agent），主动探索多模态证据（视频帧、文本、音频等），构建紧凑的证据包；2）验证器（Verifier），基于证据包判断视频真伪。智能体通过两阶段训练：先使用监督证据搜索轨迹进行行为克隆，再使用证据感知的强化学习目标（evidence-aware RL）进行优化，该目标鼓励获取信息性证据，同时惩罚不必要或无效的交互。

Result: 在多个视频虚假信息检测基准上，SIEVE一致优于所有基线方法。实验表明，仅使用紧凑的证据包即可实现可靠的验证，验证了稀疏证据足以支撑准确判断的假设。此外，证据获取过程提供了显式、可检查的证据链，提升了检测的透明度和可解释性。

Conclusion: 将多模态视频虚假信息检测解耦为证据搜索与验证两个阶段是有效的，稀疏但关键的证据足以支撑可靠判断。SIEVE框架不仅提升了检测性能，还通过显式的证据链增强了模型的可解释性和可信度，为多模态虚假信息检测提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态视频虚假信息检测，本质上是视频理解与问答任务（判断视频真伪），与多模态理解方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题高度相关。虽然该子主题名称侧重VQA和文档，但视频虚假信息检测可视为一种特殊的视频VQA/理解任务，且该子主题是[CANONICAL]条目，优先复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on video misinformation detection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal video misinformation detection, Evidence-seeking agent, Sparse evidence, Reinforcement learning, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal video misinformation detection is commonly formulated as a holistic video-understanding task, where the entire video and its associated content are processed and judged in a single pass. However, real-world misinformation often exhibits a sparse and compositional evidence structure: a reliable decision may depend on only a few coupled clues, while most video content contributes limited additional information. Exhaustive multimodal reasoning may therefore introduce substantial redundancy and obscure decisive evidence. This motivates decoupling evidence acquisition from verification: first identifying sparse, decision-relevant clues and then judging veracity based on the acquired evidence. Accordingly, we propose SIEVE, a framework for Sparse Interactive Evidence Verification via Extraction in multimodal video misinformation detection. An evidence-seeking agent actively explores the available multimodal evidence and constructs a compact evidence package, which is then used by a verifier to determine veracity. The agent is trained with supervised evidence-seeking trajectories and an evidence-aware reinforcement learning objective that promotes informative evidence acquisition while discouraging unnecessary or invalid interactions. Experiments on multiple video misinformation benchmarks show that SIEVE consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines and supports reliable verification using compact evidence packages. Moreover, the resulting acquisition process provides an explicit and inspectable evidence trail, improving the transparency and groundedness of multimodal misinformation detection.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-video_understanding_temporal'></div>

## 视频理解与时序推理 [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [GMoT: Gated Motion-Aware Tokenization for Fine-Grained Micro-Gesture Video Reasoning with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16322)
*Taorui Wang, Wei Xia, Hui Ma, Zijia Song, Jiayu Zhang, Zeheng Wang, Yong Xu, Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出GMoT（门控运动感知分词模块），通过显式提取稀疏运动证据并注入多模态大语言模型，解决微手势视频中细微运动被静态外观淹没的问题。同时引入渐进式奖励引导策略优化和半监督标注流程，在iMiGUE和SMG数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微手势识别需要检测短暂、空间局部的细微运动，但多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在通用视频理解中擅长处理静态外观，难以捕捉细微运动学特征。现有方法往往依赖静态姿态先验，缺乏对运动证据的显式建模。

Method: 提出GMoT（Gated Motion-Aware Tokenization）模块，包含三个核心组件：1）空间加权池化动态聚焦动作相关区域；2）相邻帧时序差分提取精确运动能量；3）保守初始化的语义门控自适应融合运动线索到视觉流中。此外，引入渐进式奖励引导策略优化（progressive reward-guided policy refinement）和半监督标注流程生成解剖学聚焦的描述。

Result: 在iMiGUE数据集上达到67.32% Top-1准确率，在SMG数据集上达到73.11%，相比Qwen3-VL-8B基线分别提升+6.80和+3.11个百分点。同时提出Body-Region Grounding (BRG) Recall作为解剖学定位代理指标，并在跨域迁移实验中保持优势。

Conclusion: GMoT通过显式建模运动证据有效增强了MLLM对微手势视频的细粒度理解能力，在域内准确率、鲁棒性和跨域迁移方面均取得显著提升，为视频理解中细微运动建模提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文关注微手势视频理解与推理，使用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）进行细粒度视频分析，属于视频理解与时序推理方向。论文提出的GMoT模块用于增强MLLM对细微运动的理解，核心任务仍是视频理解，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理"。

Importance reason: Focuses on micro-gesture video reasoning, which is a niche application not aligned with the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal LLM, video reasoning, motion tokenization, fine-grained action recognition, semi-supervised annotation

Abstract: Micro-gesture recognition demands the detection of fleeting, spatially localized movements that are frequently overwhelmed by dominant static appearances and background noise. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at general video understanding, they inherently struggle with subtle kinematics and often rely on static posture priors. To this end, we propose GMoT, a Gated Motion-Aware Tokenization module that explicitly distills sparse kinematic evidence into a compact sequence prior to temporal modeling. GMoT dynamically spotlights action-relevant regions via spatially weighted pooling, extracts adjacent-frame temporal differencing to capture precise motion energy, and adaptively fuses these cues into the visual stream using a conservatively initialized semantic gate. To transition from simple classification to evidence-grounded reasoning, we further introduce a progressive reward-guided policy refinement paradigm, supported by a semi-supervised annotation pipeline that generates anatomically focused captions. Beyond achieving the best Top-1 accuracy among the compared methods on iMiGUE (67.32\%) and SMG (73.11\%), improving the Qwen3-VL-8B baseline by +6.80 and +3.11 points, our framework introduces Body-Region Grounding (BRG) Recall as an anatomical-grounding proxy conditioned on correct predictions, together with an overlapping-label cross-domain transfer protocol between iMiGUE and SMG. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our GMoT-augmented model improves in-domain accuracy, retains clear gains under label-preserving corruptions, and improves accuracy-oriented cross-domain transfer under explicit small-split caveats while maintaining high anatomical grounding in its generated rationales.

</details>


### [130] [SkyVLaM: Multimodal Large Language Model for UAV Video Understanding in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17386)
*Kaiwen Jing, Ruixu Jia, Bingyao Li, Ruizhe Ou, Ming Wu, Chuang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (59.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SkyVLaM 是一个面向无人机遥感视频理解的多模态大语言模型，通过时序基感知器构建稀疏 token、正则化稀疏基以鼓励互补时序线索，并自适应选择时序一致的密集分割区域进行高分辨率检查，联合稀疏与密集 token 实现语言条件视频分割。同时构建了包含 101 个视频、33.6K 帧和 1.53M 像素级实例的 SkyVid 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视频中的目标通常较小、视觉模糊且视角动态变化，语言条件分割任务面临挑战。现有 MLLM 在遥感视频理解中缺乏对时序信息的有效利用和对小目标的精细分割能力。

Method: SkyVLaM 包含三个关键组件：(1) 时序基感知器（temporal basis perceiver），从 patch 级视频表示中构建稀疏 token；(2) 稀疏基正则化，鼓励互补的时序线索；(3) 自适应选择时序一致的密集分割区域进行高分辨率检查。稀疏和密集 token 由大语言模型联合处理，实现查询条件分割。同时构建了 SkyVid 数据集（含 SkyVid-VGCG 和 SkyVid-RVOS），用于视频对话生成和指代视频目标分割。

Result: 实验表明 SkyVLaM 能更有效地分配视觉 token 预算，在无人机场景的语言条件视频分割任务上取得改进效果。

Conclusion: SkyVLaM 通过稀疏-密集双 token 机制和时序感知设计，有效提升了无人机遥感视频中的语言条件分割性能，为遥感视频理解提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出 SkyVLaM，一个面向无人机（UAV）视频理解的多模态大语言模型，核心任务是语言条件视频分割。这属于视频理解范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on remote sensing UAV video understanding, which is a negative keyword area, and does not align with priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Remote sensing, UAV video, MLLM for segmentation, Negative keyword domain, No autoregressive/diffusion generation

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly improved remote sensing (RS) multimodal understanding. Language-conditioned segmentation is crucial for fine-grained target understanding in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) videos. However, this task remains challenging due to the prevalence of small, visually ambiguous targets and dynamic aerial perspectives. In this paper, we propose SkyVLaM, a multimodal large language model for UAV video understanding. SkyVLaM constructs sparse tokens directly from patch-level video representations through a temporal basis perceiver, regularizes the sparse basis to encourage complementary temporal cues, and adaptively selects a temporally coherent dense segment for high-resolution inspection. The resulting sparse and dense tokens are jointly processed by a large language model for query-conditioned segmentation. We further build SkyVid, consisting of SkyVid-VGCG and SkyVid-RVOS for video grounded conversation generation and referring video object segmentation, respectively. SkyVid contains 101 videos, 33.6K frames, and 1.53M pixel-level object instances. Experiments show that SkyVLaM provides a more effective allocation of the visual token budget and improves language-conditioned video segmentation in UAV scenarios.

</details>


### [131] [TimeLens2: Generalist Video Temporal Grounding with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17423)
*Yuhan Zhu, Changlian Ma, Xiangyu Zeng, Xinhao Li, Zhiqiu Zhang, Songze Li, Jun Zhang, Tianxiang Jiang, Yuandong Yang, Ziang Yan, Zikang Wang, Xinyu Chen, Haoran Chen, Shaowei Zhang, Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (77.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TimeLens2 提出了一种通用视频时序定位方法，将时间证据视为区间集合进行监督和优化。构建了 TimeLens2-93K 多跨度标注数据集，并设计了时序 Wasserstein 奖励函数，在7个基准上超越所有同规模基线，2B/4B/8B 版本分别比 Qwen3-VL 骨干提升14.2/13.0/18.1 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频多模态大语言模型能描述视频内容，但很少能定位证据发生的时间位置。现有训练策略与集合预测任务不匹配：长视频标注依赖脆弱的单遍标注，强化学习奖励要么无法区分非重叠预测，要么需要脆弱的片段匹配。

Method: TimeLens2 将时间证据视为区间集合进行监督和优化。构建 TimeLens2-93K 数据集，通过字幕生成提案、独立定位、跨模型共识、语义验证和边界精炼来构建可靠的多跨度标注。提出时序 Wasserstein 奖励，计算合并区间支撑上均匀分布之间的一维 W1 距离，提供密集、无需匹配的反馈，处理不等基数和等价碎片化；时序 IoU 补充精确重叠反馈。

Result: 在7个基准上，TimeLens2-2B 在所有基准上超越同规模基线，4B 和 8B 版本达到 SOTA，超越高达 397B 参数的开源模型。2B/4B/8B 版本分别比 Qwen3-VL 骨干提升 14.2/13.0/18.1 mIoU 点。

Conclusion: TimeLens2 通过将时间证据视为区间集合进行监督和优化，有效解决了通用视频时序定位问题，展示了多模态大语言模型在细粒度时序理解上的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频时序定位（temporal grounding），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视频理解与时序推理（video_understanding_temporal）这一规范子主题。论文使用多模态大语言模型进行视频时间证据区间预测，核心是时序定位任务。

Importance reason: Strong video grounding method but not aligned with user's priority subtopics on generation or continuous models. Signals: Video temporal grounding, Multimodal LLM, Interval set supervision, Temporal Wasserstein reward, Multi-span dataset

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can describe what happens in a video, but rarely identify when the supporting evidence occurs. We study generalist video temporal grounding, in which one model predicts a variable-cardinality set of evidence intervals across video lengths, domains, query forms, and viewpoints. Existing training strategies are misaligned with this set-valued task: long-video labels often rely on brittle one-pass annotation, while reinforcement-learning rewards either fail to distinguish non-overlapping predictions or require fragile segment matching. TimeLens2 treats temporal evidence as an interval set throughout supervision and optimization. TimeLens2-93K constructs reliable multi-span supervision through caption-derived proposals, independent localization, cross-agent consensus, semantic verification, and boundary refinement. Our temporal Wasserstein reward computes exact one-dimensional \(W_1\) between uniform distributions over merged interval supports, providing dense, matching-free feedback under unequal cardinalities and equivalent fragmentation; temporal IoU complements it with precise-overlap feedback. Across seven benchmarks, TimeLens2-2B outperforms all size-matched baselines on every benchmark, while the 4B and 8B variants achieve state-of-the-art performance, surpassing open-source models with up to 397B parameters. The 2B, 4B, and 8B variants improve over their Qwen3-VL backbones by 14.2, 13.0, and 18.1 mIoU points, respectively.

</details>


### [132] [Brain-Aligned Multi-Stream Video Transformers with Sparse Self-Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17625)
*Amir Hosein Fadaei, Mahyar Maleki, Mohammad-Reza A. Dehaqani*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种脑启发式多流视频Transformer，包含稀疏胜者全拿（winner-takes-all）token选择模块替代密集自注意力，以及双通路（高分辨率低帧率"what"流 + 低分辨率高帧率"where"流）融合架构。在Kinetics-400和Something-Something V2上达到精度-速度Pareto前沿，并通过EEG表征相似性分析验证了模型与大脑神经活动的对齐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频Transformer通常忽略灵长类视觉系统的原理，且很少用神经数据评估其生物可解释性。作者希望借鉴生物视觉电路中的竞争路由机制和双通路（what/where）分工，设计更高效且与大脑对齐的视频理解模型。

Method: 提出两个核心模块：(1) 稀疏胜者全拿（winner-takes-all）token选择模块，替代密集自注意力，通过稀疏竞争机制近似生物视觉电路中的竞争路由；(2) 神经启发的split-and-fuse视频Transformer，包含两条互补通路：高分辨率低帧率的"what"流（处理内容识别）和低分辨率高帧率的"where"流（处理运动信息），在分类前融合。使用表征相似性分析（RSA）比较模型嵌入与EEG记录的相关性。

Result: 在Kinetics-400和Something-Something V2上，最佳变体在同等规模和预训练条件下达到精度-推理时间的Pareto前沿，并对空间扰动具有更好的鲁棒性。通过RSA分析，模型与EEG记录的峰值脑-模型相关性达到0.18（约为噪声上限的78%），持续优于强视频Transformer基线。

Conclusion: 通路专业化和稀疏竞争是高效且与大脑对齐的视频理解的有效归纳偏置。该工作表明，借鉴生物视觉原理可以同时提升模型的计算效率和神经可解释性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种受神经科学启发的多流视频Transformer，用于视频理解任务（Kinetics-400和Something-Something V2），属于视频理解方向。虽然论文还涉及脑电相关性分析，但核心任务是视频理解，因此映射到[多模态理解与生成]方向下的[视频理解与时序推理]（CANONICAL）子主题。

Importance reason: The paper's focus on brain-aligned video understanding and sparse token selection is interesting but not directly aligned with the user's priority subtopics on generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: Brain-inspired video transformer, Sparse winner-takes-all token selection, Dual-stream "what/where" architecture, Accuracy-speed Pareto frontier, EEG representational similarity analysis

Abstract: Modern video transformers typically ignore principles from primate vision and are rarely evaluated against neural data, limiting their biological interpretability. We introduce a sparse winner-takes-all token selection module that replaces dense self-attention to improve efficiency and approximate competitive routing observed in biological visual circuits. We further propose a neuro-inspired split-and-fuse video transformer which uses two complementary pathways: a high-resolution, low-frame-rate "what" stream and a low-resolution, high-frame-rate "where" stream, fused before classification. On Kinetics-400 and Something-Something V2, our best variant operates on the Pareto frontier of accuracy versus inference time among models of comparable scale and pretraining, and showing improved robustness to spatial perturbations. Using representational similarity analysis between model embeddings and time-resolved EEG recordings for the same video stimuli, our model attains a peak brain-model correlation of 0.18 (about 78% of the noise ceiling) and consistently outperforms strong video transformer baselines, suggesting that pathway specialization and sparse competition are useful inductive biases for efficient, brain-aligned video understanding.

</details>


### [133] [HAS: Highlight-guided Attention Steering for Multimodal LLM Video Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17994)
*Rui Chu, Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (77.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HAS（Highlight-guided Attention Steering），一种基于高亮引导注意力调控的视频摘要方法。该方法首先为视频全局计算连续的帧级高亮分布，然后将该分布作为注意力引导向量注入 MLLM，使模型在推理时对高亮帧投入更多注意力，同时保留低高亮帧的信息而非直接丢弃，从而提升视频摘要质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频摘要方法通常依赖离散的关键帧选择和片段描述，忽略了帧重要性的全局视角。离散选择不仅降低了理解的连贯性，还会丢失重要信息，且未能充分利用 MLLM 的原始能力。因此需要一种能全局考虑帧重要性、保持视频连续性并引导模型关注重点帧的视频摘要方法。

Method: HAS 包含两个部分：(1) 全局连续帧级高亮分布计算——为整个视频的每一帧计算重要性分数；(2) 将高亮分布作为注意力引导向量注入 MLLM，在推理时引导模型对高亮帧投入更多注意力，同时对低高亮帧减少注意力而非完全遗忘，从而在保留全局信息的同时突出关键内容。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，HAS 在视频摘要任务上取得了令人信服的效果，相比现有方法有显著提升。

Conclusion: HAS 通过全局高亮分布引导 MLLM 注意力，在保持视频连续性的同时有效提升了视频摘要质量，验证了注意力引导机制在视频理解中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于多模态大语言模型（M-LLM）的视频摘要方法，属于视频理解领域。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）涵盖视频 LLM、视频问答、长视频理解等方向，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video summarization via attention steering, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous models or generation. Signals: video summarization, attention steering, MLLM, highlight distribution, not generation

Abstract: Video understanding has become more and more important with the growth of Artificial Intelligence (AI) for video generation. Recently, Multimodal Large Language Model(M-LLM) has shown its capability in video understanding. Video summarization, a specific domain of video understanding, has proven its importance for efficient navigation and retrieval. Both video understanding and video summarization require a good selection of key frames in a video. Current video summarization methods heavily focus on the selected key frames and correlated segment captions. However, existing approaches overlook the perspective of treating the importance of the frames globally. We argue that using discrete selected frames for summarization will not only reduce the understanding coherence, but also lost important information in the video, as well as wasting the original capacity of the MLLMs. In this paper, we propose HAS, a Highlight-guided Attention Steering method for video summarization. We consider a challenging but practical setting where the video given to MLLMs for summarize should be continuous but with highlight guidance. HAS mainly consists of two parts: The first part is to find a continuous frame-level highlight distribution for the video globally. The second part is to apply the highlight distribution as an attention steering vector for the MLLM, targeting a better understanding of the video, and thus during the model inference time, putting more attention on the highlighted frames, while avoiding lost entire information on less highlighted frames through putting less attention instead of forgetting them. We evaluated HAS on a variety of benchmarks, and it has shown convincing performance in video summarization.
